# ( सन्तापो मिन्नवर्चस्त्वंबरित्त्वस्थ पीतता । पाण्डुता नैत्रयोर्यंस्यपानकीलुक्षणं भवेत् ॥ ) ( च. चि. स्थां. अ. १६ )

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने पाण्डरोगकामलाकुम्भकामलाहलीमकनिदानं समाप्तम् ।। ५ ।।

( Fever, diarrhoea, yellow colouration both externally and internally and whitish yellow eyes-these are the symptoms of the disease called 'Panaki'. )

Thus ends the chapter on Paṇḍu roga, Kamalā, etc.,

#### अथ रक्तपित्तनिद्यनम्

RAKTAPITTA NIDANAM ( PURPURA AND OTHER HAEMORRHA-GIC DISEASES )

घमेव्यायामशोक्ताः व्यवायैरतिसेवितैः । तीक्ष्णोण्डस्तरत्न्द्रवणैरम्बैः कदृभिरेव च ।। १ ॥ पित्तं विद्यं स्वगुणैविंदहर्त्या श्र शोणितम् । ततः प्रवर्तते रक्तमूर्ध्वं चाधो द्विधाऽपि वा ।। २ ॥ ( सू. उ. तं. अ. ४५ ) ऊध्वे नासाक्षिकर्णास्यैमेंद्रयोनिगुद्र्र्यः । कुपितं रोमकूपैश्च समस्तैस्तत्प्रवर्तते ।। ३ ।।

Excessive exposure to sunlight, physical exercise, grief, walking long distances, sexual intercourse; foods which are hot, alkaline, salty, sour and pungent-all lead to increase of pitta in its vidagdha state ( uşņa, tīkṣṇa properties ), which in turn makes for increase of rakta (its tiksna, usna properties) and causes it to flow out of the body through upper and lower orifices, viv., nostrils, eyes, ears and mouth being the upper passages; urethra, vagina, rectum being the lower passages. In severe conditions, blood comes out of the hair follicles of the skin also. ( 1-3 )

## सदनं शीतकामित्वं कण्ठधूमायनं वमिः । लोहगन्धिश्च निःश्वास्तो भवत्यस्मिन् भविष्यति ॥ ४ ॥ ( सु. उ. तं. अ. ४५ )

Weakness, desire for cold things ( food, drink, places, etc., ) feeling as though hot fumes are coming out of the throat, vomitting, smell of iron in the breath are the premonitary symptoms. { 4 }

1

सान्द्रं सपाण्डु सस्त्रेहरं पिच्छिलं च कफान्वितम् । इयावारुणं सफेनं च तनु रूक्षं च वातिकम् ॥ ५ ॥ रक्तपित्तं कषायाभं कृष्णं गोमूत्रसंनिभम् । मेचकागारधुमाभमञ्जनामं च पैत्तिकम् ॥ ६ ॥ संस्कृष्टलिङ्ग संसर्गोन्निलिङ्ग सान्निपातिकम् । (च. चि. स्था. अ. ४ )

If kapha is associated, the blood coming out will be thick, yellowish, white, greasy and sticky;

If vata is associated, the blood will be blackish red, frothy, thin and viscid.

If pitta alone is the cause, the blood coming out will be black resembling a decoction of drugs, cow's urine, black pigment of a cymbal, chimneysoot or antimony.

If combination of two or three dosas their specific symptoms are also found together. ( 5-6 )

```
ऊर्वगं कफसंस्तृष्टमधोगं पवनानुगम् ।
द्विमार्गं कफवाताभ्यामुभाभ्यामनुवर्तते ॥ ७ ॥ ( च. चि. स्था. अ. ४ )
```
The upward one ( rakta-pitta ) is by association of kapha and the downward is by association of vāta; kapha and vāta together will cause for both the upward and downward. ( 7 )

```
ऊध्वे साध्यमधो याण्यमसाध्यं युगणद्रतम् ।   ( सु. उ. तं. अ. ४५ )
एकमार्गं बलवतो नातिवेगं नवोरिथतम् ॥ ८ ॥
रक्तपित्तं सुखे कालै साध्यं स्यान्निरुपद्रवम् ।   ( च. चि. स्था. अ, ४ )
```
Upward one is easy to cure, downward one persists all through life and that of both directions is imposible to cure.

It is easy to treat, if bleeding is from any one passage, in persons who are strong, if bleeding is not large, if it is of recent origin, if seen in good season and if not having other complications. ( 8 )

```
पकदोषानुगं साध्यं द्विदोषं याप्यमुच्यते ॥ ९ ॥
यन्निदोषमसाध्यं स्यान्मन्दाग्नेरतिवेगवत् ।
दयाधिमिः क्षीणदेहस्य वृद्धस्यानश्चतश्च यत् ॥ १० ॥ ( च. चि. स्था. अ. ४ )
```
It is easy to treat if only one dosa is associated, persists for life if two dogas are associated and incurable if all the three dosas are involved, likewise in persons having poor digestive functions, where bleeding is copious; in persons debilitated by other diseases, who are of advanced age and who do not take any food. ( 9-10 )

```
दौर्बल्यश्वासकासज्वरवमधुमदाः पाण्डुतादाहम्बद्धमुळां,
     भुक्ते घोरो विदाहरूत्वधूतिरपि सदा हृद्यतुल्या च पीडा।
तृष्णा कोष्ठस्य मेदः शिरसि च तपनं पूतिनिष्ठीवनत्वं,
      भक्तद्वेषाविपाकौँ 
                                         भवेद्रक्तपित्तोपसर्गाः ।।११।। (मू. उ. तं. अ. ४५)
                           विकृतिरपि
```
Debility, increased/difficult respiration, cough, fever, vomitting, toxicity, yellowish-white discolouration, burning sensation, fainting, severe heart-burn after food, constant anxiety, severe pain near the heart, thirst, pain in the abdomen, headache, bad smelling sputum coming out, aversion to food and indigestion these accompany as complications. ( 11 )

# मांसप्रक्षालनामं कुथितमिव च यत्कर्द्रमाम्भोनिभं वा, मेदः पूर्यास्त्रकल्पं यक्तदिव यदि वा पक्कजम्बूफलाभम् । यत्कृष्णं यच्च नीलं भृशमतिकुणपं यत्र चोक्ता विकारा स्तद्वज्ये रक्तपित्तं सुरपतिधनुषा यच्च तुल्यं विभाति ॥ १२ ॥ ( सु. उ. तं. अ. ४५ )

Patients in whom the blood coming out resembles water in which mutton has been washed, a decoction, muddy water with lot of silt, fat, pus, picce of liver, ripe fruit of jambu ( Eugina Jambolina ), which is black or blue in colour, which smells like a cadaver, or resembles a rainbow and who have one or more of the complications cited above-are to be refused treatment. { 12 )

येन चोपहतो रक्त रक्तपित्तेन मानवः । पदयेद्दृद्धं वियन्नापि तब्बासाध्यमसंशयम् ॥ १३ ॥ (च.नि.अ. २) / लोहितं छद्देद्यस्तु बहुशो लोहितेक्षणः लोहितोद्वारद्द्यों च म्रियते रक्तपैत्तिकः ॥१४॥ (गु.मू.अ. ३३)

इति श्री माधवकरविररचिते माधवनिदाने रक्तपित्तनिदानं समाप्तम् !! २ ।।

The patient ( of raktapitta ) who is unable to see anything and who vomits blood in large quantity, who sees everything as red, who gets belchings accompanied with bleeding ( or gets belchings having smell of iron ) is going to die. ( 13-14 )

Thus ends the chapter on Raktapitta.

# CHAPTER 10 अथ राजयक्ष्मक्षतक्षीणनिदानम्

# RĀJAYAKĻMA-KĻATAKĻĪŅA NIDĀNAM ( PULMONARY TUBER-CULOSIS-CONSUMPTION )

वेगरोधात् क्षयाच्यैव साहसाद्विष्माशनात् । त्रिदोषो जायते यक्ष्मा गदो हेतुचतुष्टयात् ॥ १ ॥

Suppression of natural urges, loss or deficiency of tissues, over-exertion and induigence in unhealthy foods-these four will produce the disease yaksma caused by all the three dosas together. ( 1 )

# कफप्रधानैदौं, रुद्धेषु रसवर्त्मसु । अतिव्यवायिनो वाऽपि क्षीणे रेतस्यनन्तराः । क्षीयन्ते धातवः सर्वे ततः शुध्यति मानवः ॥ २॥ ( सु. उ. अ. ४१ )

When the rasavaha srotas ( channels through which rasadhātu (blood plasma) circulates ) become obstructed by the tridosas among which kapha is predominant; or in persons who indulge in excessive sexual intercourse leading to the loss of semen, all the other dhatus of the body undergo deficiency, making the person too much emaciated. ( 2 )

# श्वासाङ्गमर्दकफसंस्रवतालुशोषवर्म्यग्निसाद्मद्पीनसकासनिद्राः । शोषे मविष्यति भवन्ति स चापि जन्तुः शुक्रुक्षणो भवति मांसपरो रिरंसुः ॥ ३ ॥ स्वप्रेषु काकशुकशलुकिनीलकण्ठा गृध्यास्तथैव कपयः कृकलासकाश्च । तं वाह्यान्ति स नदीर्विजलाश्च पश्येच्छुष्कांस्तरूपवनघूमदवादितांश्च ॥ ४ ॥ (नु. उ. अ. ४१ )

Increased/difficult respiration, pain in the body, expectoration of sputum, dryness of the palate, vomitting, poor digestion, toxicity, running in the nose, cough, excess sleep, eyes becoming bright white; person craves for meat and sexual intercourse, dreams as if he is being carried away by the crow, parrot, porcupine, peacock vultures, monkey and chamelion; sees rivers without water, trees without leaves, breeze, smoke and forest fires-are the premonitory symptoms of this disease.( 3-4 )

# अंसपाध्वीभितापश्च संतापः करपादयोः । उन्वरः सर्वाङ्गगश्चेति लक्षणं राजयक्ष्मणः ।।५।। (च. चि. अ. ८) ( भक्तद्रेषो ज्वरः श्वासः कासः शोणितदर्शनम् । स्वरभेदश्च जायेत षड्रपं राजयक्ष्मणि ।। )

Discomfort ( pain ) in the shoulders and flanks, feeling of warmth ( mild burning sensation ) in the hands and feet, fever all over the body are the symptoms of rajayaksma. ( 5 )

( Aversion to food, fever, increased/difficult respiration, cough appearance of blood ( in the sputum ) and hoarseness of voice are the six symptoms of rajayaksmā. )

# स्वरमेदोऽनिलाच्छलं संकोश्चांसपार्श्वयोः । ज्वरो दाहोऽतिसारश्च पित्ताद्रक्तस्य चागमः ॥६॥ शिरसः परिपूर्णत्वमभक्तच्छन्द् पव च । कासः कण्डस्य चोव्ध्वस्तो विश्वेयः कफकोपतः ॥७॥

Hoarseness of voice, pain in the shoulders, constriction { deformity } of shoulders and flanks due to vata; fever, burning sensation, diarrhoea, blood in the sputum, ( haemoptysis ) due to pitta; feeling of fullness of the head, aversion to food, cough and irritation in the throat due to kapha -- are the ( eleven ) important symptoms. ( 6-7 )

एकादशभिरेभिर्वा षडमिर्वाऽपि समन्वितम् । कासातीसारपार्श्वोतिस्वरभेदार्थन्दिज्वरः ।। ८ ॥ त्रिभिर्वा पीडितं लिङ्कैः कारश्वासास्यः । जह्याच्छोषादितं जन्तुमिच्छन् सुविमलं यशः ॥ ९ ॥

( सू. उ. अ. ४१ )

# सर्वेरघँस्त्रिभिर्वाऽपि लिङ्गीमां सबलक्ष्ये । युक्तो वर्ण्यश्चिकित्स्यस्त्र सर्वरूपोऽप्यतोऽन्यथा । १० ।। महाशनं क्षीयमाणमतीसारनिपीडितम् । शूनमुष्कोदरं चैव यक्ष्मिणं परिवर्जयेन् ।। ११ ।। शुक्राक्षमन्नद्वेण्डारम् ध्वासनिर्पीडितम् । कृच्छेण बहुमेहन्तं यक्ष्मा द्वन्तीह मानवम् ॥ १२ ॥

Presence of all the eleven symptoms or the six symptoms viz, cough, diarrhoea, pain in the flanks, hoarseness of voice, anorexia and fever or even the three symptoms viz., cough, increased/difficult respiration, appearance of blood in sputum -calls for refusal of such patients by physicians desiring good reputation.

Appearance of all the symptoms, or half of it ( six ) or of even three, in persons who have loss of muscles ( wasting of muscles ) and strength are also indication for refusal.

Patients of yaksma who eat large quantities of food but still undergo wasting; who are having diarrhoea, swelling of the scrotum and abdomen are also to be avoided.

Yakşmā kills such persons who have whiteness of eyes, aversion to food. heavy and troublesome expirations and who eliminate large quantities of urine with difficulty. ( 8-12 )

# ज्वरानुबन्धरहितं बलवन्तं कियासद्वम् । उपकमेद्दात्मवन्तं दीप्ताग्रिमकुशं नरम् ॥१३॥ (म्. उ. अ. ४१)

Treatment can be given to patients of yaksma who do not have fever, who are strong, able to withstand the discomforts of treatments, of strong will, digestive capacity and who are not emaciated. ( 13 )

# ब्यवायशोकवार्धक्यायायामध्वप्रशोषितान् । वणोरःक्षतसंश्वी च शोषिणौ लक्षणः श्टुणु ।।१४॥ (सू.उ.अ.४१)

Henceforth are described the symptoms of persons afficted with sosa ( consumption, wasting, cmaciation ) due to sexual intercourse, grief, old age, physical exertion, walking long distances, ulcers and injury to the chest. ( 14 )

व्यवायशोधी शुकस्य क्षर्यालङ्गैरुपद्गुतः । पाण्डुदेहो यथापूर्वं क्षीयन्ते चास्य घातबः ।।१५॥ (सु. उ. अ. ४१)

Presence of symptoms of deficiency of semen and yellowish white discolouration of the body and subsequent wasting of the other tissues of the body are seen in persons who indulge in excessive sexual intercourse. ( 15 )

प्रध्यानशीलः स्नस्ताङ्गः शोकशोष्यपि तादृशः । जराशोषी क्रुशो मन्दवीर्यवुद्धिबलेन्द्रिय: 11 १६ 11 कम्पनोऽस्विमान् भिन्नकांस्यपात्रहतस्यरः । प्रीवति श्रुष्मणा हीनं गौरवारतिपीडितः ॥१७॥ संप्रस्तुतास्यनासाक्षिः शुष्करूक्षमल्रच्छविः ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ४१ )

Too much of worry, looseness and weakness of the body are scen in persons who are wasted due to excessive grief.

Emaciation, decrease of vigour, intellect, strength and capacity of sense organs; shaking of the body parts, anorexia, voice husky, feeble and broken as that of a broken bronze vessel, patient trying hard to expectorate but brings out little or no sputum, heaviness of the body, restlessness, fluid oozing out of mouth, nose and eyes, loss of complexion and dryness ( of feces ) are the symptoms of persons wasted due to senility ( old age ). ( 16-17 )

> अध्वशोधी च 'स्नुस्ताङ्गः संभ्रष्ट्रपरुषच्छविः ॥ १८ ॥ प्रसुप्तगात्रावयवः शुष्कक्लोमगल्राननः । ( मु. उ. अ. ४१ )

Persons wasted due to constant long distance walking will be having weakness, hardness and loss of sensation of body parts, dryness of kloma, throat and mouth. { 18 }

ब्यायामशोषी भूयिष्ठमेभिरेव सम्मन्वितः । लिङ्गेरुरःक्ष्तक्ष्य क्षर्तं विना ।।१९॥ (स्. उ. अ. ४१)

Persons wasted due to excess physical exertion will also have the same symptoms as above and symptoms of injury to the chest without of course an actual tear. ( 19 )

रक्तक्षयाद्वेदनाभिस्तथैवाद्वारयन्त्रणात् । व्रणितस्य भवेच्छोषः स चासाध्यतमो मतः ॥२०॥ (मु.ज.अ. ११)

Wasting due to ulcer is caused by loss of blood, pain and control over food by the person. This condition is difficult to treat. ( 20 )

धनुषाऽऽयस्यतोऽत्यर्थं भारमुद्रहतो गुरुम् । युध्यमानस्य बल्किभिः पततो विषमोच्चतः ॥ २१ ॥ वृपं द्वयं वा धावन्तं दम्यं वाऽन्यं निर्मुहतः । शिल्प्रकाष्ठाष्टान्धर्मानान् क्षिपतो निघ्नतः परान् ॥२२॥

अधीयानस्य वाऽत्युचैदूरं वा वजतो द्रतम् । महानदीवी तरतो ह्यावर्तः ॥ २३ ॥ सहसोत्पततो दूरं तूर्ण वाऽपि प्रमृत्यतः। तथाऽन्यः कर्माभिः करैर्भूशमध्याहतस्य वा ॥ २४ ॥ विक्षते वक्षसि व्याधिबैलवान् समुद्दीर्यंते । श्रीषु चातिप्रसक्तस्य रूक्षाल्पर्श्मिताशिनः ॥ २५ ॥ उरोविभज्यतेऽत्यर्थं भिद्यतेऽध विरुञ्यते । प्रपीड्यते ततः पार्श्वे शुष्यत्युद्धं प्रवेपते ।। २६ ॥ क्रमाद्वीर्यं बलं वर्णो रुचिरम्भिश्च हीयते । ज्वरो व्यथा मनोदैन्यं विडमेदास्विष्यावर्षप् ॥ २७ ॥ दुष्टः श्यावः सुदुर्गन्धः पीतो विद्यश्यतो वहुः । कासमानस्य चीभीक्ष्णं करकः सास्तुक प्रवर्तते ।। २८ ।। स क्षती क्षीयतेऽत्यर्थं तथा शुकौजसोः क्षयात् । ( च. चि. अ. ११ )

Over-indulgence in archery, carrying heavy loads on the head, combating with strong persons, falling from a height recklessly, forceably stopping a running mighty bull or horse, or taming wild animals, throwing heavy stones, wooden rafters or catapults, fighting with enemies ( using weapons ), reading with high pitch, running fast long distances, swimming mighty rivers, running along with horses, sudden jumping, strenuous dancing and such other acts of violence and excessive sexual intercourse, partaking of dry, scanty and non-nutritious foods cause injury to the chest ( tear inside the lungs ). As a result the patient will have cutting or pricking pain in the chest and flanks, followed by emaciation, termors; loss of vigour, strength, complexion, interest and digestive ability; appearance of fever, grief. helplessness, diarrhoca, indigestion, cough with bad, black, foetid, jyellowish pellets of sputum in large quantity coming out, often mixed with blood. This condition known as uraksata makes for quick loss of sukra and ojas and leads to severe emaciation. ( 21-28 )

# अध्यक्तं लक्षणं तरूय पूर्वरूपमिति स्मृतम् ॥ २९ ॥

The very symptoms of this disease itself is seen in mild form during the premonitory period. ( 29 )

उरोहक शोणितच्छदिः कासो वैशेषिकः क्षते । क्षीणे सरक्तमूत्रत्वं पार्श्वपृष्ठकटीय्रहः ।।२०।। (च चि. अ. १६)

Pain in the chest, vomitting of blood, cough are prominant if trauma ( tear ) is present, while urine mixed with blood, catching pain in the flanks, back and waist ( hips ) are predominant if only cmaciation is present. ( 30 )

अरूपत्तिङ्गस्य दीप्ताग्नेः साध्यो बल्रवतो नवः। परिसंबरसरो याण्यः सर्वत्रिङ्गं त् वर्जयेत् ॥३१॥ (च.च.अ.१६) इनि माधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने राजयक्ष्मक्षतक्षीणनिदानं समाप्तम् ।।१०।।

Uraksata is curable which has mild symptoms, seen in strong persons, of recent origin and in whom digestive capacity {is good; that which has remained over one year does persist throughout life, and that which has all the symptoms is incurable. ( 31 )

Thus ends the chapter on Rajayaksmā,

# अथ कासनिदानम्

#### KASA NIDĀNAM ( COUGH, PERTUSIS ) धूमो पघाताद्र सतस्त थाँव व्यायामरूक्षान्न निषेवणार्थ । विमार्गगत्वाच्च हि भोजनस्य वेगावरोधात् क्षवथोस्तशैव ॥ १ ॥ प्राणो ह्युदानानुगतः प्रदुष्टः स भिन्नकांस्यस्वनतुरुयघोषः ।

निरेति वक्रात्सहस्य सदोषो मनीषिभिः कास इति प्रदिष्टः ॥ २ ॥ (तु. उ. अ. ५२ )

Inhalation of smoke, dust, over-exercise, foods which are dry, food particles entering the wrong passage (trachea ), suppression of natural urges, control of sneezing and such other activities cause ( increase of ) prāņa and udāna vāta which come out from the mouth suddenly producing a sound resembling that coming out of a broken bronze. This is called as kasa ( cough ). ( 1-2 )

पञ्च कासाः स्मृता वातपित्तरश्रेष्मक्ष्तक्ष्यैः । क्षयायोपेक्षिताः सर्वे बल्लिनश्चोत्तरोत्तरम् ।। ३।। (श्रृहः नि.अ.३)

Kāsa is of five kinds; one each from vāta, pitta, kapha, ksata ( injury to chest ) and ksaya ( loss of tissues ). The first four, if neglected will lead to ksaya ( dhātukṣaya-wasting of the tissues ). { 3 )

पूर्वरूपं मवेत्तेषां शूकपूर्णगलास्यता । कण्ठे कण्डूश्च भोज्यानामवरोधश्च जायते ।। ४ ।। (च.चि.अ. २२) Premonitory symptoms ( of cough ) are a feeling of thorns pricking the throat, irritation and obstruction to movement of food. ( 4 )

# हृच्छङ्घमूर्धोदरपार्श्वेशूल्ठी क्षामाननः क् क्लीणबलस्वरौजाः ।

प्रसक्तवेगस्तु समीरणेन भिन्नस्वरः कासति शुध्कमेव ॥ ५ ॥ ( सु. उ. अ. ५२ )

Person of vātaja kāsa suffers from pain in the region of the heart, temples, head, abdomen and flanks; dry face ( mouth ), debility, feeble voice, lack of enthusiasm and continuous husky ( small ) and dry cough. ( 5 )

# उरोविदाहृज्वरवरवक्रशोषैरभ्यदित्तस्तिक्तम् स्वस्तृषातः ।

पित्तेन पीतानि वमेत्कद्रूनि कास्तेत्सपाण्डुः परिदह्यमानः ॥ ६ ॥ (मु. उ. अ. ५२ )

Person of pittaja kāsa will have burning sensation in the chest, fever, dryness and bitter taste in the mouth, severe thirst, yellowish and bitter vomittus, yellowish white skin ( complexion ) and burning sensation inside the body. ( 6 )

## प्रत्किण्यसानैन मुखेन सीदन् शिरोरंजातेः कफपूर्णदेहः ।

अभक्तरुग्गौरवकण्डुयुक्तः कासेद्धृशं सान्द्रकफः कफेन ॥ ७ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ५२ )

Mouth full with thick saliva, weakness, headache, accumulation of kapha all over the body, anorexia, heaviness, irritation ( in the throat ) heavy cough followed by thick sputum being expectorated are the symptoms of kaphaja kāsa. ( 7 )

अतिब्यवायभाराध्ययद्धाः भवग्रविद्यहैः । रूक्षस्योरःक्ष्यतं वायुर्गद्वीरुवा कासमान्वरेत् ॥ ८ ॥ स पूर्वं कासते शुष्कंततः श्रीवेत्सशोणितम् । कण्डेन रुजताऽत्यर्थं विरुग्णेनेव चोरसा । ९ ॥ सुचीभिरिव तीक्ष्णामिस्तुद्यमानेन शूल्किना । दुःखस्पर्शन भेदपीडाभितापिना ।। १० ॥ पर्वभेदज्वरभ्वासतृष्णावैस्वर्यपीडितः । पारावत द्वाकूजन् कासवेगात् क्षतोद्भवात् ।।११।। (सु. उ. अ. १२)

Persons who are weak and indulge in too much of sexual intercourse, carrying heavy weights, walking long distances, taming or fighting with elephants, horses etc., get injury to the chest ( tear in the lungs ) and produce cough. Dry cough followed by expectoration of blood mixed sputum, pair in the throat and severe pain in the chest as though pricked by needles or cutting or knawing pain, patient resisting touch by others, pain in the joints, fever, increased respiration, thirst, voice like that of a pigeon will manifest as symptoms in ksataja kāsa. ( 8–11 )

विष्मासाःस्यस्यातिब्यवायाद्वेगनिप्रहात् । प्रृणिनां शोच्यतां नृणां ध्यापन्नेऽग्रौ त्रयो मलाः । कुपिताः क्षयजं कासं कुर्युदेहक्षयप्रदम् ॥ १२ ॥ स गात्रशुल्वरदाद्यमोद्यान् प्राणक्षयं चोपलबेत कासी । श्रृष्यन्वनिष्ठीवति दुर्बल्र्स्तु प्रक्षीणमांसो दधिरं सपूर्यम् । तं सर्वालिङ्गं भृश्रदुश्चिकित्स्यं चिकित्सितन्नाः क्षयजं द्यदन्ति ॥ १३ ॥ (मु. उ. अ. ५२)

इत्येष क्षयज्ञः कासः क्षीणानां देहनाशनः । साध्यो चलवतां या स्याद्याप्यस्त्वेव क्षतोत्थितः ॥ १४ ॥

Indulgence in irregular and untimely meals, unaccustomed foods, excessive sex, suppression of bodily urges, too much of jealousy, grief, etc., cause increase of all the three dosas, destroys the digestive functions, produce ksayaja kāsa ( cough ) and lead to wasting of all the dhatus ( tissues ) of the body. Such a person will suffer from pain all over the body, fever, burning sensation, delusion, wasting and dryness of the body, extreme weakness, coughs and brings out sputum mixed with blood and pus. This disease with all the symptoms is very hard to treat and kills the very weak ones. ( 12-14 )

नवौ कदाचित्सिध्येतामपि पादगुणान्वितौ । स्थविराणां जराकासः सर्वों याप्यः प्रकोतितः । त्रीन् पूर्वान्साधयेत्साध्यान्यध्ये योज्यांस्त् यापयेत् ॥ १५ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ५२ )

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने कासनिदानं समाप्तम् ॥ ११ ॥

Cough is curable in persons who are strong, which is of recent onset; ksata kasa and kasa of very old persons can be controlled if all the limbs of treatment and food are good but it persists through the rest of life; the first three kinds are easily curable; the chronic ones are to be controlled with suitable foods etc. ( 15 )

Thus ends the chapter on Kasa.

# अथ हिक्काश्वासनिदानम्

# HIKKA ŚWASA NIDANAM ( HICCOUGH AND DYSPNOEA )

# चिद्याहिगुरुः विष्ठः भारतक्षाभिध्यन्द्रभोजनैः । शीतपानाशनस्थानरजोभ्रूमातपानिन्द्रैः ।। १ ।। ब्यायामकर्मभाराध्ववेगाघातापतर्पणैः । हिक्का श्वासश्च कासश्च नुणां समुपज्ञायते ॥ २ ॥ ( मु. उ. ञ ८२

Persons who indulge in foods which produce heart-burn, constipation and dryness; which are heavy and those which are hard to digest, which block the peres of tissues; drinking of very cold water ( or other beverages ), starvation, unaccusto med places, exposure to dust, smoke, sunlight and breeze, physical exertion, lifiinz or carrying heavy weights, walking long distances, suppression of natural urges and malnutrition, become victims of hikkā (hiccough ), swāsa ( difficult respiration and kasa ( cough ). ( 1-2 )

# मुद्दुर्मुहुर्वायुरुदेति सस्वनो यक्तद्भिद्वान्त्राणि मुखादिवाक्षिपन् । स घोषवानाशु हिनस्त्यसून् यतस्ततस्तु हिक्केत्यभिधीयते दुधैः ॥ ३ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ५०

Vayu coming out of the mouth with pecular hik-hik sound starting from the region of liver, spleen and intestines and quickly producing tiredness to the person is named as Hikkā by the learned. ( 3 )

# अन्नजां यमलां श्रुद्रां गम्भीरां महतीं तथा। वायुः कफेनानुगतां पश्च हिक्काः करोति हि ।।।आ (मु.ज. व र ,

Vāta associated with kapha produces hikkā which is of five kinds viz., annajā, yamalā, kṣudrā; gambhīrā and mahati. ( 4 )

# कण्ठोरसोर्गुरुत्वं च वदनस्य कथायता । हिकानां पूर्वक्रपाणि कुक्षेराटोप पव च ॥ ५॥ (च. चि. व्र. २.

Premonitory symptoms of hikkā are heaviness of throat and chest, astringent taste in the mouth and gurgling noise in the abdomen. ( 5 )

# पानाश्वैरतिसंयुक्तैः सदस्य पीडितोऽनिलः । हिक्कयत्यूर्ध्वगो भूत्वा तां विद्यादन्नजां भिषक् ॥६॥ (मु. १. ३. १. ९

Annajā hikkā is produced by vāta which moves upwards by the effect of taking excess quantities of drinks and foods too quickly. ( 6 )

# चिरेण यमलेखेंगैयां हिका संप्रवर्तते । कम्पयन्ती शिरोग्रीचं यमलां तां विनिर्दिरोत् ॥ ७ ॥ (मु. उ. २. २०)

Yamala hikkā occurs in pairs, continues for a long time and produces shaking of the head and neck. ( 7 )

49

म्रृष्टकाल्ग्रेयां वेगैमंन्दैः समसिवर्तते । क्रुद्धिकानाम सा हिकाः जनुमूलात्मधाविता ।। ८ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ५० ) Ksudrā hikkā starts only from the base of the neck, is mild in intensity and continues for short time. ( 8 )

नाभियवृत्ता या दिक्का घोरा गम्भीरनादिनी । अनैकोपद्रबवती गम्भीरा नाम सा स्मृता ।।९॥ (सु.उ.अ. ५०)

Gambhīrā hikkā starts deep inside from the umbilicus, is very powerful, makes heavy sound and is accompanied with many complications ( 9 )

मर्माण्युत्पीडयन्तीय सततं या प्रवर्तते । महाद्विकोति सा क्षेया सर्वगात्रविकम्पिनी ।। १० ।। (मु. उ. अ. ५०)

Manahikka produces powerful feeling as though all the vital organs are being pulled out, is continuous without break in the middle and causes tremors/shaking of the whole body. ( 10 )

# आयम्यते हिकतो यस्य देह्ये दृश्चिश्वे नाम्यते यस्य नित्यम् । क्षीणोऽस्रद्धिद् क्षौति यश्चातिमात्रं तौ द्वौ चान्त्यौ वर्जयेद्धिकमानी ।। ११ ।। ( मु. उ. अ. ५०)

Patients of hikka who develop too much stretching of the body, gaze fixed up, body bent always, emaciation, aversion to food and too many sneezings and those who have the last two types ( gambhira and mahati ) are to be refused treatment. ( 11 )

# अतिसंचितदोषस्य भक्तच्छेदक्कशस्य च । व्याधिमिः क्षीणदेहस्य बृद्धस्यातिव्यवायिनः ॥ १२ ॥ आसां या सा समुरपन्ना द्विक्का हन्त्याशु जीवितम् । यमिका च प्रत्यापार्तिमोहरुण्णासमन्विता ॥१३॥

Likewise, those in whom all the dosas have increased too much, who are unable to take food, who are emaciated, debilitated by other diseases, who are very aged, who indulge in excessive sexual intercourse, who are having yamala type of hikka, have delerium, pain, delusion and thirst are going to succumb to proba soon. ( 12-13 )

# अक्षीणश्वाप्यदीनश्च स्थिरधारिवन्द्रियश्च यः ।

# तस्य साधयितुं शक्या यमिका हन्त्यतोऽन्यथा ॥ १४॥ ( च. चि. व. २१ )

Yamala hikka is curable in those who are not emaciated, who are having a strong will, whose tissues and senses are good, whereas it kills others. ( 14 )

मद्वोध्वविद्यन्नतमकश्चुद्रभेदैस्तु पञ्चथा । भिद्यते स महाव्याधिः श्वास पको विशेषतः ॥ १५ ॥ (मु.ज.अ. ११)

Mahati, urdhwa, chinna, tamaka and ksudra are the five kinds of Swāsa ( dyspnoea ) which is counted as a mahāvyādhi ( powerful disease ). ( 15 )

> बाताधिको भवेत् भवेत् क्ष्ट्रस्तमकस्तु काफोन्द्रवः । कफवाताधिकश्चैव संस्यृष्ट्रिछन्नस्तं क्षः । श्वासो मारुतसंख्यो महानूर्ध्वस्ततो मतः ॥ १२ ॥ { {

Ksudra is predominantly vātaja, ta.naka is predominantly kaphaja, chinna is kapha vātaja, mahat and ūrdhva are al o predominantly vātaja.

## प्राथ्रपं तस्य इत्पीडा शूलमाध्मानमेव च । अनाहो वक्रवैरस्यं श्राङ्कनिस्तोद पव च । १६ ॥

The premonitory symptoms are pain in the region of the heart, abdominal colic and distention ( tympanitis ) and enlargement, bad taste in the mouth and pain in the temples. { 16 }

# यदा स्रोतांसि संरुध्य मारुतः कफपूर्वकः । विष्वम्बज्जति संरुद्धस्तदा श्वासान् करोति सः ।।रेजा (गुजअ. २१)

Vata associated with kapha obstructs the passages ( inside the lungs ) and begins to move in all directions producing the lisease swisa. ( 17 )

# उद्धूयमानवातो यः शब्दःवद् द्वितो नरः। उच्चैः श्वस्तित् संरुद्धो मत्तर्षभ इवानिश्रम् ॥ १८ ॥ प्रनष्ठक्षानविज्ञानस्तथा विश्वन्तलोचनः । विद्युताक्यानना वद्धसूत्रद्वन्ती विशीणवाद्या १९ ॥ दीनः प्रश्वसितं चास्य दूराद्विश्वायते भृशम् । महाभ्यासोपस्पुस्त्र क्षिणगेव विपद्यते ।२०॥ (च. १२)

Vata having increased greatly, moves upward constantly with heavy sound and pain, like that of a powerful bull tie I firmly to a peg, the patient loses awareness and intellect, eyes begin to roll, with terrified look, wide open; mouth also open, no movement of feces and urine, broken voice, helplessness, the sound of respiration being heard even from a distance. These are the symptoms of Mahaswasa and such a patient dies quickly. ( 18-20 )

# ऊंध्वं श्वसिति यो दीर्घं न च प्रत्याहरत्यधः । श्लेष्मान्नतमुखस्रोताः कद्धगन्धवद्धादैँतः ॥ २१ ॥ ऊध्वेद्यविवरयंस्तु विभ्रान्ताक्ष् इनस्नतः । प्रमुहान् वेदनार्त्य श्रृङ्कास्योऽरतिर्वाडितः ॥ २२ ॥ ऊर्वश्वासे प्रकुपिते ह्यधःश्वासो निरुध्यते । मुहानस्ताम्यतश्येष्यं श्वासस्तस्यैव हन्त्यसून् ॥ २३ ॥ ( च. नि. अ. २१ )

Long expiration, inability of inspiration, mouth and other passages filled with sputum, great increase in vata causing its other symptoms, eyes lixed up, eye balls rolling, terrified look, delusion, white discolouration of the mouth, restlessness, expiration deep and inspiration obstructed, loss of consciousness/delusion-these are the features of Urdhva swasa and this also kills the patient. ( 21-23 )

# यस्तु श्वसिति विच्छन्नं सर्वप्राणेन पीडितः । न वा श्वसिति दुःखार्तो मर्मच्छेदरुगर्दितः ॥ २४ ॥ आनाहस्वेदमूच्छातौं द्द्यमानैन बस्तिना । विप्लुताक्षः परिक्षीणः श्वसन् रक्तैकलोचनः ।। २५ ॥ षिचेताः परिशुष्कास्यो विवर्णः प्रल्नरः । छिन्नश्चासेन विद्छन्नः सः शोधं विजहात्यस्यस्त्र् ॥ २५ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २१ )

He whose respiration is often interrupted in the middle, with severe distress, does not attempt at respiration for fear of pain in all vital organs, enlargement of abdomen, perspiration, faintings, burning sensation in the urinary bladder,

eyes filled with tears, emaciation, redness of one eye, loss of consciousness, severe dryness of the mouth, discolouration, delerium, looseness of all the joints-with these symptoms the Chinnaswasa-kills the patient. ( 24-26 )

प्रतिलोमं यदा वायुः स्रोतांसि प्रतिपद्यते । ग्रीवां शिरश्च संगृह्य रुप्पाणं समुदीर्य च ॥ २७ ॥ करोति पीनमं तेन रुद्धो घुर्घुरकं तथा । अतीव तीववेगं व श्वासं प्राणप्रपीडकम् ॥ २८ ॥ प्रताम्यति स वेगेन तृष्यते सन्निरुध्यते । प्रमोहं कासमानश्च स गच्छति मुहुमुद्दुः ।। २९ ॥ श्लेष्मण्यमुच्यमानि तु भूशं भवति दुःखितः । तस्यैव च विमोक्षान्ते मूहूंः लमते सुखम् ॥ ३० ॥ तथाऽस्योद्ध्वस्तते कण्ठः क्रञ्ज्ञान्छन्नोति भाषितुम् । न चापि लभते निर्द्रां शयानः श्वासपीडितः ॥ ३१ ॥ पार्श्वे तस्यावगुद्धाति शयानस्य समीरणः । आसीनो लुभते सौख्यमुणं चैवाभिनन्दति ॥ ३२ ॥ उच्छ्रिताक्षो ललाटेन स्विद्यता भृशमानिमान् । विशुष्कास्यो मुहुः श्वासो मुहुश्चैवावधम्यते ॥ ३३ ॥ मेघाम्बुशीतप्राग्वातैः श्लेभलेश्च विवर्धते । स याप्यस्तमकः श्वासः साध्यो वा स्यान्नवोत्थितः ॥ ३४ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २१ )

Vata undergoing a change to upward movement in the passages ( of respiration ) makes for increase of kapha in the throat and head Together they produce running in the nose, bubling sound in the throat, respirations greatly increased ( in number and intensity ) causing darkness of vision, thirst and bouts of severe cough, patient trying hard to bring out sputum and finds momentary relief after its expulsion, unable to talk because of obstruction in the throat, does not get sleep when he lies down because of catching of breath, finds comfort in sitting posture only, desires warm comfort, eycs widely opened, sweating on the brows, severe pain ( in the chest ), dryness of the mouth, changes postures every moment, symptoms get increased during cloudy days, by the use of cold water, breeze from east, and other causes increasing kapba. These are the symptoms of Tamaka Swasa ( bronchial asthma ) which persists throughout the patient's life even with suitable treatment. It might also get cured if it is of very recent onset. ( 27-34 )

# ज्वरमूच्छौपरीतस्य विद्यात्मतमकं तु तु तम् । उदावर्तरजोजीर्णक्लिश्चकायनिरोधजः ॥ ३५ ॥

This disease ( tamaka swasa ) if caused by upward movement of ( reverse peristalisis ) alimentary tract, inhalation of dust, indigestion of uncooked foods, or foods soaked in water, suppression of urges of the body or wrong practice of yoga asanas and such others causes will manifest with fever and faintings as additional symptoms. It is then known as Pratamaka ( śwāsa ). ( 35 )

# तमस्ता वर्धतेऽत्यर्थं शीतैश्चाशु प्रशास्यति । मज्जतस्तमसीवास्य विद्यात्संतमकं तु तम् ॥ ३६ ॥

( च. चि. अ. २१ )

Tamaka Swāsa which is due to increase of tamas ( darkness, cloudy weather

inactivity ) makes the patient to go deep into darkness ( stupor ). He finds comfort by cold things quickly. This condition is called Santamaka. ( 36 )

टिति स्त्रायासोद्भवः कोष्ठे श्रुद्रश्वासो न सोऽत्यर्थं दुःखेनाक्ष्मण्डः द्वेनाकृप्रबाधकः ।। २० ॥ क्त्र्यादास्त्र्यं नात् उद्योग्यते उद्योत्रे । न च मोजनपानानां निरुणद्व्युचितां गतिम् ॥ ३८ ॥ हिनास्त न स साधारण में च खुलादृद्धुजम् । स साध्य उक्तो बलिनः सर्वे चाव्यक्तलब्धूबणः ।। ३९ ।। ( च. चि. अ. २१ )

Ksudra Śwāsa is the one which is due to use of dry foods, physical effort, is mild, does not cause pain anywhere, nor causing any discomfort or anxiety like the millo, does not obstruct the passages of food or respiration, nor interferes with the functioning of the sense organs nor produces any pain and is curable. All other functioning of the school organisms.
kinds are also curable when they are mild and affecting strong persons. ( 37–39 )

क्षुद्रः साध्यो मतस्तेषां तमकः क्रञ्ड्यु उच्यते । त्रयः श्वासाः न सिध्यन्ति तमको दुर्बलस्य च ॥ ४० ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ५१ )

Ksudra Śwāsa is curable, tamaka śwāsa is difficult to treat, and in weak persons it is incurable; the other three swasas are incurable. ( 40 )

कामं प्राणहरा रोगा बहवो न तु ते तथा । यथा श्वासश्च हिक्का च हरतः प्राणमाश्यु च । । ४१ ।। ( च. चि. अ. २१ )

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरविते माधवनिदाने हिक्काश्वासनिदानं समाप्तम् ।। १२ ॥

Diseases which take away life are very many, but none are as quick as swasa and hikka. ( 41 )

Thus ends the chapter on Swasa-hikka.

## अथ स्वर मेदनिदानम्

# SWARABHEDA NIDANAM ( HOARSENESS OF VOICE )

#### अत्यु श्वभाषण विषाधययनाभिधातसंदू षणौः प्रकृपिताः पचनादयस्तु । स्रोतः स्त्र स्वरवद्देषु गताः प्रतिष्ठां द्वन्युः स्वरं भवति वापि हि षड्डिधः सः ।। १ ॥ ( वातादिभिः पृथक् सर्वैमेंद्स। च क्षयेण च । )

( सु. उ. अ. ४३ )

Speaking in high pitch, effect of poisons, loud study, injury and such other causes make for increase of vata and other dosas which in turn lodge in the organs of voice, producing hoarseness. This is of six kinds, one from each of the dosas, one from combination of all three, fifth from medas ( adipose tissue ) and sixth from kşaya ( depletion of body tissues ). ( 1 )

वातेन कृष्णनयनाननमूत्रवर्चा भिन्नं शनैर्वदति गर्दभवत् खरं च ! पित्तेन पीतनयनाननमूत्रवर्चा व्याद्भलेन स च दाहसमन्वितेन ॥ २ ॥ ब्रयात्कफेन सततं कफरुद्धकण्ठः स्वब्पं शनैर्वदति चापि दिवा विशेषात् । सर्वात्मके भवति सर्वविकारसंपत्तं चाप्यसाध्यमृषयः स्वरमेद्दमाहुः ॥ ३ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ५३ )

Blackish discolouration of eyes, face, urine and feces, voice broken, slow, harsh and resembling the voice of a donkey are the symptoms in vātaja type.

Yellowish discolouration of the eyes, face, urine and feces; burning sensation in the throat are found in pittaja type.

In kaphaja type, obstruction to the throat by kapha ( thick fluid ); slow, very little speech and that too slightly better during day time are the symptoms.

All the symptoms of all the dosas coexist together in tridosaja type, which is incurable in the opinion of the sages. ( 2-3 )

# धूप्येत वाक् क्षयक्ते क्षयमाप्रयाच्य वागेष चापि इतवाक् परिवर्जनीयः । अन्तर्गतस्वरमल्र्श्यपदं चिरेण मेद्रोऽन्वयाद्वदति दिन्धगल्न्स्तुषार्तः ॥ ४ ॥ ( सु. उ. अ. ४२ )

In kşayaja type, voice comes out very feeble associated with a feeling of hot furnes coming out of the throat; if the voice is completely lost, it cannot be treated.

In medaja type, the voice appears as though coming from great depth, syllables not clear and is very slow, person will have coating ( of fat or kapha ) in the throat and also thirst. ( 4 )

क्षीणस्य वृद्धस्य क्रुशस्य वाऽपि चिरोत्थितो यश्च सहोपजातः । मेदस्विनः सर्वसमुद्भवश्च स्वरामयो यो न स स सिद्धिमेति ॥ ५॥ ( सु. उ. अ. ५३ )

इति श्रीमाधवरुपविरचिते साधवनिदाने स्वरभेदनिदानं समाप्तम् १ १ = ।

Swarabheda is incurable in persons who are very weak, very aged, emaciated; which is long standing or congenital, in persons who are obese and that which is produced by all the three dosas together. ( 5 )

Thus ends the chapter on Swarabheda.

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

# अथारोचकनिदानम्

## AROCAKA NIDANAM ( ANOREXIA )

# वातादिभिः शोकभयातिलोभक्तोधैर्मनोधार्मनोधार्शनरूपगन्धैः ।

अरोचकाः स्युः,

( च. चि. अ. २६ )

Arocaka ( anorexia )' is produce I by the three dosas, grief, fear, greed, anger, and from foods, the sight and smell of which are very unpleasant to the mind. ( 1 )

## परिद्व प्रदन्तः कषायवऋश्च मतोऽनिलेन ॥ १ ॥ किद्यम्लमुण्णं विरसं च पूति पित्तेन विद्यात् लवणं च वऋम् । माधुर्यपैक्छल्यमुरुत्वशत्यविबद्धसंबद्धसंबद्धसंबद्धयूतं कर्फेन ॥ २ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २६ )

Tingling inside the gums, astringent taste in the mouth are seen in vataja type; bitter and sour taste, warmth and bad smell in the mouth in pittaja type; salty or sweet taste, mouth thickly coated with kapha which is sticky and feeling of cold arc seen in kaphaja type. ( 2 )

# अरोचके शोकभयातिलोमकोधाद्यहृद्याश्विगन्धजे स्थात् । स्वाभाविकं चारयमथारुचिध्य त्रिद्राणजे नैकरसं भवेत्तु ॥ ३ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २६ )

In arocaka due to grief, fear, greed, anger and other mental affections or that due to bad food or smell, the person will have no specific tastes as in doseaja types but in tridosaja type, many kinds of tastes are experienced by the patient. ( 3 )

# हृद्धूल्हपीडनयूतं पवनैन, पित्तात्त उदाहचोषवद्दलं, सकफप्रसेकम् । श्लेष्मात्मकं, बहुरुजं बहुभिध्य विद्याद्वैगुण्यमोहजडताभिरथापरं च ॥ ४ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २६ )

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदानेऽरोचकनिदानं समाप्तम् ॥ १४ ॥

Pain in the region of the heart is found in vataja; thirst, burning sensation and local heat in pittaja; watery exudations from the mouth, nose, etc., in kaphaja, many kinds of discomforts in tridosaja type; delusion and dullness of the mind in the last type are seen predoninantly. ( 4 )

Thus ends the chapter on Arocaka.

<sup>1.</sup> Inability to eat food though hungry is known as aruci ( arocaka ); aversion to foods which were once liked is annabhinandana, hatredness to food even by its mere hearing, remembering, sight, smell or touch is bhaktadwesa. All these three are included in the term arocaka by both Caraka and Susruta. - Madhukosa tika.

# अथ छदिनिदानम्

# CHARDI NIDANAM ( VOMITTING )

# दु प्रैदोषैः पृथक् सर्वेंबीभत्सालोचनादिभिः । छद्देयः पञ्च विक्षेयास्तासां लक्ष्णमुच्यते ।। १ ॥

Chardi ( vomitting ) is of five kinds, one from each dosa, fourth from the combination of all the three together and fifth from the sight of unpleasant things, etc. ( 1 )

#### अतिद्रवैरतिस्हिग्घैरहचैलेषुवैज्ञेन्द्रवेदनि । अकालै व्यातिमात्रैश्च तथाऽस्तारम्यैश्च भोजनैः ।। २ ॥ श्रमाञ्जूषा त्त्रथोद्वेगादृजीर्णात् किमिदोषतः । नार्याश्चापन्नस्वायास्त्रशयास्त्रस्यायास्त्रधाऽतिद्रुतमश्र्तः ।। ३ ॥ बीभत्सै हें तुमिश्चान्यैद्रुतमुत्को शितो बलात् । छादयन्नाननं व वेगैरर्द् यन्नुभञ्जनैः । निरुच्यते छदिरिति दोषो वक्र प्रधावितः ॥ ४ ॥ ( सु. उ. अ. 6६ )

Indulgence in foods which are very watery, very fatty, unpleasant, very salty; taking meals at odd time, in excess quantity and of unaccustomed food; physical exertion, fear, sudden emotions, indigestion, infection by worms, pregnancy in women, eating foods very quickly, sight of terrific, fearsome, ugly and such other which fill the mouth (a vital and similar causes make for sudden increase of dosas; which fill the mouth ( with contents of the stomation increase of adopt increase of adosas ing discomfort to the whole body. ( 2-4 )

हृहासोद्गाररोधौ च प्रसेको लवणस्तुः । द्वेषोऽन्नपाने च भूशं वमीनां पूर्वलक्षणम् ॥ ५ ॥ ( सु. उ. अ. ४६ )

Oppression in the chest, obstruction of belchings, thin ( watery ), salty salivation and aversion to foods and drinks are the premonitory symptoms of vomiting.(5)

हरुपार्श्व वीडामुख्यो षशीर्षनाम्यति कासस्वरभेदतोदैः उद्गारशब्द्यवलं सफेन विविधित्रकृष्ण तनुक कषायम् । क्त्रञ्जूषा चालपं मद्दता च वेगेनार्तोऽनिलाच्छद्दयतीह् दुःसम्ब् ॥ ६ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २३ )

Pain in the region of the heart and flanks; dryness of the mouth, discomfort in head and umbilicus, cough, hoarseness of voice, great sound during belching; interrupted bouts of vomitting, bringing out scanty, blackish, thin, astringent material, with great effort accompanied with pain all over the body are the symptoms of vataja type of vomitting. ( 6 )

# मूच्छी पिपासामुख शोषमूर्धताब्वक्षिसन्तापतमो भ्रमार्तः पीतं भूशोध्णं हरितं सतिकं धूम्रं च पित्तेन वमेरसद्दाहम् ॥ ७ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २३ )

Fainting, severe thirst, dryness of mouth, burning sensation in the head, palate and eyes; darkness before the eyes, giddiness, bringing yellowish, greenish or black with reddish tinge, very hot, bitter material with burning sensation are the symptoms of pittaja vomitting. ( 7 )

# तन्द्रास्यमा धूर्यक्रफप्रसेक्सन्तो षनिद्रार्श्वनीरवार्तः स्विग्धं घनं स्वादु कफाद्विशुद्धं सरोमद्दर्शोऽरुपरुजं वमेत्तु ॥ ८॥ ( च. चि. अ. २३ )

Stupor, sweet taste in the mouth, excessive salivation, a feeling of happiness ( which is abnormal ), sleepishness, anorexia, feeling of heaviness of the body, bringing out fatty, thick, sweetish, white coloured material, accompanied with horripulations and little or no pain are the symptoms of kaphaja type. ( 8 )

#### शुलाविपाकारुचिदाहरुदृष्णाश्वासप्रमोहम्बला प्रसक्तम् । छदिस्त्रिदोषालुवणाम्लनीलसान्द्रोष्णरक्तं वमतां नृणां स्यात् ॥ ९ ॥ ( च. वि. अ. २३ )

Pain in the abdomen, indigestion, anorexia, burning sensation, thirst, increased/difficult respirations, delusions, being severe and constant, vomitted material being salty or sour, blue or red in colour, semisolid and hot are the symptoms of tridosaja type. ( 9 )

#### विटरवेदमूत्राम्बुबद्धानि वायुः स्रोतांसि संरुध्य यदोध्वमेति । उत्सन्नदोषस्य समाचित तं दोषं समुद्धय नरस्य कोष्ठात् ॥ १० ॥ विण्मूत्रयोस्तत्समगन्धवर्ण प्रच्छद्येद् दुष्टमिहातिवेगात्तयाऽर्दितश्चाशु विनाशमेति ॥ ११ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २३ )

If the dosas invade and disturb the organs of feces, sweat, urine and body fluids and produce vomitting from deep inside the alimentary tract, the vomitted meterial will have the smell and colour similar to feces and urine; thirst, increased/ difficult respiration and hiccough which are constant and continuous bouts of vomitting with great force associated with severe discomfort - these are the symptoms of incurable vomitting. ( 10-11 )

बीभत्सजा दौहूँदजाऽऽमजा च ह्यसात्म्यजा च किमिजा च या हि । सा पश्चमी तां च विभावयेच दोषोच्छ्येणैव यथोक्तमादौ ।। १२ ॥ शूलहूल्यासबहुला किजिमा च विशेषतः । किमिहद्दोगतुल्येन लक्षणेन च लक्ष्मिता ।। १३ ॥

( सु. उ. अ. ४६ )

Vomitting due to unpleasant sight, etc., pregnancy, indigestion, unaccustomed

foods, parasitic infections are included in the fifth kind. Symptoms of dosas are to loods, parasitic meculers are and en a lier. In krimija ( caused by parasitic infection ) be noted in them as described "canner " prominent and symptoms of krimija hrdroga
pain in the abdomen and nausea are " " " " " " " " " " " " " " " " " " pain in the 'abdomen' and '
{ heart disease produced by worms } will also be found. ( 12-13 )

क्षीणस्य या छदिरतिप्रसक्ता सोपद्रवा शोणितपूर्ययुक्ता । क्षाणस्थं

ending in very emaciated persons, too many in number, associated with Vomitting in Very emachaese possess, pus and white shining particlescomplications, volinted material comments
is incurable; the curable ones and those without complications are to be treated. (14)

lcurable, the sail
( कासश्वासोऽज्वरो हिन्द्रा तृष्णा चैचित्र्यमेव च । हृद्रोगस्तमकश्चैष ज्ञेयादृश्यद्रवाद्याः सर्वे अपूर्णा ( च. चि. अ. २३ )

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने छदिनिदानं ममाप्नम् ।। १५ ।।

( Cough, increased/difficult respirations, fever, hiccough, thirst, mental torpi-( Cough, increasedpunnedit "roppractions) — are the complications of vomitting).
dity, heartache, and feeling of being in darkness-are the complications of vomitting).

Thus ends the chapter on Chardi.

58

#### अथ तृष्णानिदानम्

## TRSNA NIDANAM ( THIRST )

# भयश्रमाम्यां वलसंक्षयाद्या ह्यध्व चित्रं पित्तविवर्धनैश्य। पित्तं सवातं कुपितं नराणां तालुप्रपन्नं जनयेरिपपासाम् । स्नोतस्वपांचाहिषु दूर्षितेषु दोषैश्च तृद्ध संभवती हू जन्तोः ।। १ ।।

Pitta associated with vata, having undergone increase by fear, fatigue, loss of strength and other factors which increase pitta, etc., invade and disturb the organs of the fluid system of the body and getting localised in the palate, produce trşnā ( severe thirst ). ( 1 )

# तिस्नः स्मृतास्ताः क्षतजा चतुर्थी क्षयात्तथा ह्यामसमुद्भवा च । भक्तोद्भवा सप्तमिकेति तासां निबोध लिङ्गान्यनुपूर्वंशस्तु ॥ २॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ४८ )

It is of seven kinds, one from each dosa, the fourth from ulcerations, fifth from depletion or loss of body tissues, sixth from ama ( accumulation of undigested material ) and seventh from bad food; the symptoms of these are described further on serially. ( 2 )

## क्षामास्यता मारुतसंभवायां तोदस्तथा शङ्कश्शिरःसु चापि। स्नोतोनिरोधो विरसं च वक्रः शीताभिरद्भि विवृद्धिमेति ॥ ३॥ ( सु. उ. अ. ४८ )

Pinched or dry face, pricking pain in the temples and head, obstruction of passages ( of fluid ), bad taste in the mouth, and increase of thirst by drinking cold water-are the symptoms of vataja trsna. ( 3 )

# मूच्छान्नविद्वेष विल्हापदा रक्तेक्षणत्वं प्रततश्च शोषा। शीताभिनन्दा मुखतिक्तता च पित्तात्मिकायां परिदूयनं च ।। ४ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ४८ )

Fainting, aversion to food, delerium, burning sensation, reddish eyes, insatiable thirst, comfort by cold, bitter taste in the mouth, burning sensation ( and also yellowish urine, feces, eyes, etc. } are the symptoms of pittaja type. ( 4 )

# बाष्पावरोधात्कफसंव्रतेऽस्यौ तृष्णा बलासेन भवेच्या त् ।

## निद्वा गुरुवं मधुरास्यता च तुष्णादितः शुध्यति चातिमात्रम् ॥ ५ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ४८ )

Kaphaja type occurs by the obstruction of heat of pitta inside the body by the

increased kapha. Sleep more than usual, heaviness of the body, sweet taste in the mouth and the person becomes emaciated quickly in this type. ( 5 )

> क्षतस्य रुक्तशोणितनिर्गमाभ्यां तृष्णा चतुर्थी क्षतजा मता त । रसक्षयाद्या क्षयसंभवा सा तया तया विभूतश्च निशादिनेषु ॥ ६ ॥ पेपीयतेऽम्भः स सुखं न याति तां सन्निपातादिति केचिदाहः । रसक्षयोक्तानि च लक्ष्णानि तस्यामशेषेण भिषण्यवस्येत् ॥ ७ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ४८ )

Ksataja type is caused by pain and bleeding due to ulcerations on the body.

Ksayaja type results from loss of rasa dhātu ( the first and predominantly watery tissue of the body ). It persists both during day time and nights, does not get relieved by any amount of water intake and manifests other symptoms of rasa ksaya ( pain, tremors, feeling of dryness and emptiness of the heart, dryness of the mouth, skin, etc. ). Some authoritie: consider this type ( kṣayaja tr̥sṇā ) as sannipataja { caused by all the three dosas together }. { 6-7 }

# त्रिदोषलिङ्गाऽऽमसमुद्भवा च हच्छलनिष्ठीवनसादकर्त्री । स्निग्धं तथाऽम्लं लवणं च भुक्तं गुर्वन्नमेवाशु तृषां करोति ।। ८ ।। ( मू. उ अ. ४८ )

Amaja type also presents the symptoms of all three dosas, along with heartache, expectoration of sputum and weakness. Annaja type is produced by ingestion of very fatty, sour, salty and heavy foods (more in quantity or indigestible). (8)

दीनस्वरः प्रताम्यन् दीनः संशुध्कवक्रमलतालुः । भवति स्वलु योपसर्गां तुष्णा सा शोधिणी कष्टा ।। ९।। ( च. चि. अ. २४ )

It is very difficult to treat patients of thirst who have developed feeble voice, bouts of delusion, ( or unconsciousness ), restlessness, severe dryness of mouth, throat and palate and emaciation. ( 9 )

## ज्वरमोहक्षयकासश्वासाद्यपस्युदेहानाम् । ( च. चि. अ. २४ )

सर्वास्त्वतिप्रसक्ता रोगक्वयुक्तानाम् । घोरोपद्रवयुक्तास्तृष्णा मरणाय विश्लेयाः ॥ १० ॥ ( सु. उ. अ. ४८ )

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते मान्रवनिदाने तृष्णानिदानं समाप्तम् ।। १६ ।।

Fever, delusion, emaciation, cough, increased/difficult respiration are the complications of trsnā.

Any type of trana, which is very severe and seen in persons who are debilitated by other diseases, who are having vomittings concurrently, that associated with complications are to be understood as causing death. ( 10-11 )

Thus ends the chapter on Trsnā.

# अथ मूच्छाभ्रमनिद्रातन्द्रासंन्यासनिदानम्

# MÜRCHĀ, BHRAMA, NIDRA, TANDRĀ, SANNYĀSA NIDĀNAM ( FAINTING, GIDDINESS, STUPOR AND LOSS OF CONSCIOUSNESS )

## क्षीणस्य बहुदोषस्य विरुद्धाहारसेविनः । वेगाघातादभिधाताद्वीनसत्त्वस्य वा पूनः ।। १ ॥ करणायतनैषुग्रा बाह्येष्वास्थन्तरेषु च । निविशन्तदा मुच्छन्ति मानवाः ।। २ ।।

Persons who are emaciated ( debilitated ), in whom the dosas have undergone profound increase, those who indulge in incompatible foods and suppression of urges, those who are injured and those who are mentally weak, become afflicted with the disease murcha ( fainting ), when the dosas invade the external and internal organs of the mind1. ( 1-2 )

# संशावहासु नाडीषु पिहितास्वनिलादिभिः । तमोऽभ्यूपैति सहस्य सुखदुःख्ययपोहद्युत् ॥ ३ ॥ सुखद् खन्यपोद्दाच्य नरः पतति काष्ठवत् । मोहो मूच्छेति तामाहुः षड्विधा सा प्रकीर्तिता ॥ ४ ॥ वातादिभिः शोणितेन मद्येन व विषेण च । घटरवण्येतास्यु पित्तं तु प्रभुत्वेनावतिष्ठते ।। ५ ।। ( स्. उ. अ. ४६ )

When the sajñavaha srotas ( channels that carry sensations ) are blocked by the increased dosas, the person goes into utter darkness, without any knowledge of pleasure or pain ( becomes unconscious ) and falls on the ground like a log of wood. This condition known as moha or murcha is of six kinds; one from each dosa, one from rakta, one from madya ( alcoholic beverages ), next from viṣa ( poisons ). In all, pitta is the predominant dosa. ( 3-5 )

# हृत्यीडाः जम्भणं ग्लानिः संक्षादौर्वल्यमेव च । सर्वासां पूर्वद्रपाणि, यथास्वं ता विभावयेत् ॥ ६ ॥ ( सु. उ. अ. ४६ )

Heartache, excessive yawning, weakness, inability of sense organs to perceive are the premonitory symptoms of all kinds of murcha. ( 6 )

# नीलं वा यदि वा कृष्णमाकाशमथवाऽरुणम् । पद्यंस्तमः प्रविशति शीध्रं च प्रतिबुध्यते ।। ७ ॥ वेपशुश्राक्षमद्र्श्य प्रपीडा हृदयस्य च । कार्श्य ध्यावाऽरुणाच्छाया मूच्छाये वातसंभवे ।। ८ ॥ In vātaja mūrchā, the person sees the sky ( or his surroundings ) in blue,

<sup>1. (</sup> External are the five sense organs and the internal are the channels of the mind, the brain and neryous system. )

black or crimson colours, goes into darkness, falls down unconscious but regains consciousness quickly; tremors, pain all over the body, severe pain in the heart, emaciation, blackish red discolouration of the body are seen. ( 7-8 )

# रक्तं इरितवणं वा वियत्पीतमधापि वा । पद्यंस्तमः प्रविशति सस्वेद्ध्य प्रश्यते ।। ९ ॥ ( सपिपासः ससन्तापो रक्तपीनाकुलैक्षणः । ) ( जातमात्रे पनति च श्रीम्बं च प्रतिद्रुध्यते । ) संभिन्नवर्चाः पीताभो मूर्च्छाये पित्तसंभवे ॥ १० ॥

Sky ( surroundings ) appearing in red, green or yellow colours, loss of consciousness and regaining it after perspiration; ( thirst, increase of body temperature, eyes red or yellow, loss of consciousness and quick regaining ) diarrhoea and yellowish discolouration of the body are the symptoms of pittaja murcha. ( 9–10 )

# मेघसंकाशमाकाशमान्नतं वा तमोघनेः । पदयंस्तमः प्रविशति चिराच्च प्रतिबुध्यते ।। ११ ॥ गुरुमिः प्रात्रृतैरद्वैयँवाद्र्ण चर्मणा । सप्रसेकः सहृद्धायो मूच्छाये कफसंभवे ॥ १२ ॥

In kaphaja mürcha, the person sees the sky full of thick clouds, falls unconscious regains consciousness after long time, feels as though his body is covered with heavy moist leather, has exudations from the mouth and nausea are the symptoms. ( 11-12 )

# सर्वाकृतिः सन्निपाताद्यस्मार इवागतः । स जन्तुं पातयत्याया बिना वीभत्सचेप्टितैः॥ १३ ॥ ( अ. ह. नि. अ. ६ )

In sannipataja type ( due to all the dosas together ) there will be manifestation of all the symptoms together, the person appears as a patient of Apasmāra but without bībhatsa cestā1. ( 13 )

# पुथिब्यापस्तमोहन्तं रक्तगन्धस्त्रद्ययः । तस्माद्रक्तस्य गन्वेन मूच्छनित भुविमानवाः ॥ १४ ॥ द्रव्यस्वभाव इत्येके दृश्यद् मिमुह्यति । ( मृ. उ. श. ४६ )

Rakta ( blood ) is composed of Prthvī and Ap bhūtas which have tamo guņa predominant in them. So some persons ( those possessing more of tamo guna in their constitution) become unconscious and fall to the ground at the sight or smell of blood. Some attribute this to the specific property of the blood. ( 14 )

# ग्रणास्तीवतरत्वेन स्थितास्त्र विषमद्ययोः ॥ १५ ॥ त एव तस्मात्ताभ्यां तु मोहो स्यातां यथेरितो । ( ग्र. उ. अ. ४६ ) स्तब्धाङ्गद्यष्टिस्त्वस्त्रज्ञा गूढोच्छासश्च मूर्चिछतः ॥ १६ ॥

<sup>1.</sup> Froth coming out of the mouth, grinding of teeth, rolling of eyes, hallucinations and convulsions are known as bibhatsa ccsta and are invariably seen in apasmara ( epilepsy ).

# मद्येन विल्रपञ्जेते नष्ठविभ्रान्तमानसः । गान्नाणि विक्षिपन् भूमौ जरां यावन्न याति तत् ॥ १७ ॥ वेषशुस्वप्नतृष्णाः स्युस्तमश्च विषमूर् चित्रते । वेदितव्यं तीवतरं यथास्वं विषलक्षणः ॥ १८ ॥

( म. उ. अ. ४६ )

In mürcha caused by poisons and alcoholic drinks, the symptons are very profound. In raktaja mūrch i stiffness of the body and fixed gaze are seen; in madyaja ( alcoholic ) inconsistent speech, falling on the ground with toss of mental activities; in-coordinated movements of the body, these symptoms disappearing soon after the drink is digested; in visaja ( due to poison ) tremors ( rigors ), sleep, thirst and feeling of darkness and other symptoms specific to the particular poison ous material will be present. ( 15-18 )

# मूच्छा पित्ततमःप्राया रजःपित्तानित्छाद्धमः । तमोवातकः कात्तन्द्रा निद्दा श्रुंध्मतमोभवा ॥ १९ ॥

Mürcha is caused by predominance of pitta and tannas, bhrama by rajas, pitta and vāta; tandrā by tamas, vāta an l kapha an i nidrā by kapha and tamas. ( 19 )

# ( चकवद्ध्रमतो गात्रं भूमौ पतति सर्वदा । भ्रमरोग इति ज्ञेयो रजःपित्तानिलात्मकः ॥ १ ॥ )

( The body reeling like a turning wheel and the person falling on the ground always is known as bhrama roga ( giddincss ) and it is due to prominence of rajas, pitta and vata. )

# इन्द्रियार्थंब्वसंवित्तिगौरवं जुम्भणं क्लमः । निद्रार्तस्येव यस्येद्वा तस्य तन्द्रां विनिर्दिशेत् ॥ २० ॥

Non-perception of their objects oy the sense organs, feeling of heaviness ( of the body ), excessive yawning, weakness even without physical work, person appearing as though s ceping are the symptoms of tandra ( stupor ). ( 20 )

# दोषेषु मदम्ब्छायाः कृतवेगेषु देहिनाम् । स्वयमेवोपशास्यान्त संन्यासो नौषधैर्विना ॥ २१ ॥ ( अ. ह. नि. अ. ६ )

In cases of mada, murcha, etc., the person becomes alright soon after the bout is over and even without medicines, whereas sannyasa ( another similar disease ) does not subside without treatment. ( 21)

# वाग्देहमनसां चेण्डामाक्षिप्यातिबन्छा मलाः । संन्यस्यन्त्यबल्लं जन्तुं प्राणायतनमाश्रिताः ॥ २२ ॥ स ना संन्याससंन्यस्तः कार्ष्ठीभूतो मृतोपमः ।

प्राणैविमुच्यते शीघ्र' मुक्त्वा सद्यःफलां कियाम् ॥ २३ ॥ ( अ. हू. नि. अ. ६ )

ड्नि श्रीमाधवकरविरचित्रं माधवनिदानं भ्रमनिद्रातन्द्रामंन्यासनिदानं समासम् ॥ १७ ।।

The dosas which have become very powerful invade the chief seat of prāņa i. e., the hrdaya and makes a debilitated person unconscious and fall on the ground. All the organs of the speech, body and mind become functionless and even with quick restorative treatment the person is sure to die quickly. This disease is known as sannyasa ( coma ). ( 22-23 )

Thus ends the chapter on Murcha, etc.

# अथ पानात्ययपरमद्रपानाजीर्णपानविभ्रमनिदानम्

# PÅNÅTYAYA, PARAMADA, PÄNAJIRNA-PÅNAVIBHRAMA NIDÅNAM. ( ALCOHOLIC INTOXICATION )

ये विषस्य गुणाः प्रोक्तास्तेऽपि मद्ये प्रतिष्ठिताः । तेन मिथ्योपयुक्तेन भवत्युग्रो मदात्ययः ॥ १ ॥

All the qualities and properties present in poisons are also found in madya ( alcoholic beverages ), hence its indiscriminate use causes madātyaya ( intoxication ). ( 1 )

# किंतु मद्यं स्वभावेन यथैवान्नं तथा रम्मृतम् । अयुक्तियुक्तं रोगाय युक्तियुक्तं यथाऽम्मृतम् ॥ २ ॥ प्राणाः प्राणभृतामन्नं तदयुक्तया द्विनस्त्यसून् । विषं प्राणद्वरं तद्य युक्तियुक्तं रसायनम् ॥ ३ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. १२ )

Alcoholic drinks are similar to food, improperly used they cause diseases but used properly act as nectar; food sustains life if taken properly whereas it kills if it is improper; similarly also the poison, if improperly used it kills, but used judiciously it acts as a rejuvinator. ( 2-3 )

विधिना मात्रया कालै हितैरस्रैयंथाबलम् । प्रहृष्टो यः पिबेन्मद्यं तस्य स्याद्मम्रतोपमम् ॥ ४ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. १२ )

# स्निग्धैरतदश्रैमीसैश्च भक्ष्यैश्च सद्व सेवितम् । भवेदायुःप्रकर्षाय बलायोपचवयाय च ।। ५ ॥ काम्यता मनसस्तुष्टिस्तेजो विकाम एव च । विधिवरसेय्यमानै तु मद्ये संनिहिता गुणाः ।। ६ ।। ( सु. उ. अ. ४७ )

Alcohol taken in accordance with proper procedure, dose, time, combination of food, etc., and of body strength gives happiness ( joy ) and .acts like nectar. Alcohol taken with fatty foods, meat or other eatables rich in fat, makes for increase of span of life and strength; those desiring happiness of the mind, physical strength and valour should take alcoholic drinks judiciously. { 4-6 }

# बुद्धिस्मृतिमीतिकरः सुखन्ध पानान्ननिद्रारविवर्धनश्च । संपाठगीतस्वरवर्धनश्च प्रोक्तोऽतिरम्यः प्रथमो मद्दो द्वि ।। ७ ।।

In the first stage of alcoholic effect, there is increase of intelligence, memory, desire, happiness, craving for drinks, foods, sleep, greater interest in reading and singing and increase of sound of speech or voice. Thus the first stage is of very pleasant experience. ( 7 )

# अव्यक्तबुद्धिस्मतिवाग्विचेण्डः सोन्मस्त्रतिरद्यशान्तः । आल्ह्यनिद्राभिहतो मुहुश्य मध्येन मत्तः पुरुषो मद्देन II ८ II

If the person enters the intermediary stage, his intellect, memory and speech become confused, physical activities become irregular, he acts like an insane, is uncontrollable, develops laziness and sleeps very frequently. ( 8 )

# गच्छेदगम्यान्न गुरूंध्य मन्येन खादेदभ्याणि च नप्रसंक्षः । ध्रयाच्च गुह्यानि हृदि स्थितानि मदे तृतीये पूरुषोऽस्वतन्त्रः ॥ ९ ॥

In the third stage, the person indulges in forbiliden acts and things, does not care for elets, eatsthings which are not to be caten, having lost control speaks out secrets hidden in his mind. ( 9 )

# चतुर्थे तु मदे मुद्रो भन्नदार्विव निष्क्रियः । कार्याकायविभागज्ञो मृतादृष्यपरो मृतः ॥ १० ॥ को मदं तादृश्य गच्छेद्रन्माद्मित्र चापरम् । बहुदोषमिवामूढः कान्तारं स्ववशः कृती ॥ ११ ॥

In the fourth stage, the person falls to the ground with unconsciousness like a cut tree, incapable of any activity like a dead body, unable to determine what to do and what not . Should anyone wish to be in such a condition ? It is like desiring going into a forest ( full of fear ). ( 10-11 )

# निर्मुक्तमेकान्तत पव मद्यं निषेव्यमाणं मनुजेन नित्यम् । आपादयेल्क प्रतमान्विकारानायादयेच्यापि शरीर मेदम् ।। १२ ।। ( मु. उ. अ. ८७ )

Only such a person who drinks ( alcoholic beverages ) on empty stomach and in solitude daily becomes a victim of very difficult diseases and even loss of life. ( 12 )

# कुद्धेन भीतेन पिपासितेन शोकाभितप्रंन वु भुक्षितेन । व्यायामभाराध्वपरिक्षतेन वेगावरोधामिहतेन चापि ॥ १३ ॥ अत्यम्बुभक्षावततोद्रेण साजीर्णभुक्तेन तथाऽबल्रैन । उष्णाभितश्रेन च सेव्यमानं करोति मद्यं विविधान्विकारान्वकारान् ॥ १४ ॥ ( मृ. उ. अ. ८३ )

Alcoholic drinks produce many diseases in persons afflicted with anger, fear, thirst, grief and hunger, debilitated by physical exercises, too much of walking, those inclulging in suppression of natural urges of the body, drinking large quantities of water, eating too much quantity of food or raw or undigestible foods. Those who are very weak and those exposed to too much of heat. ( 13-14 )

पानात्ययं परमदं पानाजीणमधापि वा । पानविभ्रमम् च तेषां वक्ष्यामि लक्षणम् ॥ १५ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ४७ ) ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Pānātyaya, paramada, pānājīma and pānavibhrama are the diseases due to alcoholic excess, the symptoms of which are described further. ( 15 )

हिक्काश्वस्थिरःकभ्पपार्श्वर्त्रत्रम्नागरैः । विद्याद्वद्रमुत्रापस्य वातप्रायं मद्दात्ययम् ॥ १६ ॥ त्रष्णादाहृज्वरस्वेदमोहातीसारविभ्रमेः । विद्याद्धरितवर्णस्य पित्तप्रायं मदात्ययम् ॥ १७ ॥ छन्दरोचकहृद्धासतन्द्रास्तैमित्यगौरवैः । विद्याच्छीतपर्शतस्य कफप्रायं मदात्ययम् । क्षेयस्त्रिदोषजश्यापि सर्वालिङ्गमेदारययः ॥ १८ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. १२ )

Hiccough, increased respiration, shaking of the head, pain in the flanks, loss of sleep and excessive talking are symptoms of panatyaya in which vata is the predominant dosa.

Thirst, burning sensation, fever, perspiration, diarrhoea, giddiness and greenish discolouration of the body are symptoms of panatyaya in which pitta is dominant.

Vomitting, anorexia, nausea, stupor, laziness, fecling of heaviness and cold are symptoms of panatyaya in which kapha is predominant.

Presence of all the symptoms together is found in tridosaja panatyaya. (16-18)

## श्लेष्मोच्छ्योऽङ्गगुरुता विरसास्यता च विण्मूत्रसन्तिरथ तन्द्रिररोचकश्च । लिङ्ग परस्य च मदस्य वदन्ति तज्ञास्तृष्णा रजा शिरसि सन्धिषु चापि मेदः ।।१९॥ (मु. उ. अ. ४७)

Experts enumerate the following as the symptoms of paramada-increase of kapha ( seen as excess salivation, running in the nose, etc. ) feeling of heaviness of the body, bad taste in the mouth, non-elimination of feces and urine; stupor, anorexia, thirst, pain in the head and joints. { 19 )

# आध्यानमुग्रमथ चोद्गिरणं विदाहः पानेऽजरां सम्पूपगच्छति लक्षणानि । हृद्गात्रतोदकफसंस्नवकण्ठधुमा मुच्छावमिज्वर शिरोरुजनप्रदाहाः ।। २० ।। द्वेषः सुरान्नविकृतेष्वापि तेषु तेषु तं पानविभ्रममुशन्त्यखिलेन धीराः । ( मु. उ अ ४७ )

Profound distention of the abdomen, vomitting, burning sensation and the drink not undergoing digestion at all-are the features of panajirna.

Pain in the region of the heart and of the whole body, excess salivation, running in the nose, feeling as though hot fumes are coming out of the throat, fainting, vomitting, fever, headache, severe burning sensation in the body, hatredness even towards different kinds of alcoholic drinks and food are the symptoms of panavibhrama as described by experts. ( 20 )

हीनोत्तरौष्ठमतिशीतममन्ददाहं तैल्ठप्रभारयमापि पानद्वतं व त्यजेत्त् ।। २१ ।। जिल्लीष्ठदन्तमसितं त्वथवाऽपि नोलं पोते च यस्य नयने रुधिरप्रभे वा । ( सु उ. अ. ४७ )

Hanging down of upper lip, feeling of very cold externally and very hot internally ( cold to touch but very hot inside the body ), face appearing as though smeared with oil, black or blue discolouration of the tongue, lips, teeth ( gums ) and eyes very red, seen in a patient intoxicated by alcohol should be refused treatment. ( 21 )

द्विकाज्यसै वमधुवेपथुपर्श्युत्ताः कास्सम्रमावपि च पानद्वतं भजन्ते ॥ २२ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ४७ )

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचित्ते माधवनिदाने पानात्ययपरमदपानाजीर्णपानविश्रमनिदानं समाप्तम् ॥ १८ ॥

Hiccough, fever, vomitting, rigors, pain in the flanks, cough and giddiness also manifest ( as complications ) in alcoholic intoxication. ( 22 )

Thus ends the chapter on Madatyaya.

## दाहनिद्दानम्

# DAHA NIDANAM ( BURNING SENSATION OF THE BODY )

# त्वचं प्राप्तः स पानोष्मा पित्तरकाभिमूर्च्छतः । दाहं प्रकुरुते घोर वित्तवत्तत्र भेषजम् ॥ १ ॥ ( सु. उ. अ. ४७ )

The heat of the alcoholic drinks, associating with pitta and rakta gets localised in the skin and produces severe burning sensation. The treatment for this condition is similar to that of pitta. ( 1 )

#### कृत्कादेहानुगं रक्तमुद्रिकं दक्षति ध्रुवम् । स उध्यते तृष्यते च ताम्राभस्ताम्रलोचनः ।। २ ।। लोहगन्धाद्मवदनो वहिनेवायकीर्यते । ( मु. उ. अ. ४७ )

The rakta present all over the body gets increased and causes burning sensation as though exposed to fire nearby; thirst, coppery red colour of skin and eyes, smell of iron in the body and mouth. ( 2 )

पित्तज्वरसमः पित्तात्स चाप्यस्य विधिः स्मृतः ॥ ३ ॥ ( सु. उ. व. ४७ )

In daha due to pitta, the symptoms and treatment are similar to those of pittajwara. ( 3 )

#### तृष्णानिरोधादृश्यातौ क्षीणे तेजः समुद्धतम् । सबाह्याभ्यन्तरं देहं प्रद्देश्मन्दचेतसः ॥ ४ ॥ ( सु. उ. व. ४७ ) संशुष्कगलताल्योष्ठो जिन्नां निष्कृष्य वेपते ।

By suppressing thirst ( not drinking water even when thirsty ) the heat of the body undergoing increase causes depletion of body fluids, producing burning sensation-both internal and external; reduced activity of the mind { semiconsciousness ), dryness of the throat, palate and lips, prolapse of the tongue, the patient being in severe distress. ( 4-5 )

# अस्त्रजः पूर्णकोष्ठस्य दाह्येऽन्यः स्यात्सुदुःसन्नः ॥ ५ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ४७ )

Another kind of daha is due to accumulation of blood in the various viscera caused by injury. This condition is dreadful. ( 5 )

धातुक्षयोरथो यो दाहरतेन मूर्च्छ्रावृद्धर्दितः । क्षामस्वरः कियाद्वीनः स्त्रसीदेद्दश्रृशपीडितः ॥ ६ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ४७ ) (क्षतजोऽनश्रत श्यास्त्रं शोचतोवाप्यनेकधा । तेनांतर्दह्यतेरुदयं तृष्णा मूच्छा प्रत्नापवान् ॥) (इति क.)

Daha caused by depletion of the tissues of the body produces fainting, thirst, feeble voice, loss of all activities and even loss of life. ( 6 )

In case of injury in a person who does not eat well or who is afflicied with grief, burning sensation is experienced in the interior of the body, associated with thirst, faintings and delerium.

# मर्मामिघात जोऽप्यस्ति सोऽसाध्यः सप्तमो मतः । सर्व एव च वर्ज्याः स्युः शोतगात्रस्य देहिनः ॥ ७ ॥ ( मृ. उ. अ. ४७ )

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरर्विते माध्रवनिदाने दाद्यनदानं समाप्तम् ।। १२ ।।

There is the seventh type of daha due to injury to vital organs which is incurable. All varities of daha are to be refused treatment if the body of the patient has become cold. ( 7 )

Thus ends the chapter on daha.

70

## अधान्मादनिद्यनम्

#### UNMÄDA NIDĀNAM ( INSANĪTY )

मदयन्त्युद्रता दोषा यस्मादुन्मार्गमानाः । मानसोऽयप्रतो व्याधिरुम्माद् इति कीर्तितः।।१।। (मु. उ. अ. ६२)

Dosas which have undergone increase and traversing upwards through the channels of the min 1 get localised in the mind and cause its abnormality. This disease known as unmida, is mainly a disease of the mind. ( 1 )

# एकैकशः सर्वश्राध्य दोषरन्यर्थमूर् र्डिछतः । मानसेन च दुःख्रेन स च पश्चविधों मतः ।। २ ।। विषाद्भवति पश्चश्च यथास्वं तत्र भेषजम् । स चात्रवृद्धस्तरणो मदसंज्ञां विभर्ति च ।। ३ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ६२ )

One from cach dosa, fourth from a combination of all the three, fifth from grief, etc , sixth from poisons are lits varieties; their treatment is as appropriate to the cause. This disease is also known as mada, if it is not fully developed and is of recent on et. ( 2-3 )

# विरुद्धद्रद्राश्चिमोजनानि प्रधर्षणं देवगुरुद्विजानाम् । उन्माद्हेनुर्भयद्वर्षपूर्वां मनोऽभिघातो विषमाश्च चेण्टाः ॥ ४ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. ९ )

Indulgence in foods which are incompatible, spoilt and unclean; showing disrespect to gods, preceptors ( elders ) and the twice born; affection of the mind due to sudden emotions like fear, joy, etc., and violent physical activities are the causes for unmāda. ( 4 )

## तैरल्पसच्य मलाः पद्मण्डा वुद्धेनिवासं हृदयं प्रद्रुष्य। स्रोतांस्यधिष्ठाय मनोवहानि प्रमोहयन्त्याशु नरस्य चेतः ॥ ५॥ ( च. चि. अ. ६४ )

In persons who are mentally weak, the dosas having undergone increase invade the hrdaya the seat of mind, get lodged in all the channels of the mind and bring about their derangement quickly. ( 5 )

#### सरवरिद्वच्य प्रयोकुला द्वास्त्रता द्व प्रश्योरता च । धोविभ्रमः

अवद्धवाक्त्वं हृदयं च शून्यं सामान्यमुन्माद्गदस्य लिङ्गम् ॥ ६ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. १४ )

Improper understanding and unsteadiness of the mind, in-coordination of

sight, feeling of fear, irrelevant talk, feeling of emptiness of heart ( mind ) are the common specific symptoms of unmada. ( 6 )

> रूक्षाव्यशीताश्रविरेकधातुक्षयोपवास्वैरनिल्लोऽतिव्रद्धः चिन्तादिदुष्टं हृदयं प्रदूष्य बुद्धि स्मृतिं चाप्युपहृन्ति शीघ्रम् ॥ ७ ॥ अस्थानद्वासस्मितन्त्रत्यगीतवागद्गविक्षेपणरोदनानि पारुष्यकाद्यारुणवर्णताश्र जीणें बलं चानिलजस्य रूपम् ॥ ८ ॥ ( च. चि.[अ. १४ )

Indulgence in foods which are dry, scanty or cold; ( frequent ) purgations ( inclusive of vomitting ), depletion of basic tissues, fasting or starvation and other factors make for increase of vata in the body. Vata, in turn getting exerted by emotions like worry, etc., in persons of weak mind, brings about derangement of intellect and memory. ( 7-8 )

> अजीर्णकद्वम्लविदाह्यशीतैभोज्यैश्वितं प्रदीर्णवेगम् । उन्मादमत्युग्रमनात्मकस्य हृदि स्थितं पूर्ववदाशु कुर्यात् ॥ ९ ॥ अमर्षसंरम्भविनग्नभावाः सन्तर्जनातिद्भवणौण्यचोषाः । प्रच्छायशीतान्त्रजलाभिले।षः पीता च भाः पित्तकृतस्य लिङ्गम् ॥ १० ॥ ( च. चि. ३. १४ )

Laughing, smiling, dancing, singing, speaking, making movement of body parts and weeping at improper time and place; hardness/roughness, emaciation, blackish red discolouration of the body exacerbation of symptoms after digession of food-are the signs and symptoms of unmada caused by ( increase of ) vāta. ( 7-8 )

Indulgence in foods which are not cooked, which are very pungent or sour, which cause heart-burn, which are hot etc., make for increase of pitta, which in turn affects the mind quickly, in the same way as described earlier, in persons who are not selfcontrolled, leading to pittaja unmāda. Intolerance, uncontrollability, casting away the clothes and remaining naked, threatening others, running away, feeling of burning sensation, desiring shade, cold water and foods, yellowish discolouration of the body are the signs and symptoms of this condition. ( 9–10 )

> संपूरणैर्मन्दविचेष्टितस्य सोध्मा कफो मर्मणि संप्रदुष्टः । बुद्धिः स्मृतिं घाप्युपहृत्य चित्तं प्रमोहयन् संजनयेद्विकारम् ॥ ११ ॥ वाक्चेष्टितं मन्दमरोचकश्च नारीविविक्ताप्रयताऽतिनिद्रा । छदिश्च लाला च बलं च मुक्ते नखादिशौक्लुधं च कफारमके स्यात् ॥ १२॥(द्र चि. ३. १४)

Kapha getting increased along with pitta by indulgence in over-eating, lack of physical exercise etc., brings about derangement of intellect and memory and causes kaphaja unmāda. Its signs and symptoms are slow or weak voice ( or talk ) and body movements, anorexia, desire for women ( or sex ) and solitude, excessive

sleep, vomitting, saliva dribbing out, symptoms pronounced immediately after taking food, whitish discolouration of nails, etc. ( 11-12 )

# यः सन्निपातप्रभवोऽतिघोरः सर्वैः समस्तैः स च हेतुभिः स्यात् । सर्वाणि रूपाणि बिभर्ति तारद्वम्द्रमुद्धमैषज्यविधिर्विवर्त्यः ॥ १३ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. १४ )

Sannipātaja unmāda is produced by increase of all the three dosas together by their respective causes. The symptoms of all the dosas are seen in the patient and requires treatment of different kinds. This condition being profound is to be refused treatment. ( 13 )

# चोरैनरेन्द्रपुरुषैररिभिस्तथाऽन्यौविंत्रासितस्य गाढं क्षते मनसि च प्रियया रिरंसोर्जायेत चोरकटतमो मनसो विकारः ॥ १४ ॥ चित्रं व्रवीति च मनोऽनुगतं विसंक्षो गायत्यथो हृसति रोदिति चापि मूढः । ( मु. उ. अ. ६२ )

Getting frightened by ( sudden appearance or presence ) of robbers, government officials ( like police, etc. ) enemies and others ( wild animals, weapons and anything causing fear ), loss of money ( or wealth ), death of relatives ( next of kin such as wife, lover, sons, daughters, parents etc. ) excessive desire for sex and such other factors cause great injury to the mind and produce unmada. Person so affected talks indifferently, which he likes most, reveals even secrets, sings, laughs or weeps on his own accord and behaves senselessly. ( 14-15 )

# रक्तेक्षणो इतबलेन्द्रियभाः सुद्दीनः श्यावाननो विषकृतेऽथ्य भवेद्विसंक्षः ॥ १५ ॥ ( सु. ड. अ. ६२ )

In umada produced by poisons, the person will have red shot eyes, loss of strength of body, senses and complexion, helplessness, black discolouration of the mouth or face and loss of consciousness. ( 15 )

# अवाङ्मुखस्तूनुस्तो वा क्षीणमांसबल्ग्रे नरः । जागरूको ह्यसंदेहमुम्मादेन विनद्यति ।। १६ ॥

( मु. उ. अ. ३४ )

Patient of unmada who is found always keeping his head bent down or bent up, who is emaciated, weak and always awake ( without sleep ) is sure to die. ( 16 )

# अमर्त्यं वाग्विक्रमवीर्यंचेण्टो उम्मादकालोऽनियतश्य यस्य मूतोत्थमुन्माद्मुदाहरेत्तम् ॥ १७ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. १४ )

Bhūtonmāda ( unmāda due to possession by "spirits" ) is described as the disease in which the speech, valour, strength, intellect and knowledge of the affected person is super-human and the time of onset of the symptoms unspecific ( not in accordance with that of the dosas ). ( 17 )

# संतुष्टः शुचिरतिदि्व्यमाल्यगन्धो निस्तन्द्रीरवितथसंस्कृतप्रभाषी । तेजस्वी स्थिरनयनो वरप्रदाता ब्रह्मण्यो भवति नरः स देवजुष्टः ॥ १८ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ६० )

Contentment ( not expressing any desires ). cleanliness ( engaged in keeping his body, dress, surroundings, etc., very clean ), desire for very pleasant gariands, scents, etc., always alert and/or awake, always speaking refined ( cultured ) [anguage and truth, possessing bright complexion ( on the face ), unwinking eyes, bestowing favours and such other acts as seen in brahmanas are the symptoms of a person possessed by Deva graha ( gods/goddessess ). ( 18 )

# संस्वेदी द्विजगुरुदेवदोषवक्ता जिह्मास्तो विगतभयो विमार्गहष्टिः । संतुष्टो न भवति चान्नपानजातैरुर्घुशत्मा भवति स देवशत्रुजुष्टः ॥ १९ ॥ ( मु. उ. ३० )

Perspiring heavily, always abusing brāhmanas, teachers, gous, etc., distorted look, absence of any fear, critical of everything, never getting satisfied with fools and drinks, indulging in sinful acts are seen in the person possessed by Asura grahas ( demons ). ( 19 )

# हृष्टात्मा पुलिनवनान्तरोपसेवी स्वाचारः प्रियपरिगीतगन्धमाल्यः ।

नृत्यन्वै प्रहस्सति चारु चारुपशब्दं गन्धर्वग्रहपरिपीडितो मनुष्यः ॥ २० ॥ दुः इ. अ. ६० )

Contentment, roaming about on river banks, gardens, etc., indulzing in good deeds, fond of good company, songs, scents, garlands, danceing, laughing and speaking merrily are the symptoms of the person possessed by Gandharva grana. 20

# ताम्राक्षः प्रियतनुरक्तवस्त्रधारी गम्भीरो द्रुतगतिरल्पवाक् सन्द्विष्णुः । तेजस्वी वदति च किं ददामि कस्मै यो यक्षय्रद्वपरिपीडितो मनुष्यः ॥ २१ ॥ दुः = ३ ६० )

Red shot eyes, desire for good, thin and red coloured dress, steatiness in all activities, walking fast, very less speech, enduring any trouble, bright complexion of the face, enquiring others what they want from him are the symptomion of the person possessed by Yaksa graha. ( 21 )

# प्रेतानां स दिशति संस्तरेषु पिण्डान् शान्तात्मा जलम्पि चापसब्यवस्त्रः । मांसेप्सुस्तिलगुडपायसाभिकामस्तद्भक्तो भवति

He who is seen engaged in the act of offering pinda ( balls of copke Frice ) to the pitrs ( manes ) at places specified or otherwise ( even in water - puting on dress topsy turvy, desiring foods prepared with mutton, sesame jaggery and milk puddings is said to be possessed by Pitr graha. ( 22 )

# यस्तूबर्यां प्रसरति सर्ववत्कदाचित् स्वक्रण्यौ विलिहति जिल्लया तथैव । कोधालुर्गुडमधुदुर्धपायसेप्सुब्रातव्यो

Person possessed by Bhujanga graha is seen moving on the ground lying on his chest, licking things nearby with his tongue, getting into anger quickly, desiring jaggery, honey, milk and puddings. ( 23 )

# मांसास्वविधसुराविकारलिप्सुनिर्नुज्जो भृशमतिनिष्ठुरोऽतिशुरः । को धालुविंपुलबत्छो निशाविद्वारी शौचद्विड् भवति स राक्षसँग्रृहीतः ॥ २४ ॥

Person possessed by Raksasa graha is seen having great desire for foods prepared with mutton and blood, intoxicating drinks, total absence of shyress, is very cruel, very brave, angry, of great strength, roams about at nights as try cleanliness ( keeps himself and surrounding dirty ). ( 24 )

# उद्धस्तः क्रुशपरुषोऽऽचिरप्रलापी दुर्गन्धो भृशमश्युचिस्तथाऽतिलोलः । बह्वाशी विजनवनान्तरोपसेर्वी व्याचेश्चन् भ्रमति रद्दन् पिशाचजुष्टः ॥ २५ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ६० )

Person possessed by Pisaca graha is seen always keeping himself naked, is emaciated, talks irrelevant, emits bad smell, very dirty, very greedy for food, eats and drinks very heavily, runs about weeping alone in uninhabited places, forest, etc. ( 25 )

# स्थूलाक्षो द्रुतमटनः स फेनलैही निद्रालुः पतति च कम्पते च यो हि ।

यश्चाद्रिद्विरदनगादिविच्युतः स्यात् सोऽसाध्यो भवति तथा त्रयोदशाब्द्रे ॥ २६ ॥(मु. उ.अ. ६०) Bulging out of the cyes, running fast, licking the froth ( in the mouth ), constant tremors and a feeling as though he has fallen from a mountain, elephantback or a tree, etc., are the symptoms indicating incurability. Likewise is the case when the symptoms ( seizure ) manifest on the 13th day of the month ( 26 )

# देवश्रहाः पौर्णमास्यामसुराः सन्ध्ययोरपि । गन्धर्वाः प्रायशोऽष्टयां यक्षाश्च प्रतिपद्यथ ॥ २७ ॥ पिञ्याः कृष्णक्षये हिंस्युः पञ्चम्भयामपि चोरगाः । रक्षांसि रात्रौ वर्तत्र्थयं विशन्ति हि । २८ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ६० )

Deva grahas seize the person on full-moon days, asura grahas in the evenings, gandharvas on the cighth day, yakşas on first day, pitr grahas during the dark half of the month, naga grahas on the fifth day, raksasas during nights, and pisäca grahas on the fourteenth day. ( 27-28 )

# दर्पणादीन यथा छाया शोतोष्ण प्राणिनो यथा । स्वमणि मास्कराचिश्च यथा देहूं च देहभूक । विशान्ति च न दृश्यन्ते ग्रहास्तद्वच्छरीरिणः ।। २९ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ६० )

Just as an image in the mirror, experience of heat and cold by the body, sunlight in the gem or heat in fire are present (but cannot be felt materially ) so also the grahas cannot be felt physically even though they are present in the body
of the patient 4.90 \ of the patient. ( 29 )

# प्रविद्याशु शरीरं हि पीडां कुर्वन्ति दुःसहाम् ।। ३० ।

Having entered the body the grahas (spirits) create intolerable sufferings, (30)

( तपांसि तीव्वाणि तथैव दानं वतानि धर्मों नियमश्च सत्यम् । गुणास्तथाऽष्टावपि तेषु नित्या व्यस्ताः समस्ताश्च यथाप्रभावम् ॥ ३१ ॥ न ते मनुष्यैः सह संविशन्ति नवा मनुष्यान्कचिदाविशान्ति । ये त्वाविशन्तीति वदश्ति मोद्दान्ते भूतविद्याविषयाद्योद्याः ।। ३२ ॥ तेषां परिचारका ये कोटोसहस्राय तपद्यसंख्याः। अस्वसामांसभूजः सुभीमा निशाचिद्वाराश्च तथाऽऽविश्विश्लि ॥ ३३ ॥ )

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने उन्मादनिदानं समाप्तम् ॥ २० ॥

Observance of austerities charity, vows, right conduct, rigid discipline, truth, the eight extra-ordinary capabilities*-are the qualities found in the grahas, more in deva grahas and less in others. Hence, they by themselves do not enter the unclean bodies of human beings. Those who say so have no knowledge of demonology. It is the thousands and millions of servants or retinue of the grahas, who are fond of blood and flesh, who are very terrific and who roam about at nights who enter into the human body and take possession of the person. ( 31-33 )

Thus ends the chapter on Unmada.

<sup>*</sup> The eight extra-ordinary capabilities are ( 1 ) Anima ( capacity to assume minute size, ) ( 2 ) Mahima ( capacity to assume very huge size, ) ( 3 ) Garima ( capacity to become very heavy ), ( 4 ) Laghima ( to become very light ), ( 5 ) Prapti ( obtaining any desired thing ), ( 6 ) Prakamya ( fulfiling all desires ), ( 7 ) Isatwa ( overlordship on anything ) and ( 8 ) Vasitwa ( subjugating or controlling anything. )

According to another version ( 1 ) capacity to enter into the body of another person, ( 2 ) extraordinary sense perception, ( 3 ) to understand all sounds or meanings of all words/ language, ( 4 ) capacity to see, ( 5 ) hear and ( 6 ) remember things which are impossible by others, ( 7 ) unusual bright complexion and ( 8 ) capacity to vanish out of sight-are the extraordinary powers.

#### अथापस्मारनिदानम्

#### APASMARA NIDANAM ( EPILEPSY )

#### ( चिन्ताशोकादिभिन्दोषाः कद्धा हृत्व्योतासि स्थिताः । कृत्वा स्मृतेरण्यसम्परं प्रकुर्वे ॥ १ ॥ ) तमःप्रवेशः संरम्भो दोषोद्देकहृतस्मृतेः । अपस्मार इति ज्ञेयो गदो घोरश्चतुर्विधः ।। १ ।।

( The dosas having become increased by worry, grief, etc., get lodged in the channels of the hrdaya (mind), make for loss of memory (knowledge, consciousness) and produce the disease apasmara ). Apasmara is known as the disease which causes feeling of getting into utter darkness ( loss of consciousness ), violent movements of the eyes, limbs ( convulsions ), appearance of other symptoms of increase of dosas and loss of memory. This dreaded disease is of four kinds. ( 1 )

# हृत्कम्पः शून्यता स्वेदो ध्यानं मच्छा प्रमृदता । निद्रानाशश्च तस्मिश्च भविष्यति भवत्यथ ॥ २ ॥ ( सु. उ. अ. ६१ )

Palpitation, feeling of emptiness ( of the mind ), perspiration, worry, fainting, delusion, and loss of sleep are its prodromal symptoms. ( 2 )

# कम्पते प्रदरोहन्तान् फेनोद्धामी श्वसित्यपि । परुषारुणकृष्णानि पद्धेद्रपाणि चानिलात् ॥ ३ ॥ ( सु. उ. अ. ६१ )

Tremors, (convulsions ), grinding of teeth, frothy vomitting, increased respiration, roughness of the body, seeing things of reddish-black colour are the symptoms of vātaja apasmāra. ( 3 )

#### पीतफेनाङ्गवक्राक्षः पीतास्त्रप्रदर्शनः । सनृष्णोष्णानलव्यात्रलोकदशीं च पैसिकः ॥ ४ ॥

( सु. उ. अ. ६१ )

Yellow coloured froth at the mouth, yellowish skin, face and eyes, seeing yellow or red coloured things, thirst, feeling of warmth of the body, the surroundbeing seen as though covered with fire are the symptoms of pittaja ings apasmāra. ( 4 )

शुक्रफेनाक्षकाक्षः शीतहरणूक्षजो गुरुः । पदयेच्छुक्कानि रूपाणि श्लैभिको मूच्यते चिरात् ॥ ५ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. १५ )

Whitish froth at the mouth, white colour of the skin, face and eyes, feeling of cold, horripulations, feeling of heaviness of the sam, face and eyes, feeling of regaining consciousness after long time are the symptoms of kaphaja apasming consisted things

सर्वेरतैः समस्तैःश् लिङ्गेब्रंथस्त्रियस्त्रियस्त्रियः स्टब्स् सर्वे अपूर्णात्तिः । अपरमारः सं चार्सवानवश्यः ॥ ६ ॥ ॥ ६ ॥

It is incurable, if found in a mara. It is incurable if found in a person who is emaciated and which is not of recent onset. ( 6 )

# प्रतिस्फुरन्तं बद्युराः क्षोणं प्रचलितभ्रवम् । नैत्राभ्यां च विकुर्वाणमपस्मारो विनाशयेत् ॥ ७ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. १५ )

Too much of tremors ( convulsions ) emaciation, fierce movement of eyebrows and eyes seen in apasmāra usually kill the patient ( 7 )

# पक्षाद्धाद्वाद्दशाद्दाः मासाद्राः कुपिताः मलाः । अपस्माराय कुर्वन्ति वेगं किंचिद्यशन्तरम् ॥ ८ ॥ देवे वर्षःयपि यथा भूमौ बीजानि अप्रैल-जितन्त्रम् अवस्थान्तं का क्षिवदेशन्तरम् ॥ ३ ॥
देवे वर्षःयपि यथा भूमौ बीजानि कानिन्चित् । शरदि प्रतिरोहन्ति तथा व्याघिसमुच्छ्रयाः ॥ ९ ॥

( मु. उ. अ. ६१ )

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचित्रे माधवनिदानेऽपस्मारनिदानं समाप्तम् ॥ २१ ।।

The dosas get aggravated ( regularly ) on 5, 12 or 30 day intervals and produce episodes of apasmara ( epileptic scizures ), sometimes even apart from these intervals. Just as seeds sprout only during sarat rtu ( autumn ) even though there happens to be rain car only - during "saral" III ( autumn ) even though ) even though period of time. ( 8-9 )

Thus ends the chapter on Apasmara.

#### अथ वालव्याधिनिदानम्

## VĀTA VYADHI NIDĀNAM ( DISEASES OF NERVOUS SYSTEM )

#### रू श्रश्नशीताल्पल्रध्यवायाति प्रजागरैः । विषमादुपचाराच्छ दोषास्त्रकस्रवणाद्दपि ।। १ ॥ लङ्घनप्रवनात्यध्वव्यायामादिविचेच्छितैः । धातूनां संक्षयाचिन्ताशोकरोगातिकर्षणात् ॥ २॥ वेगसंधारणादामाद्भिघातादमोजनान् । मर्माबाधादृजोष्ट्राश्वशीध्यानापतंसनात् ॥ ३ ॥ देहे स्रोतांसि रिक्तानि पूरयित्वाऽनिल्ले बल्ही । करोति विविधान् व्याधीन् सर्वाक्तस्थ्यान् ।। ४ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २८ )
