Excessive indulgence in foods which are dry, cold, scanty, quickly digestible; sexual intercourse, keeping awake at nights, improper treatments, excessive discharge of dosas and blood from the body ( during the course of treatments like emesis, purgation, blood letting, etc. ); jumping, swimming, long distance walking and such other exercises; loss or depletion of body tissues, worry, grief, extreme debility due to protracted diseases, habit of suppression of natural urges of the body, production of ama { undigested intermediary metabolities }, trauma, fasting or diseases of vital organs, riding fast on elephants, starvation, injury or camel, horse or falling during such riding etc., cause the channels ( of vata ) in the body to become empty ( dry ) which later get filled with vata and produce different diseases affecting one or more parts of the body. ( 1-4 )

## अव्यक्तं लक्षणं तेषां पूर्वरूपमिति स्मृतम् । आत्मरूपं तु यद्भ्यक्तमपायो ल्धुता पुनः ।। ५ ।। ( च. चि. अ. २८ )

Its premonitory stage is characterised by appearance of the symptoms of the disease in milder form, the same symptoms becoming well marked and severe denotes the stage of manifestation; absence of characteristic symptoms and lightness of the body { during the course of specific diseases to be described further on ) are the signs of cure of such diseases. ( 5 )

संकोचः पर्वणां स्तम्भो भक्तोऽस्थ्रां पर्वणामपि । रोमहर्षः प्रत्नापश्च पाणिप्रष्ठशिरोयद्वः ॥ ६ ॥ खान्न्यपाङ्गुल्यकुब्जत्वं शोषोऽङ्गानामनिद्र्ता । गर्भश्रक्षरजोनाशः स्पन्दनं गात्रसुप्तत् ॥ ७ ॥ श्चित्रासाक्षिजन्नूणां ग्रीवायाश्चापि हुण्डनम् । भेदस्तोदोऽतिराक्षेपो मुद्दुश्चायास पच च ॥ ८ ॥ पवंविधानि कपाणि करोति कुपितोऽनिलः । हेतुस्थानविशेषाध्य भवेद्रोगविशेषक्त् ॥ ९ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २८ )

Contractures of body parts, rigidity of joints, fractures or breaking pain in the bones and joints, horripulations, delerium, stiffness of the joints of hands, back and head, lameness of one or both legs, curvature of the back, wasting of the body parts, loss of sleep, loss or destruction of foetus, semen and ovum; tremors, loss of sensation, curvaturs of head, nose, eyes, shoulders and neck; cutting or other types of pain in the body parts, frequent convulsions and debility. These and similar kinds of symptoms are produced by incresaed vata, corresponding to the causes and sites of affection. ( 6-9 )

# तत्र कोष्ठाश्रिते दुष्टे निग्रहो मूत्रवर्चसोः । ब्रध्नहृद्योगगुरुमार्शः पाश्वशूलं च मारुते ॥ १० ॥

If vata is increased in the kostha ( alimentary tract ) there will be obstruction to passage of urine and feces, enlargement of the scrotum, heart diseases, tumors of the abdomen, heamorrhoids and pain in the flanks. ( 10 )

# सर्वाक्कुपिते वाते गात्रस्फुरणभञ्जनम् । वेदनामिः परीताश्च स्फुटन्तीवास्य सन्धयः ।। ११ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २८ )

If vata gets increased all over the body, there will be tremors ( throbbings, twitchings, pulsations, etc. ) cutting and other types of pain, feeling of pain in the joints as though bursting out, etc. ( 11 )

# ग्रहो विषमूत्रवातानां शूल्राध्मानाद्यमशः । जङ्घोर्शत्रिकपात्पृष्ठरोगशोधौ गुद्दे स्थिते । १२ ॥

It vāta gets increased and occupies the region of the gudā (rectum), there will be stoppage of movement of urine, feces and flatus; pain and distention of the abdomen, formation of urinary caliculus and gravel, pain in the calves, thigs, pelvis, legs and back and emaciation. ( 12 )

# रुक् पाश्चोंदरहन्नामेस्तृष्णोद्गारविसूचिकाः । कासः काण्ठास्यशोषध्य श्वासश्चामाशयस्थिते ।। १३ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २५ )

If increased vata resides in the amasaya ( stomach ) pain in the flanks, abdomen, region of the heart and umbilicus, thirst, erectations, vomitting and diarrhoea, cough, dryness of throat and mouth and dyspnoca are found. ( 13 )

# पकाशयस्थोऽन्त्रक्रूजं शूलाटोपौ करोति च । क्रञ्छ्रमूत्रपुरीषंत्वमानाहं त्रिकवेदनाम् ॥ १४ ॥

If it gets lodged in the pakwasaya ( large intestine and rectum ) there will be intestinal gurglings and pain, difficulty for defecation and micturition, distention of abdomen and pain in the sacral region. ( 14 )

# श्रोत्रादिष्विन्द्रियवधं कुर्यादृदुष्टसमीरणः । ( च. चि. अ. २८ )

If vata gets increased in the indriyas ( sense organs ) it produces loss of function of those organs.

# त्वग्रक्षा स्फुटिता सुप्ता कृशा कृष्णा च तुद्यते । आतन्यते सरागा च पर्वरुक् व्वग्गतेऽनिल्डे ॥ १५ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २८ )

If it is located in the twak ( skin ), it produces dryness, cracks, loss of sensation, thinning, blackish discolouration, pricking pain, stretching, redness and pain in the joints. ( 15 )

# रुजस्तीव्यः ससन्तापा वैवर्ण्यं कृशताऽरुचिः । गात्रे चारूंषि मुक्तस्य स्तम्भ्यासुगातेऽनिले ।। १६ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २८ )

If vata gets located in asrk ( blood ), there will be severe pain, increased feeling of heat, discolouration, eniaciation, loss of taste, appearance of ulcers or boils on the skin and stiffness of the body after meals. ( 16 )

# गुर्वहः तुद्यतेऽत्यर्थं दण्डमुष्टिहतं यथा । सरुक् श्रमितमत्वर्थं मांसमेदोगतेऽनिले ।। १७ ॥

If vāta gets lodged in māņsa-medas ( muscles and fat ) it produces feeling of heaviness of the body parts associated with severe pain as though beaten by a stick or fist and extreme exhaustion. ( 17 )

# भेदोऽस्थिपर्वणां सन्धिशुलं मांसबलक्षयः । अस्वप्न: संतता रुक् च मज्जास्थिकुपितेऽनिल्छे ।। १८ ।। ( च. वि. अ. २८ )

If located in the majja-asthi ( marrow and bone ) vata causes splitting pain in the bones and joints, loss of strength of muscles, loss of sleep and constant pain of the body. ( 18 )

# सिप्रं मूझति वध्याति शुक्रं गर्भमधापि वा । विकृतिं जनयेच्यापि शुक्रस्थः कुपितोऽनिल्डः ॥ १९ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २८ )

If vata affects the sukra ( reproductive elements ) there will be either premature or delayed expulsion of semen and foctus or production of abnormalities of embrivo. ( 19 )

> कुर्यात्सरागतः शूल्रं सिराकुञ्चनपूरणम् । ( -- . नि. अ. १ ) स बाह्याम्यन्तरायामं खलीं कौब्ज्यमथापि वा ॥ २० ॥ सर्वाङ्गकाद्गरोगांध्य कुर्यात्म्रा पुगतोऽनिलः । ( च. चि. अ. २८ ) इन्नि सन्धिगतः सन्धीनू शुल्यान् याँ करोति च ॥ २१ ॥

If located in the siras ( veins and arteries ) it causes pain, contractures or shortening and engorgement ( with accumulation of blood ). If vata gets localised in the snayu ( tendons/nerves ) there will be bowlike bending of the body inwards or outwards, contractures of the fingers, shortening of the body by curvature of the back or legs and other diseases of either the whole or any part of the body. If localised in the joints, it causes loss of function, pain and cracking sound in them. { 20-21 }

( प्राणोदानी समानश्च व्यानश्चापान एव च । स्थानस्था मारुताः पञ्च यापयन्ति शरीरिणम् ॥ ) प्राणे पित्तान्नुते छदिर्दाहश्चैवोपजायते । दौर्वल्यं सदनं तन्द्रा वैरस्यं च कफावृते ॥ २२ ॥ उद्दाने पित्तयुक्ते त् दाहो मूच्छा भ्रमः क्रमः । अस्वेदहर्षौ मन्दोऽग्निः शीतता च क्रफायते ॥ २३ ॥ स्वेददाहौण्यमूच्छाः स्युः समानै पित्तसंवृते । कफेन सक्ते विष्णमूत्रे गात्रहर्षश्च ज्ञायते ॥ २४॥ अपाने पित्तयुक्त तु दाहौदण्यं रक्तमुन्नता । अधःकाये गुरुत्वं च शोतता च कफाव्रते ।। २५ ।। ( म. नि. अ. १ ) श्यानै पिनावुते दाहो गात्रविश्लेषणं क्लमः । स्तम्भनो दण्डकश्चापि शुल्ट्रशोधौ कफावृत्ते ॥ २६ ॥ यदा तु धमनीः सर्वाः कुपितोऽभ्येति मारुतः । तदाऽऽक्षिपत्याश्यु मुदुर्मुहुद्देहर्दं मुद्दश्यरः ॥ २७ ॥ मुद्दमुद्रश्रासिपणादाक्षिपक इति स्मृतः ।

Prāņa, udānā samāna, vyāna and apāna these five vātas in their normal state sustain the body in good health, )

If prāņavāta gets āvaraņa ( encompassed, surrounded, interfered, obstructed ) by pitta, there will be vomitting and burning sensation; if hy kapha there will be debility, loss of strength, stupor and bad taste in the mouth.

Udanavāta when encompassed by pitta produces burning sensation, fainting, giddiness and exhaustion; if by kapha it causes loss of perspiration, loss of enthusiasm, poor digestive ability and feeling of cold.

Samana vata when encompassed by pitta gives rise to perspiration, burning sensation, increased temperature and fainting; if by kapha it causes obstruction to elimination of urine and feces and horripulations in the body.

Apana vata if encompassed by pitta causes burning sensation, increase of temperature, elimination of urine mixed with blood and if by kapha, heaviness of the lower limbs and feeling of cold.

Vyana vāta encompassed by pitta gives rise to burning sensation, abnormal movements of the body parts and exhaustion; if by kapha, stiffness of the body like a log, pain and swelling.

When the increased vata gets localised in all the dhamanis ( blood vessels )' of the body, it produces quick, frequent and jerky movements of the body which are called Aksepaka ( convulsions ). ( 22-27 )

कुद्धः स्वैः कोपनैर्वायुः स्थानादूध्यं प्रवर्तते ॥ २८ ॥ ( न. नि. ज. १ )

82

<sup>1.</sup> This should be snayus ( tendons or nerves ) and not dhamanis ( vessels ) to be more appropriate.

पीडयन् हृदयं गत्वा शिरः शङ्को च पीडयन् । धनुर्वन्नमयेद्वान्त्राण्याक्षिपेन्मोहयेन्तदा ॥ २९ ॥ स कृञ्छादुच्छसेन्धापि स्तब्धाक्षोऽथ निर्मालकः । कपोत इव कूजेम्स निःसंक्षः सोऽपतन्त्रकः ॥ ३० ॥ दृष्टि संस्त्र्य संक्षां च हत्वा करण्डेन कुजति । हृदि मुक्ते नरः स्वास्थ्यं याति मोहं वृते पुनः ॥ ३१ ॥ वायुना दारुणं प्राहरेके तद्रपतानकम् । कफान्वितो भूशं यायुस्तास्वेव यदि तिष्ठति ॥ ३२॥ ( वा. नि. अ. १५ ) दण्डवत्स्तम्भयेद्देहं स तु दृण्डापतानकः ।

Vata undergoing increase by causes akin to its nature, moves upwards and causes pain in the hrdaya ( heart ), head and temples; makes the body to bend like a bow; associated with convulsions and loss of consciousness. The patient will be having difficulty in breathing, eyes wide open without movement of lids or eyes completely closed, makes sound in the throat like a pigeon. This condition is known as Apatantraka; a similar condition known as Apatānaka is described by other scholars, in which the patient will be having fixed gaze in the eyes, loss of consciousness, cooing sound in the throat, intermittent relief of symptoms when the heart gets freed from vata but again getting back to unconsciousness.

If in apatānaka, vāta gets associated with kapha, the body of the patient will be stiff like a log of wood; when it is known as Dandapatanaka. ( 28-32 )

# धनुस्तुल्यं नमेद्यस्त्र स धनुःस्तम्भसंस्क्रकः ।। ३३ ।। अङ्गलीगुल्फजठरहक्रक्षोगलसंधितः । स्नायुप्रतानमनिलो यदाऽऽस्सिपति वेगवान् ॥ ३४ ॥ विष्टब्धाक्षः स्त्रब्धहृनुमंन्नराज्यंः कफं वमन् । अभ्यन्तरं धनुरिव्य यदा नर्मात मानवम् ॥ ३५ ॥ तदाऽस्याभ्यन्तरायामं कुरुते मारुतो यल्ली । वाह्यस्नायुप्रतानस्थो बाह्यायामं करोति च ॥ ३६ ॥ तमसाध्यं दुधाः प्राहुर्वक्षःकटव्यूरुभञ्जनम् । ( गृ. नि. अ. १ )

Vata making the body hent like a bow is called as Dhanuhstambha; when the powerful vāta affects the net-work of snāyus ( tendons, nerves ) located in the toes, heels, abdomen, region of the heart, chest and throat, causing severe convulsive movements, the patient develops fixed gaze, stiffness of the lower jaw indrawing or collapse of the flanks, vomitting of mucus and bending of the body inwards like a bow—is known as Abhyanthrāyāma (dhanuḥstambha); when the vāta affects the tendons/nerves of the external part of the body, the patient gets an outward bending like a bow, associated with dystrophy of chest, waist and thighs. This condition known as Bāhyāyāma ( dhanuhstambha ), is in the opinion of scholars incurable. ( 33-36 )

कफपित्तान्वितो वायुर्वायुरेव च केवलः ॥ ३७ ॥ कुर्यादास्त्रेपकं त्वन्यं चतुर्थमभिघातजम् । गर्मपातनिमित्तश्च शोणितातिस्रवाच्य यः ॥ ३८ ॥ अभिघातनिमित्तध्य न सिद्ध्यत्यपतानकः । ( मु. नि. अ. १ )

Vata associated with kapha and pitta or even alone produces a fourth kind of aksepaka due to trauma to the parts of the body.

Apatānaka caused by abortions, excessive haemorrhage and trauma are incurable. ( 37-38 )

# गृहीत्वाऽर्थे तनोर्वायुः सिराः स्नायूर्विशोध्य च ॥ ३९ ॥ ( मृ. नि. त. १ ) पक्षमन्यतरं द्वन्ति सन्धिबन्धान्विमोश्लयन् । कृत्को पर्धकायस्तस्य स्यादकमण्यो विचेतनः ॥ ४० ॥ पक्राक्वरोगं तं केचिदन्ये पक्षकर्यं विदुः । सर्वाक्ररोगस्तद्रच सर्वकायाश्रितेऽनिले ॥४१॥(त्रा. वि. १५)

When vita affects the siras and snayus of any half ( right or left ) or the body, causing loosening of its joints, loss of control, inability of functioning and loss of sensation in the limbs-it is called by some as Ekaiga roga and by others as paksavadha. When the entire body ( both halves ) is thus affected it is known as Sarvāṅgavāta ( 39-41 )

# दाहसन्तापमूर्चर्छाः स्युर्वायौ पित्तसमन्वते । शैत्यशोधगुरुत्वानि तस्मिन्नेत्र कफान्विते ॥ ४२ ॥ शुद्धवातद्वतं पक्षं कच्छूसाध्यतमं विदुः । साध्यमन्येन संगुक्तमसाध्यं क्षयहेतुकम् ।।४३।। (मू. १. १)

If in the above diseases, vata is associated with pitta, there will be burning sensation, increase of temperature of the affected parts and fainting; if associated with kapha, cold, swelling and heaviness of the affected parts manifest.

Paksavadha caused by väta alone is difficult to cure, that caused by vata associated with other dosas is casy to cure and that which leads to emaciation or wasting is impossible to cure. ( 42-43 )

( गर्भिणी सूर्तिकाबाल्न्न्द्धसीणेष्वस्त्रुते । पक्षाघानं परिहरेत् वेदना रहितो यदि ॥ ) ( उति ख. )

( Paksäghata seen in pregnant women, women iccently delivered children, old people, emaciated persons and that caused by heavy bleeding and that in which the patient has no sensation at all in the affected limbs should be refused treatmet. )

# उच्चैव्याहरतोऽत्यर्थं खाद्तः कठिनानि वा । हसतो ज़म्भतो वाऽपि भारतद्विष्मशायिनः ॥ ४४ ॥ शिरोनासौष्ठचित्रुकललाटेक्षणस्त्रियाः । अर्द्यत्यत्यनिलो वक्रमर्दितं जनयत्यतः । वक्तीभवति वऋषधं श्रीवा चाप्यपवर्तते ।। ४५ ।।

# शिरश्चलति वाक्सङ्गो नेत्रादीनां च वैकृतम् । योजाविद्युकदन्तानां तस्मिन्याश्वं च वेदना ॥ ४६ ॥

Vata functioning in the region of the head, nose, lips, chin, forehead and eyes, gets affected by acts like speaking in high pitch for long periods of time, biting hard materials, indulging in excessive laughing, yawning; carrying heavy loads on the head, uneven or irregular sleeping posture, ctc., produces the disease called Ardita. The person will have irregularity or asymmetry of the face, the angles of the mouth droop, the neck is turned outwards, shaking of the head,

inability to talk, abnormalities of movement of the eyes, neck, chin and jaws associated with pain. ( 44-46 )

# ( यस्याग्रजो रोमहर्षों वेपथुर्नेत्रमाविलम् । वायुरूधर्वे त्वचि स्वापस्तोदो मन्याह्युग्रहः ।। ) तमदिंतमिति प्राहुर्थाधि व्याधिविचक्षणाः । क्षीणस्यानिभिषाक्षस्य प्रसक्तान्यक्तमाषिणः ॥ ४७ ॥ न सिध्यत्यादितं गाढं धिवर्ष वेपनस्य च । ( म. नि. अ. १ )

( Horripulations, tremors, accumulation of dirt in the eyes, loss of sensation of the skin, pricking pain, stiffness of the neck and lower jaw are the prodromal symptoms of ardita. ) Expert clinicians call this disease as Ardita.

Ardita in emaciated persons, who do not wink their eves, whose speech is slurred and incoherent and in whom it is persisting for more than three years is incurable. ( 47 )

# मते वेग भवेत् स्वास्थ्यं सर्वेष्वाक्षेपकादिषु ॥ ४८ ॥ ( ११. नि. ज. १५ )

In all types of aksepakas ( convulsive disorders ) the patient gets periods of health ( normalcy ) when the episodes of attack pass off. ( 48 )

# जिन्नानित्रंजनाच्छुकमक्षण:दृभिघाततः । कुपितो हनुमूलस्थः स्त्रं संयित्वाऽनिल्लो इतुम् ॥ ४९ ॥ करोति वित्रतास्यत्वमथवा संव्रतास्यताम ।

## हनुप्रहः स तेन स्यात्कृद्ध्युवेद्यवधाषापणम् ॥ ५० ॥ ( वा. नि. अ. १५ )

Indulgence in putting out the tongue too much for long periods, eating dry foods and injury makes for increase of vata located near the neck; which in turn, loosens the joints of the lower jaw, making the mouth either open or closed tight all the time, causing difficulty in chewing and speaking. This disease is known as Hanugraha. ( 49-50 )

# दिवास्वप्तासमस्थानवित्रनोध्वनिरीक्षणैः । मन्यास्तम्भं प्रकुरुते स एव श्लेष्मणाऽऽवृतः ॥ ५१ ॥ ( मृ. नि. अ. १ )

Vata getting increased either alone or in association with kapha by sleeping at day time, irregular postures, looking in upward direction for long periods causes Manyastambha ( stiffness of the neck. ) { 51 }

## वाग्वाहिनीसिरासंस्थो जिह्वां स्तम्भयतेऽनिलः । जिह्वास्तम्भः स तेनान्नपानवाक्येष्वनीशता ॥ ५२ ॥ ( वा. नि. अ. १५ )

Likewise vata produces Jihwastambha ( loss of movement of the tongue ) affecting the tendons/nerve near the voice box and cause inability for chewing, Srinking and talking, ( 52 )

> रक्तमाधित्य पवनः कुर्यान्मूर्धधराः सिराः। रूक्षाः सर्वेदनाः कृष्णाः सोऽसाध्यः स्यात्सिराय्रहः ॥ ५३ ॥ ( वा. नि. अ. १५ )

Vata increased, getting localised in the blood and blood vessels of the head causes them to shrink, become painful and black. This condition known as Siragraha ( or sirograha ) is difficult to cure. ( 53 )

# स्फिक्पूर्वा कटिपृष्ठोरुजानुजङ्घापदं कमात् । गृध्यसी स्तम्भरुकोदैर्णूहाति स्पन्दते मुद्दः ॥ ५४ ॥ वाताद्वातकफास्तन्द्रागौरवारोचकान्विता । ( च. चि. अ. २६ )

# ( वातजायां भवेत्तोदो देहरुयापि प्रवक्रता । जानुकद्यूरुसंधीनां स्पुरणं स्तब्धता भूशम् ॥ ५५ ॥ वातश्लेष्मोद्भवायां तु निमित्तं वहिमार्द्वम् । तन्द्रा मुखप्रसेकश्च भक्तद्वेषस्तथैव च ।। ५६ ॥ ]

Vata either alone or associated with kapha produces ( a discase called ) Grdhrasi characterised by stiffness (inability to move) and severe pain starting in the lumbar region moving downwards through thighs, knees, calves and feet. Rigidity and throbbings are also experienced intermittently if due only to vata and if due to association with kapha, stupor, heaviness and anorexia are experienced. ( Pricking pain, irregular shape of the body parts, throbbings and severe stiffness of joints of knee and waist are profound if vata is affected alone in grdhrasi but if with kapha, there will be poor digestive ability, suupor, salivation and aversion to food are profound. ) ( 54-56 )

# तलं प्रत्यद्भुलीनां याः कण्डरा बाहुपृष्ठतः ॥ ५७ ॥ बाह्वोः कर्मक्षयकरी विश्वाची चेति सोच्यते । ( मु. नि. अ. १ )

Visvaci is affection of the tendons/nerves of the fingers, the fingers, the arms and the shoulders, causing loss of function of the arm. ( 57 )

# वातशोणितजः शोधो जानुमध्ये महारुजः ॥ ५८ ॥ ज्ञेयः कोष्टुकशीर्षस्तु स्थूलः क्रोष्टुकशीर्षंवत् । ( गु. नि. अ. १ )

Vata and rakta getting affected together produces a very painful swelling of the knee joint resembling the head of a big fox. Hence known as Krostuka-sirga. (58)

# वायुः कट्याश्रितः संक्रध्नः कण्डरामाश्रिपेद्यदा ।। ५९ ॥ खञ्जस्तदा भवेजन्मुः पड्डः सक्थ्नोद्वेयोर्वधात् । ( मु. नि. अ. १ )

Vata affecting the tendons/nerves of the waist makes a man lame; known as Khañja if one leg is affected and Pangu if both legs are affected. ( 59 )

# प्रकामन् वेपते यस्तु खज्जन्मिव च गच्छति ।। ६० ।। कलायखखं तं विद्यान्मुक्तसन्धिप्रवन्धनम् । ( गु. नि. अ. १ )

Presence of pain at the commencement of walking, limping later on and Iooseness of the joints of the legs are the features of Kalāyakhaīja ( lametes caused by ingestion of pea, lentil or masūra dāla. ) ( 60 )

# रुक पादे विषमन्यस्ते श्रमाद्वा जायते यदा ।। ६१ 11 वातेन गुल्फमाश्रित्य तमाहुर्वातकण्टकम् । ( वा. नि. अ. १५ )

Improper placing of the feet on the ground while walking or exhaustion due to exertion of walking makes for localisation of vata in the region of the heels, causing severe pain. This condition is known as Vatakantaka. ( 61 )

# पादयोः करुत दाहं पित्तासुकसहितोऽनिलः ॥ ६२ ॥ विशेषत्यक्रमतः पाद्दाहं तमादिशोत् । ( मृ. नि. अ. १ )

Vata getting increased, associated with kapha and rakta ( blood ) causes burning sensation in the soles of the feet in persons who indulge in excess walk. This condition is called Padadaha. ( 72 )

# हृष्यते चरणौ यस्य मवेनां चापि सुप्रको ।। ६३ ।। पादृहपः स्व विज्ञयः कफवातप्रकोपतः । ( मृ. नि. अ. १ )

Increase of vata along with kapha produces Pädaharṣa which is characterised with a feeling of pins and needless or fecling of loss of sensation in the feet. ( 63 )

```
अंसदेशस्थितो वायुः  शोषयेदस्सवन्ध्रनम् ॥ ६४ ॥
सिराश्चाकुञ्चय तत्रस्थो जनयेद्वबाहुकम् । ( मू. नि. अ. ? )
```
Vata located in the region of the shoulder causes emaciation ( of muscles ) of the shoulder joint. ( This is known as Amsa sosa. ) Apabähuka is another condition due to contraction of the tendons/nerves of that region. ( 64 )

# आवूत्य वायुः संकफो धमनीः शब्दवाहिनीः ॥ ६५ ॥ नरान्करोत्यकियकान्मूकमिन्मिनगद्गद्गद् । ( मृ. नि. अ. १ )

Vata getting increased along with kapha invades the dhamanis ( vessels ) of the voice box and makes the person ineffective in talking ( mute ) or talk through his nose ( nasal speech ) or cause stammering. ( 65 )

# अधो या वेदना याति वर्चोमूत्राशयोत्थिता ॥ ६६ ॥ भिन्द्र्ताव गुद्रोपस्थं सा तूनी नाम नामतः । गुदोपस्थोत्थिता या नु प्रतिल्होमं प्रधाविता ॥ ६० ॥ वेगै: पक्काशयं याति प्रतितूनीति सोच्यते । ( मु. नि. अ. १ )

Tuni is that disease in which severe cutting pain is experienced in the rectum, urinary bladder and the penis, pain moving in downward direction. If the pain has an upward movement reaching the large intestines it will be called as Pratitūni ( 66-67 )

#### साठोपमर्त्युः व्यव्यव्यवस्तु द्वद्यं मुश्राम् ।। देद 11 आध्मानमिति तं विद्याद्धोरं वातनिरोधजम् ।

#### विमुक्तपार्श्वेद्वदयं तदेवामाशयोत्थितम् ॥ ६९ ॥ प्रत्याध्मानं विजानीयात्कफब्याकुल्त्रितानिल्लम् । ( मु. नि. अ. १ )

Obstruction to the movement of the flatus gives rise to the condition called Adhmana, characterised by gurgling noise in the intestines, severe pain and vistention of the abdomen; vata associated with kapha causing such a condition in the region of the stomach but away from the region of the heart and the flanks-is known as Pratyādhmāna. ( 68-69 )

```
नाभेरधस्तात्संज्ञातः संचारी याद वाऽचलः ॥ ७० ॥
अष्ठीलावद्धनो ग्रन्थिरूर्वमायत उन्नतः ।
वाताप्ठीलां विज्ञानीयाद्रहिर्मार्गावरोधिनीम् ॥ ७१ ॥
पतामेव रुजोपेतां वातविण्मूत्ररोधिनीम् ।
प्रत्यष्ठीलामिति वदेज्ज्ञाटरे तिर्यंगुत्थिताम् ॥ ७२ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १ )
```
A tumor, below the umbilicus moving or not moving, hard like a ironsmith's stone, projecting upwarads and causing obstructions to passages of feces and urine is to be understood as Vātașthīlā The same found associated with pain, obstruction to flatus, feces and urine and lying obliquely in the abdomen is called Pratyasţhīlā. ( 70-72 )

# मारुतेऽनुगुणे बस्तौ मूत्रं सम्यक् प्रवर्तते । विकारा विविधाश्यात्र प्रतिलोमे भवन्ति च ॥ ७३ ॥ ( मृ. नि. अ. १ )

Vata having its normal downward movement ( anuloma ) makes for proper elemination of urine from the urinary bladder, but causes many a disorder if it becomes pratiloma ( movement in upward direction. ) ( 73 )

```
सर्वाङ्गकम्पः शिरस्सो वाग्रुवंपथुसंक्षकः ।
खल्ली तु पादजङ्घोरुकरमूल्ठावमोटनी ॥ ७४ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २८ )
```
Vepathu is that condition caused by vāta in which all the parts of the body including the head will have tremors.

Khalli is that condition in which there will be distortions of the legs, calves, thighs, chest and arms. ( 74 )

# ( अधः प्रतिहतो वायुः श्लेष्मणा मारुतेन वा । करोत्युद्रारबाहुल्यमूर्ध्ववातः स उच्यते ।। ७५ ।। )

Vata getting obstructed in its downward movement either by kapha or by itself, begins to move upwards and produces too many erectations. This condition is known as Ürdhvavāta. ( 75 )

स्थाननामानुबन्धः श्री शिवान्विनिर्दिशेत् । सर्वेष्वेतेषु संसर्गं पित्तादैरुपलक्षयेत् ॥ ७६ ॥

Symptoms of the remaining kinds of (vātavyādhis) are to be diagnosed in respect of their site of affection and name, so also the association of vāta with pitta and other dosas. ( 76 )

#### हनुस्तम्भादिंताक्षेप पक्षाघातापतानकाः । ( च. चि. अ. २८ ) कालेन महता वाता यत्नात्सिध्यन्ति वा नवा ।। ७७ ।। नरान् बल्धवतस्त्वेतान् साधयेन्निरुपद्रवान् ।

Hanustambha, ardita, āksēpa, paksāghāta, apatānaka become severe and difficult to cure or even incurable with lapse of time. Treatment of these should be attempted for patients who are physically strong and in whom there are no complications. ( 77-78 )

# विसर्पद्राहरुक्तसङ्गमूच्छारचयग्निमार्द्वैः ॥ ७८ ॥ क्षीणमांसबलं वाता घन्ति पक्षवधाद्यः ।

Vata disorders such as paksawadha are going to be fatal to patients who develop visarpa ( erysepelas ), burning sensation, severe pain, non-elimination of flatus, feces, urine, etc., fainting, anorexia, poor degestive capacity, wasting of muscles and loss of strength. ( 78 )

# शूनं सुत्तत्वचं भग्नं कम्पाध्माननिपीडितम् । रुजातिमन्तं च नरं वातव्याधिर्विनाशयेत् ॥ ७९ ॥ ( मु. मू. अ. ३३ )

Swelling of the body ( or its parts ), loss of tactile sensation, fractures of bones, tremors, flatulance or severe pain found in vatavyadhis are also to be considered as fatal signs. ( 79 )

> अव्याहृतगतिर्यस्य स्थानस्थः प्रकृतिस्थितः । वायुः स्यात्स्योऽधिकं जीवेद्वीतरोगः समाः शतम् ॥ ८० ॥

इति श्रीमाध्यकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने वातव्याधिनिदानं समाप्तम् ॥ २२ ॥

Vata, in its normal state, whose movements are not changed, remaining in its normal locations, helps mass - novements are not changed, remaining
 any disease. ( 80) man to live a hundred years without any disease. ( 80 )

Thus ends the chapter on Vātavyādhi.

# अथ वातरक्तनिदानम्

VĀTA RAKTA NIDĀNAM ( GOUT )

ल्डवणार्म्लकदुस्रारस्निग्धोष्णाजीणेजनैः । क्लिन्नगुष्काम्बुजाम्बुजाम्बुजामून्नकैः ।। १ ॥ ्त्रवनाः स्टब्स् सर्वे अपि । दृश्यारनालसौवीरशुक्ततकसुरासवैः ॥ २ ॥ विरुद्धाः यशनकोधदिवास्वभ्रप्रजागरैः । ( च. चि. अ. २९ ) प्रायशः सुकुमाराणां स्थलानां सुखिनां चापि कुण्यते घातशोणितम् ॥ ३ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. १ ) हृस्यश्वोष्टैर्गंच्छन्नतश्च विदाह्यन्नं स विदाहोऽशनस्य । कृत्त्रं रक्तं विदृद्दत्याशु तद्य स्नस्तं दुष्टं पादयोश्चीयते तु । तत्संपृक्तं वायुना दूषितेन तत्पाबल्यादुच्यते वातरक्तम् ॥ ४ ॥ ( मु. नि. ५. १ )

Over-indulgence in foods which are salty, sour, pungent, alkaline, faity, very hot or uncooked; flesh of animals or birds of marshy or desert regions which have been soaked for long time in water, or those which have no moisture at all; eatta bles having sesamum, raddish, horsegram, blackgram, cow-pea, leafy vegetables, Dics having sugarcane juice, curds, fcrmented drinks like aranāla, sauvīraka, sukta, takra, takra, takra, takra, takra, taksa, takra, taksa, taksa, taksa, taksa, taksa, taksa mations sugaround jarvey caras ( all such fermented liquids ), foods ), foods prepared ( termented battermine ), caraj assimmore quantity of foods; anger, sleeping during by inixing awake at night and such other unhealthy foods and activities will day seeping a disease called Vatarakta; specially in those who are very tender ( not produce a arease canced cannounce of having soft and tender organs or body parts ), who are very obese and who are happy always ( not experinced with hardship or misery of any kind ); riding on elephant, horse, camel, etc., for long durations of time, indulging in foods which cause burning sensation inside during or after digestion, etc., makes for overcooking of rakta ( iypermetobolic or catabolic activity in the blood ). This improperly processed bad blood associated both increase of vata doşa, collects in the legs. This disease is called as Vatarakta because vāta dosa is the predominant one in this diseasc. ( 1-4 )

> स्वेदोऽत्यर्थं न वा काष्ण्यं स्पर्श्णयं स्पर्शाव्यं क्षतेऽतिषक् । सन्धिश्वश्यमालस्यं सद्देनं पिडकोद्भमः ॥ ५ ॥ जानुजङ्कोरुक्तद्यंसहस्तपादाङ्गसन्धिषु । निस्तोदः स्फुरणं मेदो गुरुत्वं सुप्तिरेव च ।। ६ ।।

# कण्डः सन्धिष्ठ रुग्भूत्वा भूत्वा नद्याति चासकृत् । वैवण्यं मण्डलोत्पत्तिर्वातासूकपूर्वलक्षणम् ॥ ७ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २२ )

Excessive perspiration or absence of it, black discolouration, loss of tactile sensation, severe pain even on slight injury, looseness of joints, lethargy, debility, appearance of trophies in joints such as knee, ankle, hip, shoulder, fingers and toes, severe pain such as knawing, pulsating, cutting, heavy, troublesome or itching; pain is intermittent; pigmentation and appearance of circular rashes are the prodromal symptoms of Vatarakta. ( 5-7 )

# वातेऽधिकेऽधिकं तत्र शुल्फुरणभञ्जनम् । शोधस्य रौद्यं कृष्णत्वं श्यावतावृद्धिहानयः ।। ८ ।। धमन्यद्भलिसन्धीनां संकोचोऽङ्गग्रहोऽतिरुक्त । शीतद्वेषानुपशयौ स्त्रम्भवेषथुस्तुम् ॥ ९ ॥

If vata is very predominant, pain such as pulsating, tearing/cutting will be very severe, swelling of joints will appear dry, black or blue, increasing or decreasing often, contractures of the fingers, stiffness and severe pain, dislike for cold. symptoms increasing by cold things, stifficss, tremors and loss of sensation, etc., in other parts of the body are seen. ( 8-9 )

# रक्ते शोधोऽतिहकोदस्तान्न्ति श्रिमिचिमायते । स्निग्धरूक्षैः शर्मं नैति कण्डूक्केद्समम्बितः ॥ १० ॥

If rakta is affected profoundly, the swelling will be having severe pain, pricking or a feeling of pains and needles, coppery red in colour, no relief either with oil or dry massage, itching, presence of fluid in the joints ( exudation ) are noticed. ( 10 )

# पित्ते विदाहः संमोद्दः स्वेदो मच्छा मदःसतृद्ध । स्पर्शास्त्रद्वं रुग्रागः शोथः पाको भृशोध्मता ॥ ११ ॥ ( च. च्रि. अ. २९ )

If pitta is predominant, burning sensation in the body, delusion, perspiration. fainting, toxicity, thirst, the joints will have tenderness, pain, red colour, swelling, formation of pus inside and will be very hot to touch. ( 11 )

# कफे स्त्रैमित्यगुरुतास्वशिक्षिण्धरवशीतताः । कण्डूर्मन्दा च रुग्द्वन्द्वं सर्वलिङ्गं च संकरात् ॥ १२ ॥ ( स्. नि. व. १ )

If kapha is predomicant, inactivity, heaviness, loss of sensation, greasiness of the skin, cold feeling all over the body, joints will have mild itching and pain, If two or three dosas are involved there will be combination of symptoms. ( 12 )

पादयोर्मूलमास्थाय कदान्विद्धस्तयोरपि । आखोविंषमिव कुद्धं तद्देहमुपसर्पति ॥ १३॥ ( सु. नि. अ. १ ) The pain usually starts from the legs, sometimes even from the hands and spreads to other parts of the body similar to the spreading of poison of raines aine ( in other words, slowly. ) ( 13 )

आजानु स्कुटितं यच्च प्रभिन्ने प्रस्नतं च यत् । उपद्रवैध्य यज्ञाूं प्राणमांसक्ष्यादिभिः ॥ १४ ॥ वातरक्तमस्ताध्यं स्याद्याप्यं संवत्सरोत्थितम् । ( म. नि. अ. १ ) अस्वप्नारोचकश्चासमांसकोथशिरोग्रहाः ॥ १५ ॥ संम्ब्लीमद्रक्तृतृष्णाज्वरमोहन्नवेषकाः । हिक्कापाङ्कुल्यवीसर्पंपाकतोदश्रमक्कमाः ।। १६ ॥ अड्गूल्डीवक्रतास्फोटदाहमर्ममंत्रहावुद्गः । एतेरुपद्रवैदर्यं मोहेनैकेन वाऽपि यत् ।। १७ ।। अक्तूबरेपद्रवं याप्यं साध्यं स्वान्त्रिरुपद्वम् । एकदोषानुगं साध्यं नवं याप्यं द्विदोषजम् । त्रिदोषजमसाध्यं स्याद्यस्य च स्यूरुपद्रवाः ।। १८ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २८ )

इति थीमाधवकरविरचिते माध्यवनिदाने वातरस्तिदानं समाप्तम् ॥ २३ ।।

All the joints upto the knee getting affected, skin over the joints cracked and exudations coming out, presence of other complications (( enumerated further on ), muscular wasting and loss of chief functions of life found in a patient of vatarakta is impossible to cure. If the disease is not more than one year of duration it persists for the rest of the life and requires constant treatment.

Loss of sleep, anorexia, increased/difficult respirations, putrefaction of muscles, headache, faintings, toxicity, pain, thirst, fever, delusion, rigors, hiccough, lameness ( inability to walk ), erysepelas ( or other skin diseases ), formation of ulcers, pricking pain, giddiness, exhaustion, contractures of fingers ( or tocs ), crackings, burning sensation, pain in vital organs, appearance of nodules or cysts-are the complications of Vatarakta; presence of one or more of these is an indication for refusal of such patients. The disease not associated with complications persists for the rest of life; that without complications is curable, likewise that in which only one dosa is involved and that of recent onset is curable, if two dosas are involved the disease persists for life, if all the three dosas are involved and that associated with complications is impossible to cure. ( 14-18 )

Thus ends the chapter on Vātarakta ·

#### अथोरुस्तम्भनिदानम्

#### URUSTAMBHA NIDANAM ( STIFFNESS OF THE LEG )

# शीतोष्णद्रवसंशुष्कगुरुद्रगुरुद्धिम्बिनिः । जीर्णाजीर्णं तथाऽऽयाससंक्षोभस्वम्नजागरैः ।। १ ॥ स्त्रश्रेष्ममेदःपद्यनः साममत्यर्थसंचितम् । अभिभूयेतरं दोषमूरू चेत्र्यतिपद्यते ॥ २ ॥ सक्थ्यस्थिनी प्रपूर्यान्तः श्लेष्ठमणा स्तिमितेन च । तदा स्त्रभाति तेनोरू स्तब्धौ शीतावचेतनौ ।। ३ ।। परकीयावित्र गुरू स्थातामतिभूशयथौ । ध्यानाङ्गमर्द्रस्तै।मत्यतन्द्राच्छन्द्रच्छद्यस्विज्वरैः ।। ४ ।। संयुक्तौ पादसदनक्रुकोद्धरणसुसिभिः । तम् रुस्तम्भमित्याद्युराढ्यवातमथापरे ॥ ५ ॥ (श्रा. नि. ५. १४)

Indulgence in foods and other activities which are a mixture opposites such as cold and hot, watery and dry, heavy and light, fatty and non-fatty, cooked and uncooked, etc., severe exertion, mental strain, over-sleeping awake without sleep and such other factors, cause for accumulation of ama ( improperly processed intermediary metabolities ) in the body which in turn increases the quantities of kapha, medas ( adipose tissue ) and vāta. These together accumulate in the leg, kapha fills up the cavities of the bones; making the limbs inactive; cold to touch, with loss of control over it, the patient feels as though the leg is not his own, severe pain, grief or anxiety, pain and stiffness all over the body, stupor, vomitting, anorexia, fever, weakness of the legs, difficulty in lifting and loss of sensetion in them. This disease is known as Urustambha; some others call it Adhyavata also. ( 1-5 )

## प्राप्रूपं तस्य निद्राऽतिध्यानं रितमितता ज्वरः । रोमहर्षोऽर्सचिद्धदितिः सदनं तथा ।। ६ ।।

Prodromal symptoms of this disease are too much of sleep, worry, loss of movement, fever, horripulations, anorexia, vomitting and weakness of the legs. ( 6 )

# वात शङ्क्रिभिरत्नानात्तस्य स्यात्स्रहनात्पूनः । पादयोः सदनं स्त्रसिः क्रञ्चादद्धरणं तथा ।। ७ ॥ जङ्घोरुन्सानिरत्यथे शश्वधादाहवेदने । पादं च व्यथते न्यस्तं शीतस्पर्शं न वेत्ति च ॥ ८ ॥ संस्थाने पीडनै गत्यां चालने वाण्यनीश्वरः। अन्यस्येव हि संभग्नावूरू पादौ च मन्यते ।। ९ ॥

Without knowing the specific features of this disease and suspecting it to be a disease of vata, if oil massage, etc., is done, the symptoms exacerbate ( increase ) quickly, cause severe debility in the legs, loss of sensation, difficulty in lifting it up, quick exhaustion of legs, loss of heat and pain. The patient gets pain on placing the foot on the ground, does not understand cold touch, is unable to keep the legs in any one posture, move it or do any type of activity, feels as though it belongs to someone else or feels as though it is broken in the middle. ( 7-9 )

> यदा दाहातितोदार्ती वेपनः पुरुषो भवेत् । ऊरुस्तम्भस्तदा इन्यात्साधयेदन्यथा नवम् ॥ १० ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २७ )

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने अरुस्तम्भनिदानं समाप्तम् ।। २४ ।।

Complaint of burning sensation, severe pain and tremors in the legs are to be understood as fatal symptoms. Treatment should be done to those who have no complications and those who have developed the discase very recently, ( 10 );

Thus ends the chapter on Urustambha

#### अधामवातनिदानम्

AMAVATA NIDĀNAM ( RHEUMATISM )

विरुद्धाद्वारचेप्रस्य मन्दाग्नेनिश्चलस्य च । स्निग्यं भुक्तवतो ह्यान्नं कुर्वतस्तदा ।। १ ॥ वायुना प्रेरितो ह्यामः श्रेष्मस्थानं प्रधान्नति । तेनात्यर्थं विद्ग्धोऽसौ धमनीः प्रतिषद्यते ।। २ ।। वानवित्तकर्फर्मूयो दूर्षितः सोऽन्नजो रसः । स्रोतांस्यभिष्यन्दयति नानावणों ऽतिपिच्छिकः ॥ ३ ॥ जनवरयाशु दौर्बल्यं गौरवं हृद्यस्य च । व्याधीनामाश्रयो होष आमसंक्रोऽतिदारुणः ॥ ४ ॥ युगपत्कुपितावन्तस्त्रिकसन्धिप्रवेशको । स्तब्धं च कुरुतो गात्रमामवातः स उच्यते ॥ ५ ॥

Indulgence in incompatible foods and habits, lack of physical activity, or doing exercise after taking foods and those who have poor digestive capacity even normally also; produce ama ( improperly digested food ) in the body. "This even normally also, produce and ( the body filling them and the dhamanis ( blood vessels ) with wasy material. Thus the bad end product of digestion associated with vata, pitts and kapha I hus the bad end product the tissue porcs and passages with thick waxy It produces weakness and heaviness of the heart, which becomes the material. scat of the disease. It also affects simultaneously the joints of the body such as those of waist, neck, shoulder, etc. This dreadful disease also ( ) 5, ) those of wills, "houly" the body becomes a cause of many other diseases also. ( 1-5 )

# अङ्गमदोऽर्दविस्तृष्णा ह्यालस्यं गौरवं उचरः । अपाकः शूनताऽङ्गानामामवातस्य त्वक्षणम् ॥६॥

Clinical features of amavata are pain all over the body, ( joints ), loss of taste, . thirst, lack of enthusiasm, heaviness, fever, indigestion and swelling of the body parts ( joints ). ( 6 )

# स क्रप्टः सर्वरोगाणां यद्। प्रकुपितो भवेत् । हस्तपादशिरोगुरूफश्रिज्ञानूरुसम्बद्धाः ।। ७ ॥ स केप्टः सर्वरागिणा चरा मेडुः रता करीति सरके शास्य वर्त्र राज्यस्य प्रार्थरानि वैरस्यं दाहं च बहुमूनताम् ॥ ९ ॥
जनयेरसोऽप्रिन्दीर्वेब्यं प्रसेक्षारचम् । उत्साहृद्धानि वैरस्यं दाहं च बहुमून्नताम् ॥ ९ ॥ जनवरसाठान्त्रर्यं तथा निद्राविपर्ययम् । तृद्धदिंभ्रममून्छन्दैश्च हृद्रमहं विड्विचद्धताम् । जाड्यान्त्रकूजमानाहं कष्टांश्चान्यानुपद्रवान् ॥ १० ॥

It becomes incurable when it affects all the joints of the hands, feet, head, heels, waist, knee and thighs causing painful swelling, which shifts from place to place ( joint to joint ) when the dosas move from one place to another, severe pain resembling that of a pecorpion sting. Poor digestion, salivation, anorexia, heaviness of the body, lack of enthusiasm, bad taste in the mouth, burning sensation, profuse urination, hardness of the abdomen and pain, loss of sleep, thirst, vomitting, giddiness, fainting, pain in the heart, constipation, incapacity of movement, intestinal gurglings and distention and many such difficult complications also develop. (7-10)

# पित्तात्सदाहरागं च सशूल्रं पवनानुगम् । स्तिमितं गुरुकण्डूं च कफदुष्टं तमादिशेस् ॥ ११ ॥

If pitta becomes the predominant dosa, there will be burning sensation and redness of the affected part ( joint ), if vāta is predominant, pain will be very severe, and if kapha is predominant loss of movement and itching will be seen. ( 11 )

# एकदोषानुगः साध्यो द्विद्रोषो याप्य उच्यते । सर्वदेहचरः शोधः स कृष्ठ्यः सान्निपातिकः ॥१२॥

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने आमवाननिदानं समाप्तम् ॥ २५ ॥

The disease is easy to cure if anyone dosa is involved, difficult if two dosas involved and incurable if all the three dosas are involved, so also if the are swelling is seen moving to all the joints of the body. ( 12 )

Thus ends the chapter on Amavata.

#### अथ शूलपरिणामस्त्रालान्द्रवाद्यन्त्रनिद्यानम्

# ŠŪLA, PARIŅĀMAŠŪLA, ANNADRAVAŠŪLA NIDĀNAM ( COLIC, PEPTIC ULCERS )

दोषैः पृथक् समस्तामद्वन्द्वैः शूल्कोऽप्रधा भवत् । सर्वेष्वतेषु श्रूणैषु प्रायेण पवनः प्रभुः ॥ १ ॥

Sula ( colic, pain in the abdomen ) is of eight types; one from each dosa, one from the combination of all three, one each from the combination of two dosas and the eighth from ama. In all types, vata is the predominant causative doşa. ( I )

> ध्यायामयानाद्रतिमेशुनाच्य प्रजागराच्छीत जलातिपानात् । कलायमुद्रगाढकिकोरद्र घादर्त्यर्थरूक्षाध्यशनाभिधातात् ॥ २ ॥ कषायतिकातिविरुढजान्नविरुद्धवलुरकशाकात् । विटश्रकम् श्रानिलवेगरोधारच्छोकोपवासादतिहास्यभाष्यात् ॥ ३ ॥ वायुः प्रवृद्धो जनयेदि शूल्तं हृत्यार्श्वपृष्ठप्रविकबस्तिदेशे । जीर्णे प्रदोषे च घनागमे च शीते च कोपं समुपैति गाढम् ॥ ४ ॥ मुहुम् हुन्नोपशामप्रकोपी विद्वातसंस्तम्भनतो द्वभेदैः । संस्वेदनाभ्यञ्जनमद्दनाद्यैः स्निग्धो ध्णभोज्य श्र श्रामं प्रयाति ॥ ५ ॥

Over-indulgence in physical exercises, riding, sexual intercourse, keeping awake at nights, drinking very cold water, ingestion of food articles like peas, greengram, tuvar dal, koradusa and others which cause dryness inside the body, over-eating, injury, foods which are astringent and bitter; germinated grains, incompatibles, dried up flesh and leafy vegetables; suppression of urges of feces, semen, urine, flatus, grief, starvation or fasting, excessive laughing and speakingall make for increase of vata in the body which produces colic; pain is found in the region of the heart, flanks, back, waist and urinary bladder, gets exacerbated during or after the digestion of food, in the evenings, during cloudy and cold weather; pain increases or decreases of its own accord frequently, of pricking or tearing in nature, there is obstruction to the movement of feces and flatus, pain gets reduced by use of hot fomentation, oil massage, fatty and warm foods and drinks. ( 2-5 )

> क्षारातिती क्णोषणविद्याद्वित लिष्पाव पिण्याक कुल्ययूषैः । कद्वम्लस्वीवीरसुराविकारै: कोधानलायासरविपतापै: 11 ६ 11

8 M

# म्राम्यातियोगादशनैर्विद्युः पित्तं प्रकूप्याश्य करोति शुल्म । तृष्मोहदाद्वादितंकरं हि नाम्यां संस्वेदमूच्छाभ्रमचोषयुक्तम् ॥ ७ ॥ मध्यन्दिने कुण्यति चार्धरात्रे विद्याहकाले जलदात्यये च । शीते च शीतैः समुपैति शान्ति सुरवादुशीतैर्राप मोजनैश्च ।। ८ ।।

Over-indulgence in articles of food which are alkaline, heat producing, very hot, causing burning sensation during digestion, oils, cow-pea, oil cake, soup of horsegram; which are pungent and sour, formented beverages like sauvira, sura, etc., anger, exposure to fire, fatigue, prolonged exposure to hot son; sexual intercourse, and irritating foods make for increase of pitta which in turn produces colic. Thirst, delusion, burning sensation near the umbilicus, perspiration, fainting, giddiness, localised burning sensation; pain exacerbates during midday or midnight, during digestion of food and in rainy weather; gets reluced by cold comforts, cold and sweet foods and drinks. ( 6-8 )

# आनूपवारिजकिलार पयोविकार में से से स्वर्धि प्रक्रुशा तिल्हश कुली मिः । अन्यैबेल्डासजनकैरपि हेतुभिश्च महेष्मा प्रकोपम्पगम्य करोति श्रूलम् ॥ ९ ॥ हृत्यासकाससद्दनारुचि संप्रसेकैरामा शये स्तिमित कोष्ठशिरोगुरुत्वैः । भुक्ते सदैव हि रुजं कुरुतेऽतिमात्रं सूर्योद्येऽथ्य शिशिरे कुसुमागमे च ॥ १० ॥

Over-indulgence in foods prepared out of flash of animals of marshy land and those living in water, buttermilk and other milk products, mutton, suger, foods containing powders of sesamum, blackgram, etc., and such other foods and habits make for increase of kapha which in turn produces colic. Oppression in the chest ( nausea ), cough, debility, anorexia, salivation, pain near the stomach, fullness and loss of movement in the abdomen, heaviness of the head, pain is severe soon after taking food, in the mornings at sunrise, in sisira and vasanta rius ( autumn and spring seasons ). ( 9-10 )

# सर्वेषु दोषेषु च सर्वालिङ्ग विद्याद्विषक सर्वभवं हि शुल्म । सुक्रष्टमेनं विषयज्रकरणं विवर्जनीयं प्रवद्ग्ति तज्ञ्वाः ॥ ११ ॥

The colic produced by the combination of all the three dosas together will have all the symptoms in severe form, during all the times, powerful and hard to treat like poison or fit to be refused treatment. ( 11 )

# आरोपहृल्लासवमीगुरुत्वस्तैमित्यकानाहकफप्रसेकैः । कफस्य लिङ्गिन समानलिङ्गमामोन्द्रवं शुल्मदाहरन्दि ॥ १२ ॥

Gurglings in the intestines, oppression in the chest ( nausea ), vomitting, feeling of heaviness, loss of movement and distention of the abdomen, salivation and colic having the symptoms similar to those of kaphaja type are all found in Amaja sula ( colic due to indigestion ). ( 12 )

# बस्तौ हृत्याश्वप्रष्ठेषु स शूलः कफवातिकः । कुक्षौ हृन्नाभिमध्येषु स शूल्रः कफपौत्तिकः ॥ १३ ॥ दाहज्वरकरो घोरो विज्ञेयो वातपैत्तिकः ।

If pain is found in the region of the heart, flanks and back it is due to increase of kapha and vata together; pain near the heart, umbilicus and centre of the abdomen it is due to kapha and pitta, severe pain accompanied with burning sensation and fever is due to vata and pitta closas together. ( 13 )

# पकदोषोत्थितः साध्यः कृद्धसाध्यो द्विदोषज्ञः ॥ १४ ॥ सर्वदोषोत्थितो घोरस्त्वसाध्यो भूर्युपद्रवः ।

Colic produced by the increase of anyone dosa is easily curable, that produced by combination of any two dosas is curable with difficulty, that produced by combination of all three dosas, that which is very severe and associated with many complications is impossible to cure. ( 14 )

# स्वैनिद्यानैः प्रकुपितो वायुः संनिद्वितस्तदा ॥ १५ ॥ कफपित्ते समावृत्य शूलुकारी भवेद्वली। भुक्ते जीर्यति यच्छूलं तदेव परिणाम्जम् ॥ १६ ॥ तस्य लक्षणमण्येतत्समासेनाभिधीयते । आध्यानानाटोपविण्मून्त्रविबन्धारतिवेपनैः ।। १७ ।। स्त्रिग्धोपश्रामधायं वातिकं तद्रदेद्रिषक्

Indulgence in foods and activities which cause the increase of all the three dosas together produces another severe type of colic which comes on soon after digestion of food. This is known as Parināmaja śūla and the following are its symptoms. In pariņāmasūla due to vāta, distention, gurgling noise in the intestines, obstruction to the movement of feces and urine, restlessness, severe suffering; pain gets reduced by fatty and warm foods are seen. ( 15-17 )

# तृष्णादाद्वारतिस्वेदं कद्गम्लवणोत्तरम् ॥ १८ ॥ शूलं शीतशामप्रायं पैत्तिकं लक्ष्येद्रधाः ।

In pittaja pariņāmasūla, there will be thirst, burning sensation, restlessness, perspiration, pain increases by use of pungent, sour and salty foods but gets reduced by cold foods and comforts. ( 18 )

# छदिहृलाससंमोहं स्वल्परुग्दीर्घसन्तति ॥ १९ ॥ कदृतिक्तोपशान्तं च तथ्य होयं कफात्मकम् ।

In kaphaja parināmašūla, vomitting, nausea, delusion, long continued mild pain getting reduced by pungent and bitter foods are seen. ( 19 )

# संस्यूष्टलक्षणं बुद्ध्वा द्विदोषं परिकल्पयेत् ॥ २० ॥ त्रिदोषजमसाध्यं तु क्षीणमांसबलानलम् ।

Appearance of symptoms of two or three dosas together are seen in conditions of increase of two or three dosas respectively. The tridosaja type associated with emaciation, loss of strength and digestion is considered as impossible to cure. ( 20 )

# जीणें जीर्यत्यजीणें वा यच्छूलमुपजायते ॥ २१ ॥ पथ्यापध्यप्रयोगेण मोजनामोजनेन च । न शमं याति नियमात्सोऽस्रद्रव उद्गहृतः ।। २२ ॥ ( अन्नद्रवाख्यशूलैषु न तावत्स्वास्थ्यमद्भुते । वान्तमान्त्रो जरन्त्रमाशु व्यपोद्यति ॥ १ ॥ )

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचित्र माध्रनिदाने शुलपरिणामशुलान्नद्रद्रवणूननिदानं समाप्तम् ।। २६ ।।

Yet another type of colic known as Annadrava sula, there will be severe pain persisting throughout and not subsiding during or after digestion, with food or no food, use of comforts or no comforts. ( In this type the pain is reduced only after vomitting of accumulated pitta. ) ( 21-22 )

Thus ends the chapter on Sula, etc.

# अथोदावर्तानाहनिदानम्

# UDĀVARTA-ĀNĀHA NIDĀNAM ( REVERSE PERISTALISIS, ETC., )

वातविण्मूत्रजम्भास्त्रम् दोद्गारवर्मीन्द्रियाः । क्ञुत्तरणोच्छासनिद्राणां धूत्योद्दावर्तसंभवः ॥ १ ॥ (सु.ज.अ ४१)

Suppression of the urges of flatus, feces, urine, yawning, tears, sneezing, belching, vomitting, semen, hunger, thirst, respiration and sleep, is the cause for the disease Udavarta. { } }

# बातम्र्मपूरीषाणां सङ्कोऽध्मानं क्लबो रज्ञा । जटरे वातज्ञान्ये रोगाः स्यूर्वातनित्रद्वातु ।। २ ।। (च.सू.अ. ७)

Suppressing the movement of flatus leads to difficulty for expelling flatus, feces and urine, distention of the abdomen, weakness, pains in the abdomen and other symptoms of Vata vrddhi. ( 2 )

# आरोपशूली परिकर्तिका च सङ्गः पुरीषस्य तथोध्ववातः । पुरीष्मास्थाद्यवा निरेति पूरीषवेगेऽभिद्यते नरस्य ॥ ३ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ५५ )

Suppression of urge of feces produces intestinal gurglings, pain in the abdomen, cutting pain in the rectum, constipation and even upward movement of flatus and feces through the mouth. ( 3 )

# बस्तिमेद्वनयोः शूल्यं मुत्रकृष्ण्ड शिरोरुजा । विनामो वङ्कणानाद्यः स्याल्त्रिङ्गं मूत्रनिग्रहे ।। ४ ।। ( च. मु. अ. ७ )

Suppression of the urge of urine causes pain in the bladder and urethra, difficulty to urinate, headache, bending of the body forward and distention/obstuction of the groins. ( 4 )

# मन्यागल्ध्तम्भशिरोविकारा जम्भोपधातात्पवनात्मकाः स्य् : । तथाऽक्षिनासावदनामयाश्र भवन्ति तीव्राः सह कर्णरोगैः ॥ ५ ॥

Suppression of yawning produces stiffness of the neck, throat, headaches, other symptoms of Vata vrddhi and also disorders of eyes, nose, face and ears. ( 5 )

# आनन्दजं वा प्रथ शोकजं वा नैत्रोदकं प्राप्तमम्भूतो हि । शिरोगुरुत्वं नयनामयाश्च भवन्ति तीवाः सद्ध पोनसेन ॥ ६ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ५ र )

Suppres ion of tears of either pleasure or grief causes heaviness of the head, disorders of the eyes and severe nasal catarrh. ( 6 )

## मन्यास्तम्भः शिरःशूलुमदितार्धावभेदकौ । इन्द्रियाणां च दौर्बल्यं क्षयथोः स्याद्विधारणात् ॥ ७ ॥ ( च. सू. अ. ७ )

Suppression of the bout of sneeze, gives rise to stiffness of the neck, headache of one side, migraine and debility of sensory organs. ( 7 }

# कण्ठास्यपूर्णत्वमतीव तोदः क्रूजश्च वायोरथवाऽप्रवृत्तिः । उद्गारवेगेऽभिइते भवन्ति घोरा विकाराः प्रवनप्रसूताः ॥ ८॥ ( मु. उ. अ. १५ )

Suppression of the belchings causes feeling of fullness and pain of the throat and mouth, movement of air upward with mild sound or total obstruction and such other symptoms of Vatavrddhi. ( 8 )

# कण्डकोठार्यन्त्र्याण्ड्रामयज्वराः । कुष्ठवीसर्पद्धृहृासाध्छदिनित्रहजा गद्दाः ।। ९ ॥ ( च. सु. अ. ७ )

Suppression of vomitting produces irritation ( itchings ) and appearance of rashes on the skin, anorexia, black patches, oedema, anaemia, fever, leprosy, ervsepelas and oppression in the chest. ( 9 )

# मुत्राये वै गृदमुष्कयोश्च शोधो रुजा मूत्रविनिय्रहश्च। शुकाद्दमरी तत्स्रवणं मवेच्य ते ते विकारा विद्युते च शुक्रे ॥ १० ॥

Oedema and pain in the bladder, rectum and scrotum; formation and discharge of gravel of semen are the effects of suppression of seminal discharge. ( 10 )

# तन्द्राङ्गमदावरुचिः श्रमश्च क्षुधामिघातात्कृशता च दृष्ट्रेः । कण्ठास्यशोषः श्रवणावरोधस्तृष्णाविधाताद्धदये व्यथा च ॥ ११ ॥

Stupor, bodyaches, anorexia, exhaustion, weakness and loss of eye sight are the effects of suppression of hunger; suppression of thirst leads to dryness of the throat and mouth, difficulty in hearing and pain in the region of the heart. ( 11 )

## श्रान्तस्य निःश्वासविनिग्रहेण हृद्रोगमोहावश्यवाऽपि गुल्मः । जम्भाऽङ्गमर्दोऽक्षिशिरोरिजाड्यं निद्राभिधाताद्यवाऽपि तन्द्रा ॥ १२ ॥ ( सु. ज. ३५ )

Suppression of the respiration after exertion causes heart diseases, delusion and abdominal tumors. Suppression of urge of sleep produces yawning, bodyaches, diseases of the cyes and head and stupor. ( 12 )

# वायुः कोष्ठानुगो रूसैः कषायकदृतिक्तः । भोजनैः कुपितः सद्य उदावर्तं करोति हि ॥ १३ ॥ वातमूत्रपुरीषासुक्रफमेदोवहानि वै। स्रोतांस्युदावतंयति पुरीषं चातिवर्तयेत् ॥ ४ ॥ ततो हृद्वस्तिशुलातों हलुस्तसारतिपीडितः । वातमन्त्रपरीषाणि कृच्छेण लुभते नरः ।। १५ ॥ श्वासकासम्रतिद्दयायदाहमोहत्तृष्ठाज्वरान् । वमिहिकाशिरोगमनःश्रवणविश्रमान् । बहूनन्यांश्च लमते विकारान् वातकोपजान् ॥ १६ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ५५ )

Ingestion of foods which are very dry, astringent, pungent and bitter might cause sudden increase of vata in the abdomen and produce Udavarta instantly. The normal direction of movement in the passages of flatus, urine, feces, blood, kapha ( fluids ) and medas ( adipose tissue ) gets reversed ( or obstructed ), feces gets dried up; pain in the region of the heart and bladder; nausea, restlessness, passing of flatus, urine and feces with difficulty, increased respiration, cough, running in the nose, burning sensation, confusion, thirst, fever, vomitting, hiccough, headaches, unsteady gait, disorders of hearing and many such symptoms of Vataprakopa ( increase ) are seen in this condition. ( 13-16 \

> आमं शक्तद्वा निचितं क्रमेण भूयो विबद्धं विगुणानिलैन । प्रवर्तमानं न यथास्वमेनं विकारमानाहमुदाहरन्दित ।। १७ ।। तस्मिन् भवन्त्यामसमुद्भवे तु तृष्णार्वतिशयायशिरोविदाहाः । आमाशये शूलमधो गुरुत्वं हृत्स्तम्भ उद्भारविधातनं च ॥ १८ ॥ स्तम्भः कटीपृष्ठपुरीषमूत्रे शूलोऽथ मूर्खा शक्तथ्य छदिँः । श्वासश्च पकाशयजे भवन्ति तथाऽल्हसोक्तानि च लक्ष्णानि ।। १९ ।। ( सु. उ. अ. ५६ )

Gradual accumulation of ama ( undigested food materials ), or feces inside the alimentary tract, causes obstruction to their normal movement and produces the disease known as Anaha. If it is due to accumulation of ama; thirst, running in the nose, burning sensation in the head, pain and heaviness in the stomach, pain in the heart and non-movement of belching will be seen; if it is due to accumulation of feces, stiffness of the joints of the waist and back, obstruction of feces and urine, pain, faintings, fecal vomitting, difficult respiration and other symptoms of the disease Alasaka will be found. ( 17-19 )

तृष्णादितं परिक्किष्ठं क्षीणं शूलैरभिद्रतम् । शक्नद्वमंतं मतिमान् उद्दावर्तिनमुरस्जेत् ॥ २० ॥ (मु. उ. अ. ५४)

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने उदावर्तानाहनिदानं समाप्तम् ।

A patient of udavarta suffering from severe thirst, restlessness or severe distress, emaciation, continuous obstinate abdominal pain and fecal vomitting should be refused treatment. ( 20 )

Thus ends the chapter on Udavarta.

## अथ गुल्मनिदानम्

# GULMA NIDANAM ( ABDOMINAL TUMORS )

# द्र्था वातादयोऽत्यर्थं मिथ्याहारविद्वारतः । कुर्वान्ति पञ्चा गुरुमं कोष्ठान्तर्यन्थिन्थरूपिणम् । तस्य पञ्चविधं स्थानं पार्श्वदृष्ट्रन्नाभिबरतयः ॥ १ ॥

Vata and other dosas un lergoing morbid increase by indulgence in unhealthy foods and habits, cause the five kinds of Gulma ( tumor ) to develop inside the abdomen. Its five common sites are the two parsivas ( sides of the abdomen, hypochondrium, lumbar and iliac regions ), the region of the brdaya ( epigastrium ) the region of the nabhi ( umbilical region ) and the region of the basti ( hypogastric region ). ( 1 )

( मु. उ. अ. ४२ )

Thirst, increase of body temperature, burning sensation, localised warmth, tiredness ( wcakness ) of the hear), feeling of movement of hot air inside the chest, fainting, perspiration and dryness of the mouth are the symptoms of Pittaja hrdroga. ( 4 )
