Nausea, salivation, pricking pain, oppression in the chest, drowsiness, loss of taste, blackish appearance of the eyes and oedema are the symptoms of Krimija hrdroga. ( 6 )

# man श्रद्धाः स्टब्स् शोधो ज्ञेयास्तेषा पुपद्रवाः । किमिजे किमिज्ञातीनां स्व ये मताः । ७ ॥

इति धीमाधयकरविरचित्रं आध्रप्रनिद्याने हुद्रोगनिदानं समाप्तम् ॥ २९ ॥

Debility, exhaustion, giddiness, emaciation and such other complications as seen in kaphaja krimi roza will also be seen in krimija hrdroga. ( 7 )

Thus ends the chapter on Hrdroga.

# अथ मूत्रक्रच्छनिदानम्

# MŪTRAKRCHRA NIDANAM ( DYSURIA, STRANGURY )

# व्यायामतीक्ष्णौ वधरूक्षमद्यप्रसर्व्यवस्त्रप्रद्यानात् । आनूपमांसाध्यशनादजीर्णात्स्युर्मू त्रकृद्धूणणि नृणां तथाऽध्यौ ।। १ ।। प्रथख्यालाः स्वैः कुपिता निद्दानैः सर्वेऽथवा कोपमुपेत्य बस्तौ । मूत्रस्य मार्ग परिपीडयन्ति यदा तदा सूत्रयतोह कृच्छ्रात् ॥ २ ॥ ( च. चि. १. २६ )

י הי

Over-indulgence in physical exercise, consuming heat producing medicines, and alcoholic drinks; fast riding on animals ( or other vehicles ) for long time, eating flesh of animals ( or birds ) of marshy places, eating foods too frequently and indigestion; these and other causes make for increase of one or all the dosas together, which in turn get localised in the urinary bladder and urinary tract producing difficulty for micturition. This disease called as Mütrakrchra is of eight kinds. ( 1-2 )

> तीवार्तिरुग्वर्ष्वरणबर्स्तिमेट स्वल्पं मुद्रमुत्रयतीहू वातात् । पीतं सरक्तं सरुजं सदाहं क्रच्छू मुहुम्न्यतीह पित्तात् ॥ ३ ॥ बस्तेः सलिङ्गस्य गुरुव्वशोधौ मूत्रं सपिच्छ कफमूत्रकृत्वृ । सर्वाणि रूपाणि नु सन्निपातान्द्रवन्ति तत्कृद्ध्रतमं हि कृष्ट्रम् ॥ ४॥ ( च चि. अ. २६ )

In vātaja type, the patient experiences severe pain in the groins, region of the bladder and penis, passes scanty urine frequently. In pittaja type, the urine is yellow, mixed with blood and is passed out with difficulty associated with pain and burning sensation. In kaphaja type, the patient feels heaviness and swelling of the region of the bladder and penis and the urine is thick and viscid. In tridosaja type, all the above symptoms are found together and in severe form. ( 3-4 )

# सम्बाहिषु शल्येन क्षतेष्ठ्यमिद्रतेषु वा । मूत्रकृच्छे तदाघाताज्ञायते भूशदारुणम् ॥ ५ ॥ वातकृछेण तुल्यानि तस्य लिङ्गानि निर्दिशेत् ।

Injury to the urinary tract by foreign bodies also cause difficulty for urination, which resembles vātaja type in its symptoms. ( 5 )

# शक्तस्तु प्रतीघाताद्वायुविगुणतां गतः ।। ६ ।। आध्यानं वातशूलं च मूत्रसङ्गं करोति च ।

Obstruction to the movement of feces by suppression of the urge, causes its reverse movement, producing distention of the abdomen, colic of vataja type and difficulty in micturition. ( 6 )

# अध्यरीहेत् तत्पूर्वं मूत्रकृद्धुदाहरेत् ॥ ७ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ५६ ) शुक्रे दोषैरुपहृते मूत्रमार्गे विधाविते । सशुक्रं मूत्रयेल्कब्ळ्राद्धस्तिमेहनशूलवान् ॥ ८ ॥

Mūtrakrchra is also seen as an early symptom of asmarī ( urinary caliculii ), sukra ( semen ) vitiated by the dosas also causes difficulty in micturition by the color a sukra ( schien ) Vitated by the doped .
with elimination of urine mixed with semen, pain in the region of the bladder and penis. ( 7-8 )

# अक्सरी शर्करा चैव तुल्यसंभवल्क्षणे । विशेषणं शर्करायाः श्टणु कीर्तयतो मम ॥ ९ ॥ जद्मस्य रामाशः व दुरुपर्यमाणा च वायुना । विमुक्तकक्रसन्धाना क्षरन्ती शर्करा मता।।१०॥ हृत्पीडा वेपथुः शूलं कुक्षावस्मिश्च दुर्बलः ।

तया भवति मूच्छा च मूत्रकृद्धूं च दारुणम् ॥ ११ ॥ ( सु. उ. अ. ५९ ) मूत्रवेगनिरस्ताभिः प्रशमं याति वेदना । यावदस्याः पुनर्नँति गुडिका स्रोतसो मुखम् ॥ १२ ॥

इति श्रीमात्रवकरविरचिने माधवनिदाने मुत्रक्रच्छ्रनिदानं समाप्तम् ।। ३० ।।

Asmari ( calculii ) and sarkara ( gravel ) are similar in their causes and symptoms; asmari ( calculus ) undergoing processing by pitta, dehydration by vāta and losing the property of cohesion ( or sticking together ) of kapha is broken into tiny particles and passed out. This is called sarkara ( gravel ). Difficulty in micturition caused by sarkara ( gravel ) is associated with pain in the region of the heart, shivering, pain in the egigastrium, poor digestive capacity, fainting and great vering, pain in the one of the urinary tract subsides after climination of urine and appears again when the passage is obstructed by the gravel or stone. ( 9–12 )

Thus ends the chapter on Asmari.

# अथ मूत्राघातनिद्यनम् ।

# MŪTRĀGHĀTA NIDĀNAM ( SUPPRESSION OF URINE )

# जायन्ते कुपितैद्रौषिमूं त्राघातास्त्रयोदश । प्रायो मूत्रविधाताधैवः तकुण्डलिकाद्यः ।। १ ॥

By causes such as suppression of natural urges, etc., thirteen kinds of Mutraghāta commencing with vātakuņḍalikā are produced by one or more of the tridoşas. ( 1 )

# रौक्ष्याद्वेगविघाताद्वा बायुर्बस्तौ सवेदनः । मूत्रमाविद्य चरति विगुणः कुण्डलोकृतः ।। २ ।। मूत्रमल्पाल्पमथवा सर्वजं संप्रवर्तते ।

# वातकुण्डलिकां तां तु व्याधि विद्यात्सुदारुणम् ॥ ३ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. १ = )

Indulgence in dry foods, suppression of urges, etc., make for increase of vata in the urinary bladder, which moves all around with severe pain and scanty urination. This dreadful disease in known as Vatakundalikā. ( 2-3 )

आध्यापयन्बस्तिगुदं रुद्ध्वा वायुश्चलोन्नताम् । कुर्यात्तीवार्तिमर्शलां मूत्रविण्मार्गरोधिनीम् ॥ ४ ॥ Vata getting increased, causes enlargement of aşthilā (prostrate gland) producing distention of the bladder and rectum, severe pain and obstruction to the passage of urine and feces. This disease is known as Asthila. ( 4 )

# वेगं विधारयेद्यस्त्र मूत्रस्याकुशल्लो नरः। निरुणाद्धि मुखं तस्य बस्तेबस्तिगतोऽनिलः ।। ५ ॥ मूत्रसङ्गो भवेत्तेन बस्तिकुक्षिनिर्पीडितः ।

वातवस्तिः स विज्ञेयो व्याधिः कृञ्कृप्रसाधनः ॥ ६ ॥ ( मु. ३. अ. २५ )

In an unwise person who suppresses the urge of urination for long, vata located in the bladder blocks the urinary passage causing retention of urine and pain in the region of the bladder, This disease known as Vatabasti is difficult to treat. { 5-6 }

# चिरं धारयतो मूत्रं त्वरया न प्रवर्तते । मेहमानस्य मन्दं वा मूत्रातीतः स उच्यते ।। ७ ।।

Müträtita is that condition in which the person who has suppressed the urge of urination for a long time, fails to void the urinc with the normal speed ( force ) but voids it very slowly. ( 7 )

# मूश्रस्य वेगेऽभिहते तदुदावर्तद्वेतुकः । अपानः कुपितो वायुरुदरं पूरयेःद्धशम् ॥ ८ ॥ नाभेरधस्तादाध्मानं जनयेन्तीववेदनम् ।

तन्मूत्रजठरं विद्याद्दधोबस्तिनिरोधनम् ॥ ९ ॥ ( गु. ज. अ. ५३ )

Suppression of urge of urination is the cause for udavarta. Apanavāta getting increased, begins to move upwards, accumulates in the abdomen, causing distention of the abdomen below the umbilicus, severe pain and obstruction in the urinary bladder. This condition is known as Mūtrajaṭhara. ( 8-9 )

# बस्तौ घाऽप्यथवाः नालै मणौ वा यस्य देहिनः । मूत्रं प्रवृत्तं सज्जेत सरकं वा प्रवाद्यतः ।। १० ।। स्वेच्छनररूपमरूपं सरुजं याऽथ नीरजम् ।

विगुणानिलजो व्याधिः स मूत्रोत्सदृसंसितः ॥ ११ ॥ ( मु. उ. अ. ५.= )

The disease characterised by obstruction to the flow of urine either in the bladder, urethra or glans penis, the urine gets obstructed and flows out after straining mixed with blood, slowly and in small quantities, with or without pain is known as Mūtrotsanga and is due to increased vata. ( 10-11 )

# रूक्षस्य क्लान्तदेहस्य बस्तिस्थौ पित्तमारुतौ । मूत्रक्षयं सरुण्दाहं जनयेतां तदाह्नयम् ॥ १२॥ ( मृ. उ. अ. ५६ )

In a person who is habituated to dry foods and physical fatigue, pitta and vata undergoing increase in the urinary bladder make for Mutraksaya ( reduction vatur andorg of urine in it, ) associated with pain and burning sensation inside. ( 12 )

# अन्तर्वस्तिम्तमुखे वृत्तः स्थिरोऽल्पः सहस्रा भवेत् । अक्मरीत्रूयरुग्रन्थिर्मूत्रम्रन्थः स उच्यते ।। १३ ॥ ( वा. नि. अ. ९ )

A round, stationary mass developing quickly inside the urinary bladder, presenting symptoms similar to that of asmari ( calculus ) is known as Mütragranthi. ( 13 )

# मूत्रितस्य स्त्रियं यातो वायुना शुक्रमुद्धतम् । स्थानाच्युनं मूत्रयतः प्राक् पश्चाद्धा प्रवर्नते ।। १४ ॥ ( वा. नि. ऋ. ९ ) भस्मोद्दकपतीकाशं मूत्र्युक्रं तदुच्यते ।

In a person who indulges in sexual intercourse suppressing the urge of urination, the semen getting dislodged flows out of the body either before or after micturition, making the urine to resemble a solution of ash. This disease is known as Mūtraśukra. ( 14 )

# व्यायामाध्यातपैः पित्तं बस्ति प्राप्यानिलान्वितम् ॥ १५ ॥ बरिंत मेढ़ गुदं चैव प्रदहेत्स्रावयेदधः । सुध्य हारिद्रमथवा सरक्तं रक्तमेव वा ॥ १६ ॥ { मु. उ. अ. ५६ } कृञ्छात्पुनः पुनर्जन्तोरुष्णवातं ब्रवन्ति तम् ।

Over-indulgence in physical exercise, long distance walking and exposure to sun make for increase of pitta, which getting associated with vata in the urinary sun make for mercase of pritas in the bladder, urethra and rectum; urine becomes bladder Causes burning semation in blood or only blood flows out in small quantities frequently. This disease is known as Usnavāta. ( 15-16 )

# पित्तं कफो द्वावपि वा संहन्येतेऽनिलेलेन चेत ।। १७ ।। क्रुख्छान्मूत्रं तदा पीतं श्वेतं रक्तं घनं स्नजेत । सदाहं रोचनाशङ्गचुर्णवर्ण भवेत्त तत् ॥ ? ? ॥ शुष्क समस्तवणं वा मूत्रसादं वदन्ति तम् । ( वा नि. अ. ९ )

Pitta and kapha either alone or in combination associating with v tta produce the disease called Mutrasada, characterised by difficulty for micturiti n, urine is coloured yellow, white or red resembling the colour of oxbile ( yel ow or powder of conch shell ( white ) or other different colours; is thick, causes burning sensation is non-viscid. ( 17-18 )

# रूक्षद्रबंलयोर्वातेनोदावृत्तं शक्तद्यदा ।। १९ ।। मुत्रस्रोतोऽन्रपद्येत विटसंख्यं तदा नरः । विद्वन्धं मुत्रयेत्कृच्छाद्रिडविघातं विनिर्दिशेत् ॥ २० ॥ (वा. नि. अ. २

In a person suffering from udavarta caused by suppression of urge of defacation, the feces getting into the urinary bladder produces difficulty for micturition, the urine assuming the smell of feces. This condition is known as Vidvighata. (19-20)

# द्रताध्वल्रबनायासेरमिघातारप्रपीडनात् । स्वस्थानाद्रस्तिरुद्रत्तः स्थूलस्त्रस्ति गर्मवन् ॥ २१ ॥ शूल्स्पन्दनदाहातों बिन्दुं विन्दुं स्नवत्यपि । पीडितस्न् स्नुजेद्धारां संस्तम्भोद्वेष्टनार्तिमान् ॥ २२ ॥ बस्तिकुण्डलमाहस्तं घोरं शस्त्रविषोपमम् । पवनप्रवलं प्रायो दुर्निवारमनुद्धिभिः । तस्मिन्वित्तान्विते दाहः शुलं मन्त्रविवर्णता ।। २३ ॥

# श्लेष्मणा गौरवं शोथः स्निधं मूत्रं घनं सितम् । श्लेष्मरुद्धविलो बस्तिः पित्तोदीणों न सिध्यति ॥२४॥ अविध्यन्तबिलः साध्यो न त् यः कुण्डलीकृतः । स्याद्वस्तौ कुण्डलीभूते तुण्मोहः श्वास पव च ॥२५॥ इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने मुत्राधातनिद्यनं समासम् ।। ३१ ।।

By indulgence in fast walking, jumping and other exercises causing fatigue, injury ( by blunt weapons ) ete. the vata getting increased displaces the urinary bladder upwards to the centre of the abdomen, making it to resemble a pregnant uterus; urine is voided with difficulty, drop, accompanied by pain, tremors and burning sensation (inside the bladder), urine flows out ouly on pressing the abdomen hard but stops if pressure is released, the pain and obstruction being very severe due to predominance of vāta. This disease known as Basiikuņdala is dreadful like a sharp weapon or poison and is incurable. If pitta also gets involved there will be burning sensation, pain and discoluration of urine; if kapha is involved, heaviness, swelling, the urine is oily, thick and white; the disease becomes incurable if the orifice of the bladder is blocked by thick kapha, or if the bladder gets ulcerated by pitta, or it becomes rolled up like a ball, associated with thirst, delusion and dyspnoea. (21-25)

Thus ends the chapter on Mutraghata.

#### CHAPTUR 32

# अथाइमरीनिद्यनम्

# AŠMARĪ NIDĀNAM ( VESICAL CALCULUS )

# वातपित्तकफैस्तिस्तस्रश्चनुर्थो शुक्रजा ऽपरा प्रायः श्लेष्माश्रयाः सर्वा अदमर्यः स्युर्यमोपमाः ॥ १ ॥

One each from vata, pitta and kapha, fourth from their combination together and another one from sukra ( semen ); thus Asmari ( stone ) is of five kinds. All of them are having kapha as the basic dosa. Asmaris are dreadful like the god of death. ( 1 )

#### विशोषयेद्वस्तिगतं सशुकं मूत्रं सपित्तं पवनः कफं वा । यदा तदाऽधमर्युपजायते तु क्रमेण पित्तेष्विव रोचना गोः ॥ २ ॥ निकःदोषाश्रयाः सर्वाः, ( ज. चि. अ. २६ )

Asmart ( stone, calculi ) gets formed when vāta dries up the semen, urine, pitta or kapha stored in the urinary bladder, just as biles up the senen, urine,
bladder of \ the coup All t bladder of ) the cow. All types of asmaris are caused by the combination of all the three dosas. ( 2 )

#### अध्ययां : पूर्वल्रक्षणम् । बस्त्याध्मानं तदासन्नदेशेष परितोऽतिरुक् ॥ ३ ॥ मूत्रेषु बस्तगन्धत्वं मूत्रकृद्ध ज्वरोऽर्रुचिः । ( वा. नि. व. ६ )

Premonitory symptoms of Asmaris are distention of the bladder, severe pain in and around it, the smell of urine resembling that of a goat, difficulty in micturition, fever and loss of appetite. ( 3-4 )

> सामान्यलिङ्कं रूङ्ग्नाभिसेवनीबस्तिमूर्धसु ॥ ४ ॥ विशीर्णधारं मूत्रं स्यात्तथा मार्गें निरोधिते । तद्ध्यपायात्सूखं मेहेदच्छुं गोमेदकोपमम् ॥ ५ ॥ तत्संक्षो भारत्क्षते सास्त्रमायासाच्यातिरुग्भवेत् । ( वा. नि. अ. ९ )

General signs and symptoms are pain in the region of the umbilicus, perineum and dome of the bladder; urinary flow interrupted when blocked by the stone and resuming on its dislodging, urine is clear, having the colour of gomedaka gem (yellowish red in colour ), mixed with blood due to the bleeding caused by friction and experience of severe pain on movement. ( 4-5 )

```
तत्र वाताद्भुशं चार्तों दन्तान् खादति वेपते 11 ६ 11
ग्रहाति मेहनं मेहनं नामि पोडयरयनिशं क्वणन् ।
          मुश्चति     शक्तु-मुद्दुमेहति    बिन्दुशः II ७ II
सानिलं क
द्यावारुणाऽदमरी चार्त्य स्याच्चिता कण्टकौरिव ।  ( वा. नि. अ. ९ )
```
Symptoms of Vātaja asmarī are very severe pain making the person to grind his teeth and tremble, to weep and squeeze his penis and umbilicus constantly, feces is expelled along with flatus, urine drop by drop frequently; the stone will be black or reddish black in colour with thorn/sprouts. ( 6-7 )

# पित्तेन द्वाते वरितः प्रध्यमान इवोध्मवान् II ८ H भल्नातकास्थिसंस्थाना रक्तपीताऽसिताऽद्यमरी ।

In Pittaja type, there will be severe burning sensation inside the bladder ( as though smeared with alkalies ), region of the bladder is hot to touch; the stone resembles the seed of bhallataka, ( semecarpus anacardium ) having red, yellow or black colour. ( 8 )

> बास्तिर्मित्रद्यत इच श्रेष्मणा शोतलो गुरुः ॥ ९ ॥ अद्यमरी मद्वती श्रृक्ष्णा मधुवर्णाऽथवा सिता । ( वा. नि. व. ९ )

In Kaphaja type pricking pain in the bladder, cold to touch, feeling of heaviness of the bladder; the stone will be big in size, smooth, slightly brown or white in colour. ( 9 )

#### प्रता भवन्ति बालानां तेषामेव च भूयसा ।। १० ।। अश्रयो प्रचयार्व्यव्यव्यव्यव्यवे

These ( the above three types ) are common in young boys and girls and it is easy to remove the stones ( by surgical operation ) in them because of the small size of the bladder and the stone. ( 10 )

# शुकारमरी तू महतां जायते शुक्रधारणात् 11 ११ 11 स्थानाच्च्यूनम् नुक्तं हि मुफ्करयोरन्तरे जिल्हः । शोषयत्युपसंगृह्य शुक्रं तब्छुकमध्मरी ।। १२ ॥ बस्तिरुङमूत्रकृत्वमुष्ठः श्वयथुकारिणी । तस्यामुत्पन्नमात्रायां शुक्रमेति विल्लीयते ।। १३ ॥ पीडिते त्वयकाशोऽस्मिन् ( ( वा. नि. अ. ९ )

Sukraja asmari gets formed in adults who withhold the expulsion of semen in the middle ( of the urethra ). Thus obstructed, the semen gets dried up by vata, inside the scrotum forming a stone. Such a person will have pain in the bladder, difficulty for micturition, swelling of the scrotum;' the semen getting released by rubbing the urethral passage. ( 11-13 )

<sup>1.</sup> Testes to be more appropriate.

अद्ययेव च शर्करा । अणुशो वायुना भिन्ना सा तस्मिन्नुलुलोमगे ।। १४ ।। निरोति सह ( वा. नि. अ. द ) मूत्रस्नोतःप्रवृत्ता सा सक्ता कुर्यादुपद्रवान् 11 १५ 11 दौर्वल्यं सदनं कार्श्ये कुक्षिशूल्रमथारुचिम् । पाण्डुत्वमुष्णवातं च तृष्णां हृत्यीडनं वसिम् ॥ १६ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. ३ )

Sarkara ( gravel ) is the same as asmari, having been broken up into minute particles by vata, being eliminated out mixed along with urine. If it gets bigger and obstructs the passage, complications such as debility, exhaustion, emaciation, pain in the lower abdomen, loss of appetite, anaemia, burning micturition, thirst, pain in the heart and vomitting will develop. ( 14-16 )

> बद्धमूत्रं रुजातूरम् । प्रशुननाभिन् बणं अद्यूनिवासी क्षेपयत्याशु सिकला शर्करान्विता ॥ १७ ॥ ( सु. मू. अ. ३३ )

इति श्रीमाधवक्तरविरचित्रे माधवनिदानेऽश्मरीनिदानं समाप्तम् ॥ ३२ ॥

Asmari associated with gravel or without it, is going to kill the person who develops severe distention of the umbilical region and scrotum, total obstruction to micturition and pain. ( 17 )

Thus ends the chapter on Asmarl.

# अथ प्रमेहप्रमेहपिडकानिदानम्

PRAMEHA, PRAMEHA PIDAKĀ NIDĀNAM ( POLYUREA ) DIABETES, CORBUNCLES )

भास्यासुखं स्वप्तसुखं दधीनि ग्राम्यौदकानूपरसाः पर्यासि । गुडवैदृतं च नव्यान्नपान्नं ( च. चि. अ. ७ )

Indulgence in sitting on soft cushions for long periods ( thus avoiding physical g activity ), sleeping for long hours, use of curds, flesh of animals of domestic, aquatic or of marshy places, milk ( and its preparations ), fresh grains, fresh water, puddings made of jaggery/sugar ( and its other products ) and all other similar factors wahich bring about increase of kapha in the body are the causes for Prameha ( polyurealdiabetes ). ( 1 )

> मेदश्य मांस च शरीरज च क्रेदं कफो बस्तिगतः प्रदृष्य । करोति मेहान समुद्दीर्णमुण्णैस्तानैव पित्तं परिदूष्य चापि ॥ २ ॥ क्षीणेषु दोषेष्ववृद्य धातून् संदूष्य मेद्वान् कुरुतेऽनिल्हश्य। साध्याः कफोत्था दश, पित्तजाः षड् याप्या, न साध्यः पवनाच्यतुष्कः ॥ ३ ॥ समक्रियत्वाद्विषमक्रियरवान्मद्वात्ययः वाद्याक्यं यथाकमं ते ॥

Kapha undergoing increase, vitiates the medas ( fat ), mamsa ( muscle ) and kleda ( body fluids ), draws them to the urinary bladder and produces prameha; similarly the pitta affects them, vata also brings about vitiation in them and produce prameha. The ten kinds produced by kapha are easily curable, six kinds by pitta are controllable and four kinds by vāta are incurable respectively; because of similarity ( in respect of the causitive dosas and the vitiated dhātus having identical properties ); dissimilarity ( in respect of causitive dosa and vitiated dhātus having different properties ) and grave consequences ( vātaja types giving rise to severe complications quickly ). ( 2-3 )

# कफः सपित्तः पवनश्च दोषा, मेदोऽस्वशुक्राम्बुवसाल्रसीकाः । मज्जा रसौजः पिशितं च दूष्याः, प्रमेद्विणां विशतिरेव मेहाः ।। ४ ।।

( वा. नि. अ. १० ) In all the twenty varieties of prameha, vāta, pitta and kapha together form the causitive dosas; medas ( fat ), asrk ( blood ), sukra ( semen ), ambu ( body fluids ), vasā ( muscle fat ), lasīkā ( tissue fluid ), majjā ( marrow ), rasa ( lymph ), ojas ( essence of all the dhātus ) and pišita ( muscle tissue ) are the dūsya, ( affected tissues ). ( 4 )

# दन्तादीनां मलाढ्यत्वं प्राम्रपं पाणिपादयोः । दाहश्चिकणता देहे तृट् स्वाद्वास्यं च जायते ॥ ५ ॥ व ( वा. नि. अ. १० )

Premonitory symptoms are accumulation of dirt on the teeth ( mouth, eyes, nose, ears ), feeling of burning sensation in the palms and soles, stickiness of the skin all over the body, thirst and a sweet taste in the mouth. ( 5 )

# सामान्यं लक्षणं तेषां प्रभूताविलमूत्रता । दोषदूर्ष्याविशेषेऽपि तत्संयोगविशेषतः ॥ ६ ॥ मूत्रवर्णादिभेद्देन भेदो मेहेषु कल्प्यते ।

Increased quantity and turbidity of urine arc the characteristic features. Due to the combination of dosas and dusyas; different colours are seen in the urine and pramehas ( diabetes ) are classified depending upon the colour of the urine and
etc. ( 6, 7 ) etc. ( 6-7 )

अच्छं बहु सितं शीतं निर्गन्धमुद्कोपमम् ॥ ७ ॥ मेहत्युदकमेहेन किंचिदाविलपिच्छिम् । इक्षो रसमिवात्यर्थं मधुरं चेश्वुमेहतः ।। ८ ।। सान्द्रीभवेत् पर्युषितं सान्द्रमेहेन मेहति । सुरामेही सुरातुरुयमुपर्यच्छमधो घनम् ।। ९ ॥ संदृष्टरोमा पिष्टेन पिष्टवद्रहुलं सितम् । शुक्राभं शुक्रमिश्रं वा शुक्रमेही प्रमेहति ।। १० ।। सूर्ताणून सिकतामेही सिकतारूपिणो मल्रान् । श्रीतमेडी सुबहुशो मधुरु भृशशीतत्त्रम् ।। ११ ॥ शनैः शनैः शनैमेही मन्दं मन्दं प्रमेहति । लालातन्तुयुतं मूत्रं मूत्रं लालामेहेन पिच्छिलम् ॥ १२ ॥ ( वा. नि. अ. १० )

Clear, copious, white, cold, odourless, with little turbidity and greesiness, almost resembling water are the features of urine in Udakameha.

Urine, very sweet like the juice of sugarcane is found in Iksumeha.

Urine kept undisturbed for sometime in a pot assuming thickness is seen in Sāndrameha.

In Surameha the urine resembles sura (alcoliolic beverage prepared with flour ) with clear fluid on top and sediments at the bottom.

In Pistameha, the urine appears thick as though mixed with flour and is white in colour, the patient experiences horripulations often.

In Śukrameha, the urine resembles semen or even be mixed with it.

In Sikatameha, the urine contains small particles resembling sand.

In Sitameha, the urine is copious, swect in taste and very cold to touch.

In Sanairmeha, the elimination of urine is very frequent and very slow.

In Lalameha, the urine resembles saliva with appearance of threads and is sticky.

( These ten varities are predominantly kaphaja. ) ( 7-12 )

# गन्धवर्णरसस्पर्शः क्षारेण क्षारतोयवस् । नोल्हमेहेन नीलाभं कालमेही मसीनिभम् ॥ १३ ॥ हारिद्रमेही कटुकं हरिद्रासंनिभं दृहत् । वित्नं माञ्जिष्ठमेहेन मञ्जिष्ठासलिलोपमम् ॥ १४ ॥ विस्रमुध्णं सल्क्वणं रक्ताभं रक्तमेहृतः ।

In Ksarameha, the urine resembles solution of alkali in smell, colour, taste and touch.

In Nilameha it is blue, in Kālameha it is like charcoal.

In Haridrameha, the urine is pungent and deep yellow and the patient experiences burning sensation during urination.

In Mānjisthameha, the urine is foul smelling and resembles decoction of mañjistha ( Rubia cardifolia ).

In Raktameha the urine is having foul smell, hot, salty taste and red in colour like that of blood. ( 13-14 )

( The above six varities are predominantly pittaja. )

# वसामेही वसामिश्रं वसामं मूत्रयेन् मूहः ।। १५ ।। मज्जामं मज्जमिश्रं वा मजमेद्वी मुहुमुंहुः । कथायं मधुरं रूक्षं क्षौद्रमेहं वदेह्वधः ॥ १६ ॥ इस्ती मत्त इवाजस्तं मूत्रं वेगविवर्जितम् । सलसीकं विबद्धं व हस्तिमेही प्रमेद्दति ।। १७ ।। ( वा. नि. अ. १० )

In Vasameha, the urine is mixed with muscle fat or appears like that and is voided frequently.

In Majjāmeha, the urine is mixed with marrow or appears like that and is voided frequently.

In Ksaudrameha, the urine looks like a decoction, is sweet, and non-sticky.

In Hastimeha, the person passes urine continuously like an elephant in heat, slowly ( without pressure ) and it is mixed with lasīkā ( tissue fluid ).

( The above four varities are predominantly vātaja. ) ( 15-17 )

# अविपाकोऽरुचिष्ठविनिद्र। कासः सर्वानसः । उपद्रवाः प्रजायन्ते मेहानां कफजन्मनाम् ॥ १८ ॥

Indigestion, loss of appetite, vomitting, too much of sleep, cough and running in the nose are the complications during the course of Kaphaja pramehas. ( 18 )

# बस्तिमेहनयोस्तोदो मुक्ताबदरणं ज्वरः । दृश्द्दस्तुष्णाऽस्लिका मूच्छा विडमेदः पित्तजन्मनाम् ॥ १९ ॥

Pain in the urinary bladder and penis, cracks in the skin of the scrotum, fever, feeling of burning sensation, thirst, sour erectations, faintings and diarrhoea are the complications of Pittaja pramehas. ( 19 )

> बातजानामुदावर्तः कम्पद्धद्वद्रद्युद्वद्यक्त्रेलताः । मूल्क्स्विद्युता शोधः कार्सः श्वास्तव्य जायसे ।। २० ।।

In Vātaja pramehas, movement in upward direction inside the abdomen ( reverse peristalisis ), tremors, heartache, desire for eatables of all tastes, pain ( in the bladder and penis }, loss of sleep, emaciation, cough and increased breathing are the complications. ( 20 )

# यथोक्तोपद्भवाविष्टमतिप्रस्तमेव च ।

#### पिडकापीडितं गाढः प्रमेहो हन्ति मानवम् ॥ २१ ॥ ( स्. सू अ. ३३ )

Pramehas associated with the above complications, with elimination of very large quantities of urine and appearance of tubercles ( or ulcers ) on the body are going to kill the patient.( 21 )

## जातः प्रमेही मधुमेहिनो वा न साध्य उक्तः स हि बीजुदोषात् । ये चापिकेचित्कुल जा विकारा भवन्ति तांस्तान् प्रवद्दन्त्यसाध्यान् ॥ २२ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. ६ )

A person who is born with prameba ( heriditory diabetes ) or a person who is suffering from madhumcha are said to h.: incurable because of defect in genesis itself before their birth. Likewise, many other heriditory diseases also are said to be incurable. ( 22 )

#### सर्वे पत्र प्रमेहास्तु कालेनावरिणः ।

# मधुमेद्दरवमायानित तदाऽसाध्या भवन्ति हि ।। २३ ।। मधुमेहे मधुसमं जायते स किल्र द्विधा । कुद्धे धातुक्षयाद्वायौ दोषावृतपथेऽथवा ॥ २४ ॥

All varieties of prameha, if not treated in time, will ultimately become madhumeha which is incurable. In Madhumeha, the urine is like honey ( sweet in taste ) and it is of two kinds, viz., one due to vāta vrddhi caused by dhātu kṣaya ( depletion of tissues ) and another by vata vrddhi caused by obstruction of the channels of vata by the other dogas. ( 23-24 )

# आवृतो दोषन्त्रिक्षानि सोऽनिमित्तं प्रदर्शयन् । क्षणात्क्षणः भजते कृच्छूसाध्यताम् ॥ २५ ॥ मधुरं यच्च मेहेषु प्रायो मध्यव मेहति ।

( वा. नि. अ. १० ) सर्वऽपि मधुमेद्दाख्या माधुर्याच्याच्याच्या तनोरतः ॥ २६ ॥

In case of obstruction of channels, the symptoms of Josas appear sometimes mild and sometimes severe. Any variety of prameha in which the urine is sweet like honey and the whole body is also sweet, it is to be named as Madhumeha itself. ( 25-26 )

# शराविका कच्छपिका जालिनी विनताऽलजी । मसूरिका सर्वापिका पुत्रिणी सर्वदारिका ॥ २० ॥ विद्रधिश्चिति पिडकाः प्रमेहोपेक्षया दश ।

स्सन्धर्मर्मसु जायन्ते मांसलेपु च धामसु ॥ २८ ॥ ( वा. नि. अ. १० )

Šarāvikā, kacchapikā, jālini, vinatā, alajī, masūrikā, saršapikā, putriņī, vidārikā and vidradhika are the ten types of tubercles ( corbuncles ) in relation to pramehas. They appear on joints, vital parts and other fleshy parts of the body. ( 27-28 )

im 100

#### Mādhava Nidānam

अन्तोष्ठता तु तद्रूपा निम्नमध्या शराविका । गौरसर्षपसंस्थाना तत्प्रमाणा च सर्वपी ॥ २९ ॥ सदाहा कूर्मसंस्थाना ज्ञेया कव्छविका दुधैः । जालिनी तीवदाहा तु मांसजालसमावृता ।। ३० ।। अवगढरुजाक्लेदा पृष्ठे वाऽप्युदरेऽपि वा । महती पिडका नोला विनता नाम सा स्मृता ॥ ३१ ॥ महत्यल्पाचिता ज्ञेया पिडका चापि पुत्रिणी । मसूराकृतिसंस्थाना विश्लेया तु मसूरिका ॥ ३२ ॥ रक्ता सिता स्कोटचिता दारुणा त्वलजी भवेत् । विद्यरीकन्दवद्वत्ता कठिना च विद्शरिका ॥ ३३ ॥ विद्रधेर्नक्षणैर्युक्ता श्रेया विद्र्श्यका तु सा । ये यन्मयाः स्मृता मेहास्तेषामेतास्तु तन्मयाः ॥ ३४ ॥ ( सु. नि. अ. ६ )

Sarāvikā resemble a saucer with elevated edges and depressed centre; sarsapikā is similar to white mustard seeds in colour and size; kacchapikā resembles a tortoise shell and causes sensation of burning; jalini is shaped like a seive of muscle fibres and will have severe burning sensation.

Vinata appears on the back or abdomen, with hard, blue, big sized tubercles associated with pain and exu lation.

Putrinī is a bigger ulcer with small ulcers spread around.

Masūrikā is ulcer in the shape and size of masūra dāla ( pea ), Alajī is red or white in colour, bursts out and very dreadful; Vidarika is round and hard like tuber of vidari.

Vidradhikā will be having all the features of an abscess.

These ulcers are also said to be caused by the same dosas which cause a particular variety of prameha ( 29-34 )

# विना प्रमेद्दमप्येता ज्ञायन्ते दुष्ट्रमेदसः। तावच्येता न लक्ष्यन्ते यावद्धास्तुपरिद्रहः ।। ३५ ।। ( च. सू. अ. १३ )

These ulcers might occur, even without pramelia ( diabetes ) in persons in whom the medas ( fat/adipose tissue ) has undergone abnormal change; but it cannot be recognised till it gets localised in specificd parts. ( 35 )

> गुद्दे हृदि शिरस्यंसे प्रष्ठे मर्मसु चोन्धिताः । सोपद्र्वा दुर्बलाग्नेः पिडकाः परिवर्जयेन् ।। ३६ ॥ इति थीमाधवकरविरचिते मात्रवनिदाने प्रमेहप्रमेहपिडकानिदानं समाप्तम ।। ३३ ।।

Patients who have developed these ulcers in the rectum, "region of the heart, head, shoulders, back and vital parts of the body, associated with complications and who have poor digestive capacity are to be refused treatment. ( 36 )

Thus ends the chapter on Prameha.

# C 'APTER 34 अथ मेदरोगनिदानम्

#### MEDA ROGA NIDANAM ( OBESITY )

अध्यायामदिवास्वप्नरुष्ठेष्मलाहारसेविनः । मधुरोऽन्नरसः प्रायः स्न्रेहान्मेद्ः प्रवर्धयेत् ।। १ ॥ मेदस्ताऽऽवृतमार्गत्वात् पुष्यन्त्यन्ये नधातवः । मेदस्तु चीयते तस्माद्र्शक्तः सर्वकर्मस्त्र् ॥ २ ॥ श्चुद्रश्वासतृष्ठामोद्दस्वप्रकथनसादनैः । युक्तः श्रुत्स्वेद्दशैर्गन्ध्यैरत्वप्राणोऽल्पमैथुनः ।। ३ ॥ मेदस्तु सर्वभूतानामुदरेखस्थिपु स्थितम् । अत प्रवोदरे वर्न्द्रः प्रायो मेदस्विनो भवेत् ॥ ४ ॥ मेद्साऽऽवृतमार्गत्वाद्वायुः कोष्ठे विशेषतः । चरन् सन्धुक्षयत्यन्निमाहारं शोपयत्यपि ।। ५ ॥ तस्मात् स शोध्यं जर्यस्याहारमानिकाङ्कृति । विकारांध्याप्रुते घोरान् कांध्यित् कालब्धतिकमात् ॥ ६ ॥ पतानुपद्रवकरौ विशेषादृश्निमारुतौ । एतौ तु दहतः स्थूलं बनदावो वनं यथा ॥ ७ ॥ मेदस्यतीय संवृद्धे सहस्वैवानिलादयः । विकारान् द्रारुणान् कृत्वा नाशयन्त्याशु जीवितम् ॥ ८ ॥

Absence of physical activity, sleeping during day, and intake of foods which increase kapha, make the end product of digestion to become ( abnormally ) sweet which in turn causes increase of medas ( fat ). This obstructs the nutrient channels of the remaining tissues depriving them of nutrition. So only fat accumulates in large quantities in the body making the person incapable of all activities. Difficulty in breathing even on slight exertion, thirst, delusion, sleep, sudden catching of breath, exhaustion, excessive hunger, bad smell of the body and poor physical and sexual capacity gradually develop. As the abdomen and bones are the chief depots of fat, the abdomen gets enlarged in such persons. The channels of vata become obstructed by the increased fat inside the abdomen; vata then begins to act fast inside the abdomen, increases the digestive activity, making for voracious hunger and craving for large quantity of food. In course of time many more distressing symptoms develop. Agni ( gastric fire ) and vata together are very harmful. Just as the forest fire destroys the forest, these destroy the body. With the increase of fat, the three dosas also undergo increase, produce many severe complications and even loss of life quickly. ( 1-8 )

मेदोमांसातिवृद्धत्वाचलस्क्रियागुदरस्तनः । अयधोपचयोत्साहो नरोऽतिस्थूल उच्यते ।।९।। (च. मु. अ. २१) इति थीमाधवकरविरविदति माधवनिदाने मेदोनिदानं समाप्तम् ॥ ३४ ॥

A person is said to be very obese when his buttocks, abdomen and breasts begin to show movement ( during activity ) due to accumulation of fat in those places and when his body build and enthusiasm are found to be disproportionate ( to his age ). ( 9 )

Thus ends the chapter on Meda roga.

# अधोदरनिदानम्

## UDARA NIDANAM ( ENLARGEMENT OF ABDOMEN )

रोगाः सर्वेऽपि मन्देऽग्नौ सुतरामुद्र्राणि च । अजीर्णान्मलिनैश्चान्ते मलसंचयात् ॥१॥ (वा.नि.अ. १२)

All diseases are produced by mandagni ( poor digestive activity ) specially so the Udara roga ( enlargement of the abdomen ). It occurs duc to indulgence in uncooked, dirty or spoiled food and accumulation of waste products in the body. ( 1 )

# रुद्ध्वा स्वेदाम्युवाहीनि दोषाः स्नोतांसि संचिताः ।

## प्राणाग्न्यपानान् संदूध्य जनयन्त्युद्रं नृणाम् ॥ २ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. २३ )

The dosas undergoing increase, obstruct the channels of sweat and other body fluids, excite prana, apana and agni ( pitta ) and produce Udara roga ( enlargement of abdomen ). ( 2 )

# आध्यानं गमनेऽदाक्तिदूँ बेल्यं दुर्बलाग्निता । शोथः सदनमङ्गानां सक्लो वातपुरीषयोः ।। ३ ।। दाहस्तन्द्रा च सर्वेषु जठरेषु भवन्ति हि ।

Distention of the abdomen with gas, inability to walk, weakness, poor digestion, inactivity, ocdema and debility of the extrimities, obstructed movement of flatus and feces, feeling of burning sensation and stupor are generally seen in all types of udara ( 3-4 )

# प्रथम्दोष्टः समस्त्रैश्च प्रतिहवद्धस्तोदकैः ॥ ४ ॥ संभवन्त्युदराण्यघ्यौ तेषांलिङ्गं पृथक् श्टणु ।

Udara is of eight kinds; one from each dosa, one from their combination; plihodara, baddhodara, ksatodara and udakodara; the symptoms of each will be described separately further on. ( 4 )

> तत्र वातोदरे शोथः पाणिपान्नाभिकुक्षिपु ॥ ५ ॥ कुक्षिपाश्वोंदरकटीपृष्ठ रुक् पर्वभेदनम् । शुष्ककासोऽङ्गमदोऽधोगुरुता मलुखंग्रहः ॥ ६ ॥

# द्याचारुणत्वगादित्यमकस्माद्धद्वहासवत् । सतोदभेदभुदरं तनुकृष्णसिराततम् ॥ ७ ॥ आध्यातदतिवच्छन्दमाहतं प्रकरोति च । वायुश्चात्र सरक्ष्शब्दो विचरेस्सर्वतोगतिः ॥ ८ ॥ (त्रा.नि.अ. १२)

In Vatodara the symptoms are, oedema of the hands, feet, umbilicus and lower abdomen; pain in the abdomen, flanks, waist, back and joints; dry cough, bodyaches, feeling of heaviness of the lower parts of the body, accumulation of wastes products in the body, bluish or brownish discolouration of skin, etc., increase

#### Ch. 35, Enlargement of abdomen

or decrease of size of the abdomen without any apparent reason, pricking and other types of pain, appearance of thin, black network of veins on the abdomen, highly resonant sound elicited on percussion and movement of gas with gurgling noise and pain inside the abdomen. ( 5-8 )

# पित्तोदरे ज्वरो मूच्छा दाइस्छट् कटुकास्यता । भ्रमोऽतिसारः पीतत्वं त्वगादाबुदरं हरित् ॥ ९ ॥ पीततास्रसिरानद्धं सस्वेदं सोष्म द्वते । धूमायते मृदुस्पर्शं क्षिप्रपाकं प्रदूयते ॥ १०॥ ( वा. नि. अ. १२ )

In Pittodara, there will be fever, fainting, feeling of burning sensation, thirst, bitter taste in the mouth, giddiness, diarrhoea, yellow discolouration of skin, etc., appearance of network of veins of green, yellow or red colour on the abdomen, perspiration, feeling of heat and burning as though by movement of hot smoke; abdomen soft to touch and painful. The condition is quickly progressive ( to end up as Jalodara ). ( 9-10 )

# श्लेष्मोदरेऽङ्गसदर्नं स्वापः श्वयथुगौरवम् । निद्रोत्कुशोऽर्सचिः श्वासः कासः शुरूलवरादिता ॥ ११ ॥ उदरं स्तिमितं स्निम्धं शुक्लराजीततं महत् । चिराभिदृद्धं कठिनं शीतस्पर्शं गुरु स्थिरम् ॥ १२ ॥

The symptoms of Kaphodara are debility, loss of activity, oedema and heaviness of body parts, too much of sleep, nausa, loss of appetite, difficulty in breathing, cough, whiteness of the skin, etc. . The abdomen is tense, soft, having white striae, large in size, increasing slowly, hard, cold to touch, heavy and without movement. ( 11-12 )

यस्मै प्रयच्छन्त्यरयो गरांश्च दुष्टाम्बुद्र्ष्यीविषसेवनाद्या ॥ १३ ॥

Ingestion of harmful substances containing nails, hairs, urine, feces, menstrual blood, etc., administered by wicked women along with food and drink to their foes; ingestion of polluted water, slow poisons etc., tend to increase the dosas which in turn vitiate rakta ( blood ) and produce a severe form of udara roga having the symptoms of all the three dosas together. The condition exacerbates on days of cold breeze and over-cast clouds, the patient feels severe burning sensation inside, faints frequently, is pale, emaciated, and suffers from severe thirst. This condition is called as Dūsyodara also. ( 13-14 )

प्रीहोदरं कीर्तयतो निबोध ।। १५ । विदाह्यभिष्यन्दिरतस्य जन्तोः प्रदुष्टमत्यर्थमस्त्रक् कफश्च । प्रीहाभिवृद्धि कुरुतः प्रवृद्धौ प्रीहोत्थमेतज्जठरं वदन्ति ।। १६ ।। तद्वामपार्श्वे परिवृद्धिमेति विशेषतः सीदृति चातुरोऽध्र । मन्दञ्चराग्निः कफपित्तलिङ्ग्रेरुपद्रतः क्षीणबलोऽति घाण्डुः ।

Understand the symptoms of Plihodara further-in persons who indulge in foods which produce burning sensation during digestion and which block the tissue pores by greasy material, the rakta ( blood ) and kapha undergo abnormal changes, cause the cnlargement of spleen and produce Plihodara ( splenomegaly ). Abdomen is enlarged more on the left side, patient is very debilitated, with mild fever, poor digestion, symptoms of increased kapha and pitta, loss of strength and severe anacmia. ( 15-16 )

स्वयान्यपार्श्वे यकृति प्रवृद्धे श्रेयं यक्द्दाल्युद्दरं तदेव ॥ १७ ॥

If there is such an enlargement of the liver on the right side, the disease is to be named as Yakrtodara ( hepatomegaly ). ( 17 )

उद्गवर्तरुजानाहैमोहन्द्रद्दनन्वरः । गौरवार्सचिकाठिन्यैविद्यात्तत्र मलान् कमात् ॥१८॥ (वा. नि. अ. १२)

In both these conditions, symptoms of vata such as upward movement, pain and distention of abdomen with gas; symptoms of pitta such as delusion, thirst, feelallu distention of abuletter and fever; symptoms of kapha such as fecling of heaviness, loss of appetite and hardness of the abdomen are to be noticed. ( 18 )

# यस्यान्त्रमन्त्ररुपलेपिभिवां बालाश्मभिवां पिहितं यथावत् । संचीयते तस्य मलः सदोषः शनैः शनैः संकरवच्च नाड्याम् ॥ १९ ॥ निरुध्यते तस्य गुद्दे पुरीषं निरेति कृच्छ्राद्रपि चाल्पमरूपम् । हृत्नाभिमध्ये परिवृद्धिमेति तस्योदरं बद्धगुद्दं वद्दन्ति ॥ २० ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. ७ )

Obstruction of the intestines by food materials, hairs, stones, etc., make for accumulation of wastes ( feces ) inside the intestines. The feces is totally obstructed accumulation of wastes ( reces ) nith great difficulty, abdomen is enlarged between or enminated in billish polition is called as Baddhagudodara ( intestinal obstruction ). ( 19-20 )

श्ल्यं तथाऽन्नोपहितं यदन्त्रं भुक्तं मिनत्यागतमन्यथा वा । तस्मात्स्त्रतोऽन्त्रात्सलिल्प्रकाशः स्वावः स्ववेद्वै गुद्तस्तु भूयः ॥ २१ ॥ नाभेरधश्चोदरमेति वृद्धि निस्तुद्यते दाल्यति चातिमात्रम् । ( मु. निः अ. ७ ) पतत्परिस्नाव्युदरं प्रदिष्टं,

Perforation of the intestines by sharp foreign bodies ingested along with food, I croration of the of ther cause, makes for exudation of watery material from inside producing enlargement of the abdomen below the umbilicus; with pricking pain and, apparent fluid movement. This condition is known as Parisravi udara ( intestinal perforation and peritonitis ). ( 21-22 )

> दकोदर्र कीर्तयतो निबोध ।। १२ ।। यः स्नेह्रपीतोऽप्यनुवासितो वा वान्तो विरिकोऽप्यथवा निरूढः । पिवेज्जलं शीतलमाश्र तस्य स्त्रोतांसि दृष्यान्ति हि तद्वहानि ॥ २३ ॥ स्त्रेहोपलिष्ठ्यवाऽपि तेषु दकोदरं पूर्ववदश्युपैति । स्त्रिग्धं महत्तत्परिवृत्तनाभि समाततं पूर्णमिवाम्बुना च । यथा द्वतिः क्षुभ्यति कम्पते च शब्दायते चापि दकोदरं तत् ॥ २४ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. ७ )

Understand the symptoms of Dakodara now-drinking of cold water by the patient who has been administered therapics like oleation, oil enema, emesis, purgation or decoction enema; ( or by any other cause also ), the channels of fluids in the body get obstructed by oily material and lead on to the production of dakodara. The abdomen is greatly enlarged with the umbilicus protruding out, smooth and hard to touch, producing sound and movement like a leather bag filled with water. This condition is known as Dakodara ( iscitis, enlargement of the abdomen with fluid ). ( 23-24 )

# जन्मनैवोदरं सर्वे प्रायः क्रच्छतमं मतम् । बलिनस्तद्जाताम्यु यत्तस्राध्यं नवोत्थितम् ॥ २६ ॥ पक्षाद्वद्धगुदं तुध्वं सर्वं जातोदकं तथा । प्रायो भवत्यभावाय छिद्रान्त्रं चोदरं नुणाम् ॥२६॥ (च. चि. अ. १८)

Generally all kinds of udaras are, even at their onset, difficult to cure. In persons of strong constitution, before formation of fluid and of recent onset it can be cured with great effort. Baddhagudodara after a lapsc of a fortnight, all other types which have progressed into the stage of accumulation of fluid, and that in which the intestines are perforated-are going to be fatal. ( 25-27 )

# श्रानाक्षं कुटिलोपस्थमूपक्किन्नतनुत्वचम् । बल्ठशोणितमांसाम्निपरिक्षीणं च वजयेत् ॥२७॥ ( च. चि. १८ ) पार्श्वभूक्षश्रविद्वेषशोथातीसारपीडितम् । विरिक्तं चाण्युदरिणं पूर्यमाणं विवर्जयेत् ॥२८॥ (मु. सू. अ. ३४)

इनि श्रीमाधवकरविरर्विते माध्रवनिदाने उदरनिदानं ममाप्तम् ॥ ३५ ॥

Thus ends the chapter on Udara.

Patients of udara who have developed oedema of the eyes, curvature of the penis, smooth and thin skin, loss of strength, loos of blood, muscles and digestive capacity are to be refused treatment. Similarly also those who have pricking pain in the flanks, aversion to food, oedema, diarrhoea and fluid accumulating quickly inspite of purgations are to be rejected. ( 27-28 )

# अथ शोधनिदानम्

# ŠOTHA NIDĀNAM ( OEDEMA, ANASARCA )

# रक्तपित्तकफान् वायुर्दुष्टो दुष्टान् बहिःसिराः । नीत्वा रुद्धगतिस्तौद्धि कुर्यान्त्रस्र्यम् ॥ १ ॥ उत्सेधं संद्वतं शोधं तमाहुनिंच्यादतः । सर्वे हेतुविशेषैस्तु रूपमेदान्नवात्मकम् ॥ २ ॥ दोषैः पृथग्द्वयैः सर्वैरभिघाताद्विषादपि ।

Vata undergoing increase pushes out the increased rakta ( blood ), pitta and kapha to the extcrior ( skin ) by blocking their channels and produces swelling of the skin and muscles. It is called as utsedha, samhata and sotha in view of its increased size. Depending upon the characteristic features of dosas it is classified into nine kinds-one from each dosa, one each from the combination of two dosas, one from the combination of all three dosas together, one from injury and one from poison. ( 1-2 )

तत्पूर्वरूपं दवथुः सिरायामोऽङ्गगौरवम् ॥ ३॥

Its premonitory symptoms are mild burning sensation of the skin ( eyes, nose, etc. ), appearance of venous network ( engorgement of veins ) and feeling of heaviness of the body parts. ( 3 )

> शुद्ध्यामयाभुक्तकृशाबलानां क्षाराम्हतीक्ष्णोष्णगरूपसेन्ना । दध्यामम्मूच्छाकविरोधिद्धार्यारोपस्युमन्ननिषेवणं च । ४ ॥ अर्शास्यचेष्ठा न च देहश्चिमर्मोपधातो विषमा प्रस्तिः । मिथ्योपचारः प्रतिकर्मणां च निजस्य हेतुः श्वयथोः प्रदिष्ठः ॥ ५ ॥

Improper management of different kinds of purificatory therapies, effects of diseases; lack of food, etc., will make men emaciated and weak. Such weak persons indulging in foods which are alkaline, sour, heat producing, hot, hard to digest, curds, uncooked foods, mud, leafy vegetables, incompatable recipes, spoilt and poisoned foods; presence of haemorrhoids, absence of physical activity, not undergoing purificatory measures for long periods of time, disorders of vital organs of the body, abnormal delivery in women and improper administration of purificatory therapies are the causes for systemic oedema. ( 4-5 )

# संगौरवं स्यादनवस्थितत्वं सोल्सेधमूष्माऽध्य सिरातनुत्वम् । सलोमद्दर्श्व विवर्णता च सामान्यलिङ्गं श्वयथोः प्रदिष्टम् ॥ ६ ॥

Swelling associated with fecling of heaviness, increasing or decreasing frequently, warmth, thinning of the veins, hairs standing on ends, discolouration are the general symptoms of ocdema. ( 6 )

# चलस्तनुत्वक् परुषोऽरुणोऽस्ितः सुषुपुतिहर्षार्तियुतोऽनिमित्ततः । प्रशास्यति प्रोस्नमति प्रयीडितो दिवाबली च श्वयथुः समीरणात् ॥ ७ ॥

Skin on the oedematous part being movable, thin, rough, brown or black in colour, insensitive or with a feeling of pins and needles inside; oedema subsiding temporarily without any apparent cause, pitting on pressure and the pit filling up quickly, increasing during day time are the symptoms of Vataja sotha ( ocdema due to increase of vāta ). ( 7 )

## मृद्धः सगन्धोऽसितपीतरागवान् भ्रमञ्जरस्वेदनृषामदान्वितः । य उष्यते स्वष्टरुगक्षिरागकृत् स पित्तशोधो भृशदाहपाकवान् ॥ ८ ॥

Skin on the oedematous part being soft, emitting some smell; black, yellow or red in colour; the patient having giddiness, fever, perspiration, thirst, toxicity, severe burning sensation in the affected part, redness of the eyes and oedema forming into an ulcer quickly are the symptoms of Pittasotha ( oedema caused by increased pitta ). ( 8 )

# ग्रुः स्थिरः पाण्डररोचकान्वितः प्रसेकनिद्रावमिवहिमान्द्यक्तु । स कृञ्छजन्मप्रशमो निपीडितो न चोन्नमेद्राविबल्डी कफात्मकः ॥ ९ ॥

The oedematous part being heavy and immovable; the patient having pallor, loss of appetite, too much of salvation, sleep, vomitting and poor digestion; swelling very slow to manifest and to subside, pitting on pressure and the pit not filling up and swelling profound during nights are the symptoms of Kaphaja sotha ( oedema due to increase of kapha ). ( 9 )

# निदानाकृतिसंसर्गान्छ्ययथुः स्यादिद्वदोषज्ञः ।

सर्वाकृतिः सन्निपाताच्छोथो व्यामिथ्रस्त्रक्षणः ॥ १० ॥

Presence of symptoms of iwo dosas and of all the three dosas together are to be noticed in dvidosaja and sannipātaja šothas respectively. ( 10 )

# अभिधातेन शख्यादिच्छेदभेदक्षतादिभिः । हिमानिलोद्द्ध्यनिलैर्मलुप्तकापिकच्छुजैः ॥ ११ ॥ रसँ: शूक्रैश्च संस्पर्शान्छ्ययथुः स्याद्विसर्पवान् ।

भृशोष्मा लोहिताभासः प्रायशः पित्तलक्ष्णः ॥ १२ ॥ ( वा. नि. अ. १३ )

Assault by weapons causing cutting, breaking or tearing of the parts, assault by frost, breeze, cyclone; contact with fumes or oil of bhallātaka ( marking nut ) and fine thorns of the pod of kapikacchu ( mucuna pruriens ), irritating juices of herbs, etc., bring about an oedema which spreads quickly, associated with severe burning sensation, redness and other symptoms of pittavrddhi. ( 11-12 )

विषजः संविषयाणिपरिसर्वणम् त्रणातु । दृष्ट्रादन्तनस्वाधारादविषयाणिनामपि ।। १३ ॥ विण्मूत्रशुकोपहतमलबद्धस्त्रसंकरात् । विषदृक्षानिल्त्रस्पर्शाद्ररयोगावचूर्णनात् ॥ १४ ॥ मृदुश्वलोऽवलम्बी च शोधो दाहरुजाकरः । ( वा. नि. अ. १३ )

#### Mādhava Nidānam

Poisonous animals crawling on the body, urinating, stinging, biting with teeth, scratching with nails; contact with feces, urine, semen or coverings of even nonpoisonous animals; contaminated cloth, poisonous trees, air ( gases ) and artificial poisonous materjals also produce oedema. The skin over she swollen part is soft, moyeble, the part is seen drooping downward with severe burning sensation' and pain manifesting quickly. ( 13-14 )

दोषाः श्वयथुमुध्यं हि कुर्वन्त्यामाशयस्थिताः ।। १५ ।। पक्काशयस्था मध्ये तु वर्षः स्थानगतास्त्वः । कृत्स्रदेहमनुप्राप्ताः कुर्युः सर्वसरं तथा ।।१६॥ (मू.चि.अ. २३)

Dosas residing in the stomach produce oedema in the upper part of the body, residing in the large intestinc they produce oedema in the trunk, residing in the rectum they produce oedema in the lower parts and if they are circulating all over the body they produce ocdema every where. ( 15-16 )

यो सध्यदेशे श्वयुक्तः स कष्टः सर्वगश्च यः । अर्धाङ्गे रिप्टभूतः स्याद्यश्चे परिसर्पति ॥ १७॥ श्वासः पिपासा छदिंश्व दौर्वेल्यं ज्वर एव च । यस्य चात्रै रुचिनीस्ति श्वयगुं तं विवर्जयेत् ।।१८।।(सु.चि.अ.२३) अनन्योपद्मचकृतः शोथः पादसम्बन्धिनः । पुरुषं हन्ति नारीं च मुखजो ग्रह्युज्ञो द्वयम् । नवोऽन्तपद्वः शोधः साध्योऽस्मध्यः पूर्ररितः ॥ १९ ॥ विवर्जयेत्कक्ष्यदराश्रितं च यथा गलै मर्मणि संधितं च । स्थूल्रः खरश्र्यापि भवेद्विवर्ण्यो यश्चापि वालस्थविरावलानां ॥ २० ॥

इनि श्रीमाध्यकरविरचित्रे माधवनिद्याले लोयनिद्यानं समाप्तम् ॥ ३३ ॥

Oedema manifesting in the trunk, that which moves to all the parts, that which presents fatal signs, oedema seen only in half of the body, that which spreads upwards are to be considered as fatal. Patients of oedema who have increased/difficult respiration, severe thirst, vomiting, debility, fever, lack of desire for food are to be refused treatment.

Oedema not produced by its own specific causes, that appearing in the feet in men and in the face in women, that appearing in genital organs in both are going to be fatal.

Oedema which is of recent onset, having no complications, is curable but not others.

The patient who has oedema in his abdomen, neck and vital organs; oedema which is very big in size and hard and that affecting children, the very aged and women are also to be rejected for treatment. ( 17-20 )

Thus ends the chapter on Sotha.

# अथो वृद्धिनिद्दानम्

# VRDDHI NIDANAM ( SWELLINGS OF THE SCROTUM )

# द्युद्धोऽनूर्ध्वगतिर्वायुः शोथशूल्क रूश्चरन् । मुध्कौ वङ्कुणतः प्राप्य फलक्रोपाभिवाहिनीः ।। १ ।। प्रपीड्य धमनीर्वृद्धि करोति फल्क्रीषयोः । दोषास्त्रमेदोमूत्रान्त्रैः स वृद्धिः सत्रधा गद्ः ॥ २ ॥ मूधान्त्रज्ञावर्ष्यनिलाउनुभेदस्तु केवलम् । ( वा. नि. अ. ११ )

Vata undergoing increase, makes a downward movement to the groins and scrotum; deranges the vessels there and produces enlargement of the scrotum associated with oedema and pain. This disease known as Vrddhi roga is of seven kinds, one from each of the tridosas, one from rakta ( blood ), one from medas ( fat ), one from mūtra ( urine ) and seventh from the āntras ( intestines ). Even mūtraja and āvtraia are mainly due to vata, though named separately after their causes. ( 1-2 )

# वातपूर्णद्रतिस्पर्शों रूक्षो वातादहेतुरुक् ॥ ३ ॥ पकोदुम्बरसंकाशः पित्ताहाहोष्मपाकवान् । कफाञ्छीतो गुरुः स्निभ्धः कण्डूमान् कडिनोऽल्परुक् ॥ ४॥ कृष्णस्कोटावृतः पित्तवृद्धिलिङ्गश्च रक्तजः । कफबन्मेदसा वृद्धिमुदुस्तालफलोपमः ।। ५ ॥ मूत्रधारणशीलस्य मूत्रजः स् तु गच्छतः । अम्भोभिः पूर्णदतिवत् क्षोमं याति सरङङ्मुदुः ।। ६ ॥ मूत्रकृद्ध्रमधः स्याच्च चाल्ठयन् फलकोषयोः । ( (

In vātaja vịddhi, the scrotum resembles a bag filled with air both for sound and touch, is rough and painful without any apparent reason.

In pittaja vrddhi, the scrotum resembles a ripe fruit of udumbara ( wild figh Ficus glomerata ) accompanied with burning sensation, heat and progresses very fast.

In kaphaja type, the scrotum is cold to touch, heavy, smooth, itching, hard and, with very little pain.

Raktaja type will be having all the symptoms of pitta and studded with reddish-black petichae.

Medaja vrddhi will be having all the symptoms of kapha, is soft and appears ( big ) like a tala fruit ( wild palm ).

Mūtraja vrddhi occurs in persons who are in the habit of suppressing the urge of urination. The scrotum is filled with fluid, resembles a water bag producing sound, is painful and soft, micturition also being difficult. ( 3-6 )

वातकोपिभिराहारै: शीततोयावगाहनै: ।।७।। धारणेरणभाराध्वविषमाङ्गप्रवर्तनैः । क्षोभणैः क्षोभितोऽन्यैश्च क्षुद्रान्त्रावयवं यदा । १८।। पवनो विगुणीकृत्य स्वनिवेशादधो नयेत् । कुर्याद्वङ्कणसन्धिस्थो ग्रन्थ्याभं श्वयथुं तदा ।। ९ ।। उपेक्षमाणस्य च भुष्कवृद्धिमाध्मानरूक्स्तम्भवतीं सं वायुः । प्रपीडितोऽन्तः स्वनवान् प्रयाति प्रध्मापयन्नेति पुनश्च मुक्तः ।। १० ।। अन्त्रवृद्धिरसाध्योऽयं वातवृद्धिसमाकृतिः । (वा. नि. अ. ११)

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने वृद्धिनिदानं समाप्तम् ।।३७।।

Indulgence in foods which increase vata, sitting inside cold water for long hours, suppressing the urges of the body or initiating them prematurely, carrying heavy loads, walking long distances, doing acrobatics and such other tiresome activities, make for increase of vāta which in turn brings down the small intestines into the groin producing a mass there. If not treated soon, it descends further down into the scrotum causing its enlargement. The scrotum is painful, has no movement, on pressure it (intestine) produces sound, recedes, but comes back again on release of pressure. This antraja vrddhi is incurable and has all the symptons of vātaja vrddhi. (7-11)

Thus ends the chapter on Vrddhi roga.

# अथ गलगण्डगण्डमालापचीग्रन्थ्यर्बुदनिदानम् GALAGAŅDA-GAŅDAMĀLĀ-APACI-GRANTHI-ARBUDA NIDĀNAM (CERVICAL LYMPHADENOMA, SCROFULA-TUMORS, CANCER)

# निबन्धः श्रयथूर्यस्य मुष्कवल्लम्बते गले।

# महान् वा यदि वा ह्रस्वो गलगण्डं तमादिशेत् ।। १।।

Galagaṇḍa is defined as a swelling (tumor) in the neck, big or small hanging like the scrotum. (1)

# वात: कफश्रापि गले प्रदुष्टो मन्ये च संश्रित्य तथैव भेद: ।

# कुर्वन्ति गण्डं क्रमशः स्वलिङ्गैः समन्वितं तं गलगण्डमाहुः ।। २।। (सु. नि. अ. ११)

Vata and kapha undergoing increase bring about changes in the medas (fat) and produce swellings in the neck and its sides. The symptoms of the dosas will manifest gradually. This disease is known as Galaganda. (2)

# वैरस्यमास्यस्य च तस्य जन्तोर्भवेत्तथा तालुगलप्रशोषः । (सु. नि. अ. ११) तोदान्वितः कृष्णसिरावनब्धः श्यावोऽरुणोः वा पवनात्मकस्त् । पारुष्ययुक्तश्चिरवृद्धयाको यदृच्छया पाकमियात्कदाचित् ।। ३। १

Bad taste in the mouth, dryness of the palate and throat, swelling having pricking pain, presence of network of black coloured veins, swelling-blue or red in colour, rough, slow rate of growth, usually not forming pus, but occasionally pus getting formed-are the symptoms of vātaja galaganda. (3)

स्थिर सवर्णो गुरुरुग्रकण्डू: शीतो महांश्रापि कफात्मकस्तु ।। ४।। चिराभिवृद्धिं भजते चिराद्वा प्रपच्यते मन्दरुजः कदाचित् । माधुर्यमास्यस्य च तस्य जन्तोर्भवेत्तथा तालुगलप्रलेपः ।।५।। (सु. नि. अ. ११)

In kaphaja galaganda, the swelling is immovable having the same colour as the skin, heavy, with severe itching, cold to touch, big in size, slow rate of growth, occasionally suppurates slowly, has mild pain; the patient will be having sweet taste in the mouth and furring of the palate and throat. (4-5)

# स्निग्धो गुरू: पाण्डुरनिष्टगन्यो मेदोभव: कण्ड्र्यतोऽल्परूक् च । प्रलम्बतेऽ लाबुवदल्पमुलो देहानुरूपक्षयवन्धियक्तः ।। ६ ।।

स्निग्धास्यता तस्य भवेच्च जन्तोर्गलेऽनुशब्दं कुरुते च नित्यम् । (सु. नि. अ. ११) Medaja galagaṇḍa will have the swelling which is smooth, heavy, yellowishwhite in colour, emits unpleasant smell with itching and mild pain, hangs like an alabu ( pitcher gourd ), attached very losely, undergoes increase or decrease in accordance to that of the body; the patient will have copious salivation and makes cooing sound in the throat. ( 6-7 )

# क्रञ्जाद्धवसन्तं मदसर्वगात्रं संवत्सरातीतमरोचकार्तम् ॥ ७ ॥ क्षीणं च वैद्यो गलगण्डग्रक्तं मिन्नस्वरं चापि विवर्जयेच्च । ( स्. नि. अ. ११ )

The patient of galaganda who has difficulty in breathing, very soft body parts, having it { swelling } for more than a year, with loss of appetite, emaciation and broken voice is to be rejected for treatment. ( 7-8 )

## ऋकेन्धकोलामलकप्रमाणैः कक्षांसमन्यागलवर्ष्वणेषु ॥ ८ ॥ सेदःकफास्यां चिरमन्द्रपाकैः स्याद्गण्डमाला वहभिश्च गण्डैः । ( ग. नि. अ. ११ )

More than one ganda ( swelling ) appearing in the axilla, shoulders, sides of the neck, front of neck or the groin, varying in size from that of karkandhu and kolā ( fruit of jujube ) to that of amalaka ( goose berry, phyllanthus emblica ), having kapha and medas ( fat ), and undergoing suppuration after long period of time is known as Gandamala ( chain of swellings ). ( 8 )

## ते प्रन्थयः केचिदवालपाकाः स्त्रवन्ति नद्यान्तः भवन्ति चान्ये ।। ९ ।। कालगनुवन्धं चिरमादधाति सैवापचीति प्रवद्गन्त तज्ञाः । साध्याः स्मृताः प्रानसपार्श्वश्रृत्कासज्वरच्छदियुतारत्वसाध्याः ॥ १० ॥

Some of the swellings undergoing suppuration, burst, discharge their contents and disappear and new ones appear, this process continuing for long time-such a condition is known Apaci and are curable. But if the patient has running in the nose, pain in the flanks, cough, fever and vomituing it will then become incurable. ( 9-10 )

# वातादयो मांसमस्यक प्रदृष्टाः संदृष्य मेदश्य तथा सिराश्च । न्न्तोन्नतं विग्रशितं च शोधं कुर्वन्त्यतो प्रन्थरिति प्रदिष्टः ॥ ११ ॥

Vita and other two dosas undergoing increase, bring about abnormalities in the asrk ( blood ), mamsa ( muscle ), medas ( fat ) and siras ) and produce round shaped big masses known as Granthi ( tumors ). ( 11 )

# आयम्यते व्यवि तद्यते नद्यते च प्रत्यस्यते मध्यति भिद्यते च । क्रमणो मुदुर्वस्त्ररिवाततश्च भिन्नः स्नवेच्यानिलजोऽस्त्रमच्छम् ॥ २२ ॥ (गु. नि. अ. ११)

Symptoms of vataja granthi ( tumors ) are; different types of pain such as expanding ( siretching ), tearing, pricking, pulling, churning and cutting; mass is black in colour, soft, resembles a water bag and exudes thin, pure blood if pricked. ( 12 )

# द्रन्द्युते धूप्यति वृद्द्यते च पापच्यते प्रज्वलतीय चापि । न प्रक्तः सर्वातोऽप्यथवाऽपि पित्ताद्भिन्नः स्नवेदुध्णमतीव चास्त्रम् ॥ १३ ॥ ( गु. नि. अ. ११ )

Symptoms of pittaja granthi are severe burning sensation, feeling as though touched by fire or by strong alkalies; mass is red or yellowish-red in colour and exudes large amount of warm blood if pricked. ( 13 )

# श्रीतोऽविवर्णांऽरुपरुजोऽतिकण्डुः पाषाणवत् संहृननोपपन्नः । शासाठीवृद्धश्यः कफप्रकोपाद्भिन्नः स्ववेच्द्रुबन्धनं च पूर्यम् ॥ १४ ॥ ( गु. नि. अ. ११ )

Symptoms of kaphaja granthi are cold to touch, not having any discolouration, mild pain but severe itching, very hard like stone, slow growth rate and exudes thick white pus, if pricked. ( 14 )

# शरीरवृद्धिक्षयवृद्धिहानिः स्निग्धो महान् कण्डुयुतोऽरुजश्च । मेदः कृतो गच्छति चात्र भिन्ने पिण्याकसर्पिः प्रतिमं तु मेदः ॥ १५ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. ११ )

Medaja granthi will have increase or decrease in size in accordance to that of . Notaga grainen will here ing, painless and if pricked exudes thick fatty material resembling paste of sesame seeds or ghee. ( 15 )

> व्यायामजातैरबलस्य हैस्तैराशिष्य वायुस्तु सिरामतानम् । संकृत्य संपीड्य विशोध्य चापि प्रन्थि करोत्युवनमाशु वृत्तम् ॥ १६ ॥ ग्रन्थिः सिराजः सः तु कृद्धस्यो भवेद्यदि स्यात् सरुजध्यलश्च । श्रीम्यः सिराज्यं संस्कृति महान्द्र मर्मोत्त्वित्रधारि विवर्जनीयः ॥ १७॥ ( मु. नि. अ. ११ )

In persons who are weak, but do strenuous activity, vata un'lergoing, increase invades the network of veins, making them constricted, dried and rolled into a round big mass known as śiraja granthi ( tumor of veins ). It becomes incurable when it becomes painful and moving. If it appears on vital organs, it is to be refused treatment even if it is painless and immobile. ( 16-17 )

> गात्रप्रदेशे कचिदेव दोषाः संमूर्चिछता मांसमस्तुक् प्रदूध्य । वृत्तं स्थिरं मन्द्रुजं महान्तमनल्यमूलं चिरवृद्ध्यपाकम् ॥ १८ ॥ कुर्वन्ति मांसोच्छयमत्यगाधं तदर्युद् शास्त्रविद्यो वद्गित । वातेन पित्तेन कफेन चापि रक्तेन मांसेन च मेदसा वा ॥ १९ ॥ तज्ञायते तस्य च लक्षणानि ग्रन्थेः समानानि सद् भवन्ति । ( सु. नि. अ. ११ )

Dosas undergoing increase, invade the muscle and blood and produce round, immovable, slightly painful, big, decp-scated, slowly increasing, non-suppurating mimovaole, sogarry pannon avgy the learned as Arbuda ( cancer ). They are tumors of mu zanaja, pittaja, kaphaja, raktaja, māmsaja and medaja and cach of of ( six mixts ) www.juppms similar to their respective types of granthis. ( 18-19 )

सास्नावमुन्नह्यति  मांसपिण्डं  मांसाङ्करैराचितमाशुवृद्धम्  ।

Assault on the body by the fist, etc.; makes for derangement in māmsa (muscle tissues) and gives rise to tumor which is painless, smooth, of the same colour (snathe skin, non-suppurative, hard like stone and immovable. This is said to be more common in those who are fond of eating flesh in their food. This disease known as Mănsărbula is also incurable, (22-23)

# साध्येष्वपीमानि त् वर्जयेच्च । संप्रस्नुतं मर्मणि यच्च जातं स्रोतः सु वा यच्च भवेदचाल्यम् ।। २४।। (सु. नि. अ. ११)

# यज्जायतेऽन्यत् खलु पूर्वजाते ज्ञेयं तदध्यर्युदमर्मद्र्वद्र्यः ।

यद्द्वन्द्रजातं युगपत् क्रमाद्वा द्विरर्दुदं तच्च भवेदसाध्यम् ।। २५।। (सु. नि. अ. ११)

A second arbuda developing after the course of an earlier one is called as Adhyarbuda by specialists of arbuda; while a second arbuda developing concurrently with the first one is called Dvirarbuda. Both these are incurable. (25)

# न पाकभायान्ति कफाधिकत्वान्मेदोबहुत्वाच्च विशेषतस्त् । दोषस्थिरत्वाद्युयनाच्च तेषां सर्वार्बुदान्येव निसर्गतस्तु ।। २६।। (सु. नि. अ. ११)

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचित्ते माधवनिदाने गलगण्डगण्डमालापवीग्रन्थ्यर्नुदनिदानं समाप्तम् ॥३८॥

Arbudas do not suppurate in view of predominance of kapha and medas; by the hardness of the dosas and by their very nature. (26)

# CHAPTER 39 अय श्लीपदनिदानम्

# ŚLIPADA NIDĀNAM (FILARIASIS) यः सज्वरो वङ्क्षणजो भृशार्तिः शोथो नृणां पादगतः क्रमेण । तच्छलीपदं म्यातु करकर्णनेत्रशिश्नौष्टनासास्वपि केचिदाहः ।। १।।

A swelling starting at the groins and gradually involving the leg, associated with fever and severe pain is known as Ślīpada. It may occur even in the hands, ears, eyes, penis, lips and nose, so say others (1)

# वातजं कृष्णरूक्षं च स्फुटितं तीव्रवेदनम् । अनिमित्तरुजं तस्य बहुशो ज्वर एव च ।। २।। पित्तजं पीतसङ्काशं दाहज्वरयुतं पृद् । श्लैष्मिकं स्निग्धवर्णं च श्वेतं पाण्डु गुरू स्थिरम् ।। ३।।

In vātaja variety, the swelling is black, rough, fissured, severe pain appearing without apparent reason and accompanied with fever.

In Pittaja variety, it has yellow colour, burning sensation, fever, soft to touch and in the kaphaja variety swelling is smooth, white in colour, heavy and hard. (2-3)

# वल्मीकमिव सञ्जातं कण्टकैरुपचीयते । अब्दात्मकं महत्तच्च वर्जनीयं विशेषतः ।। ४।।

The swelling growing upwards like an ant-hill with many sprouts, that which is more than one year old and that which is very big in size are to be rejected for treatment. (4)

# त्रीण्येतानि जानीयाच्छ्लीपदानि कफोच्छ्यात् । गुरुत्वं च यस्मान्नास्ति कर्फ विना ।। ५। ।

In all the three varieties of slīpada, kapha is the predominant dosa, because heaviness and increase in size cannot be without kapha. (5)

# पुराणोदकभूयिष्ठाः सर्वर्तुष् च शीतलाः । ये देशास्तेषु जायन्ते श्लीपदानि विशेषतः ।।६।।

Slipada is generally prevalent in such places (areas) where water stagnates for long time and which is cold in all the seasons of the year. (6)

# यच्छलेष्मलाहारविहार जातं पुंस: प्रकृत्याऽपि कफात्मकस्य ।

# सास्नावमत्युन्नतसर्वलिङ्गं सकण्डुरं श्लेष्मयुतं विवर्ज्यम् ।।७।।

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने श्लीपदनिदानं समाप्तम् ।।३९ !।

Slipada arising out of foods and habits which increase kapha, in persons who by birth belong to kapha constitution, that which has exudation, of very big size, with severe itching and with all symptoms is to be rejected for treatment. (7)

Thus ends the chapter on Ślipada.

# अथ विद्र्धिनिद्यनम्

## VIDRADHI NIDANAM (AB\$CESSES)

# त्वय्रक्तमांसमेदांसि संदूष्यास्थिताः । दोषाः शोधं शोधं श्रानैघोंरं जनयन्त्युच्छ्रता भृशम् । १ । महामूल् रजावन्तं वृत्तं वाऽप्यथवाऽऽयनम् । स विद्रधिरिति ख्यातो विक्षेयः बड्डिघश्च सः ॥ २ ॥ पृथग्दोषैः समस्तैश्च क्ष्तेनाप्यस्त्रज्ञा तथा । षण्णामापि हि तेषां तु लक्षणं संप्रवक्ष्यते ।। ३ ।। ( सु. नि. अ. ६ )

Dosas undergoing increase bring about abnormal changes in the skin, blood, muscle, fat and bones and produce a localised swelling, slowly increasing in size, deep seated, painful, round or elongated in shape, known as Vidradhi. It is of six types, one from each dosa, one from the combination of all the three, and one from Their features will be described further on. ( 1-3 ) rakta ( blood ).

# क्रुणोऽरुणो वा विषमो भृशमत्यर्थवेदनः । चित्रोत्थानवर्षाकश्च विद्युधिर्वातसंभवः ॥ ४ ॥ मु. निः ज्ञः २

Swelling black or brown in colour, increasing or decreasing in size often, with severe pain and variations in the duration of onset and time of suppuration are the symptoms of vidradhi due to increase of vāta. { 4 }

# पकोदुम्बरसङ्काशः दयावो वा ज्वरदाहवान् । क्षिप्रोतथानप्रपाकश्च विद्र्धिः पित्तसंभवः ॥ ५ ॥ ( मु. नि. ३. २ )

Swelling resembling a ripened u lumbara fruit ( wild fig ) or blue in collur associated with fever and burning sensation, quick in manifesting and suppurating are the symptoms of vidradhi due fo increase of pitta. ( 5 )

# शरावसदृशः पाण्डुः शीतः स्निग्धोऽल्पवेद्रनः । चिरोत्थानप्रपाकध्य विद्र्श्यः विद्र्श्यः कफसंभवः ॥ ६ ॥ ( सु. नि. ऋ. ९ )

Swelling resembling a saucer, yellowish-white in colour, cold, smooth, with mild pain, slow in manifesting and suppurating fare the symptoms of vidratin' includin' inc to increase of kapha. ( 6 )

# तनुपीतसिताश्चैषामास्त्रावाः क्रमशः स्मृताः स्मृताः ।

The pus exuding out from the above three will be thin, yellow and white respectively.

# नानावर्णरुजास्त्रावो घाटालो विषमो महान् ॥ ७ ॥ विषमं पच्यते चापि विद्र्धिः साश्निपातिकः । ( सु. नि. अ. ९ )

Sannipataja vidradhi ( due to increase of all the three dosas ) will be having different kinds of colours, pain and exudation, with elevated sprout, unsteady in nature, very troublesome and with irregular time of suppuration. ( 7 )

तैस्त्रर्मावैरभिहते क्षतोष्मा वायुविस्तः सरक्तं पित्तमीरयेत । ज्वरस्तृष्णा च दाहश्च जायते तस्य देहिनः ।। ९ ।। अ।गन्तविद्र धिह्येष पित्तविद्रधिलक्षणः । ( सु. नि. अ. ९ )

Persons who have been the victims of assault by weapons, indulging in inappropriate activities, will make themselves suitable for the heat of the injury to cause increase of pitta and rakta and produce vidradhi. Fever, thirst, burning sensation and other symptoms of pitta vrddhi will be seen in this kind of agantuja vidradhi ( traumatic abscess ). ( 8-9 )

> कृष्णस्फोटान्नुतः द्यावस्तीवदाहरुजाकरः ॥ १० ॥ पित्तविद्र्श्विलिङ्गस्तु रक्तविद्र्धिरुच्यते । ( सु. नि. अ. ९ )

Symptoms of raktaja vidradhi are black or blue in colour, more than one in number, causing severe burning sensation, pain and other symptoms of man one hi manifesting. ( 10 )

# पृथक् संभूय वा दोषाः कुपिता गुल्मरूपिणम् ।। ११ ।। वर्ल्मीकवत् स दुच्छमन्तः कुर्वन्ति विद्रधिम् । गुद्दे बस्तिमुखे नाभ्यां कुक्षौ वङ्क्षणयोस्तथा ॥ १२ ॥ वृक्षयोः प्रीहि यक्ति हृदि वा क्लोस्रि वाऽप्यथ । तेषामुक्तानि विद्यानि बाह्यविद्वधित्वस्थानं बाह्यविद्यधिकक्षणैः । १३ । अधिष्टानविशेषण लिङ्ग श्रणु विशोषतः ।

Dosas cither alone or in combination undergoing increase produce vidradhis internally, in different organs resembling an ant-hill or a bush; the rectum, orifice of the urinary bladder, umbilicus, abdomen, groins, the recum, brince, pancreas or any other place may be the site of the abscess; they have all the synptoms of external abscesses generally and in addition have special symptoms related to their site which will be described now : ( 11-14 )

गुद्दे वातनिरोधश्च वस्तौ कृद्धारुपमूत्रता ।। १४ ।। नाभ्यां हिक्का तथाऽऽटोपः कुक्षौ मारुतकोपनम् । कटीपृष्ठम्रद्वस्तीवो बङ्घणोरथे तु विद्र्धौ ।। १५ ।। वृक्कथोः पार्श्वसङ्कोचः प्लीह्रवुच्छ्वासावरोधनम् । सर्वाङ्गप्रद्रस्तीव्रो हृदि कास्सश्च जायते ।। श्वासो यकृति हिंद्या च क्लोस्नि पेपीयते पयः ॥ १६ ॥ ( मु. नि. १. २ )

If the absees is in the rectum, it causes obstruction to movement of flatus, if in the urinary bladder there is different and scanty urination, if it is in the unbilicus,

hiccough and gurgling noise in the abdomen manifest, if it is in the abdomen, there will be symptoms of vatavrddhi ( pain, distension etc. ), if it is in the groins, there will be severe catching pain in the waist and back, if it is in the kidney, constriction of the flanks is present, if in the pliha, there is obstruction to expiration, if it is located in the region of heart, there will be severe catching pain in all parts of the body and cough; if it is in the liver, it produces increased/difficult repiration and hiccough and if it is present in the pancreas, the patient drinks large quantity of water often. ( 14-16 )

> नाभेरुपरिजाः पक्का यान्त्यूध्वमितरं व्यधः । अधःस्त्रतेषु जीवेत्त स्त्रतेषुध्ये न जीवति ।। १७ ।। हन्नाभिवस्तिवर्त्या ये तेषु भिन्नेषु बाह्यतः ! जीवेन् कदाचित् पुरुषो नेतरेषु कदाचन 11 १८ 11 ( सु. नि. अ. ६ )

Those vidradhis which develop above the umbilical region, burst and discharge their contents in upward routes; while others discharge in the downward routes, if the discharge comes out in the downward route the patient will survive but if it takes the upward route he will die; those occuring in the ( region of ) heart, umbilicus and bladder are to be rejected; those in whom the vidradhi opens externally may survive but not others. ( 17-18 )

> साध्या विद्रधयः पञ्च विवज्यः सान्निपातिकः । आमपकविद्यम्यत्वं तेषां शोथवद्गदिशेन् ॥ १९ ॥ ( वा नि. अ. ११ ) आध्मातं वद्धनिष्यन्दं छदिविहकातुषान्वितम् । विद्रधिनाशयेन्नरम् ॥ २०॥ ( गु. मू. ज. ११ ) रुजाश्वाससम्प्रयुक्तं द्वाता श्रीमाधवकरावरावर्तिः भाववनिदाने विद्रश्रिनिदानं समाप्तम् ॥ ४० ॥

The first five kinds of vidradhis are curable, that caused by all the three dosas is to be rejected. The unripe, ripening and ripened stages are the same as described for sotha ( inflammatory oedema to be described in the next chapter ), distention of the abdomen, obstruction of urine, vomitting, hiccough, thirst, severe pain, increased/difficult respiration, if found in a patient of vidradhi, it is going to kill him. ( 19-20 )

Thus ends the chapter on Vidradhi.
