# CHAPTER 41 अथ् व्रणशोधनिदानम्

# VRAŅAŠOTHA NIDĀNAM ( INFLAMMATORY SWELLING ) एकदेशोधितः शोथो व्रणानां पूर्वलश्नुणम् । षड्डिघः स्यात् पृथक्सर्वरकागन्तुनिमित्त्रः ॥ १ ॥ पर्कदशातियतः शायां मणीषा भूजलहामध्ये । विदोषः वर्षस्य व्यवस्थाः

A localised swelling prior to the formation of an ulcer is called as Vraṇasotha ( inflammatory occlema ). It is of six kinds, one from each dosa, one from the ( inflamiliation of all the three, one from rakta ( blood ) and one from agantu ( extercollibition of the the will have the same kind of symptoms previously described for sother Now only their special features of unripe and rivened stages will be described. ( 1-2 )

# विषमं पच्यते वातात् पित्तोत्थश्चर्मान्दराच्चित्तम् । कफजः पित्तवच्छोयो रक्तागन्तुसमुद्धवः ॥ ३ ॥

Vitaja type undergoes ripening inconsistently, pittaja type very quickly, kaphaja type very slowly, raktaja type similar to pittaja, so also the agantuja type. ( 3 )

मन्दोष्मताऽरुपशोधत्वं काठिन्यं त्वक्सवर्णता । मन्दवेदनता चैतदछोथानामामलक्षणम् ॥ ४ ॥ मन्द्राज्यसम्बद्धाः स्टब्स् कारण्ड्यते । पिणीलिकागणेनैव दृदयते । छिद्यते तथा ॥ ५ ॥ भियते चंद्र शख्येण दृश्डेनेटा च ताउयते । पीड्यते प्राणिनेवान्दाः गुर्वीसिरिव तुद्यते । ६ ।। सिधानीषा चिवणः स्थान्डुल्येवावघट्यते । श्रासने शयने स्थाने शानित वृक्ष्यकविद्ववत् ॥ ७ ॥ लापादादा विवेश

Symptoms of the unripe stage are swelling is stightly warm, slightly elevated, hard, of normal colour of the skin and with mild pain.

Severe burning sensation as though touched by fire, or corroded by powerful alkali, bitten by a swarm of ants, cut or torn by sharp instruments, beaten by baton, squeezed by hand, pricked by needles, sucked or pressed by fingers, etc., discolourasqueezed by mans, priving comfort either on sitting up, lying down or elevating ( the affected part ), pain is continuous similar to that of a scorpion sting, swelling anceced party, point is , is without wrinkles, associated with fever, thirst and loss of appetite. These are the symptoms during the stage of ripening. ( 4-8 )

वेदनोपदामः शोधोऽलोहिनोऽल्पो न चोन्नतः । प्रादुर्भावो वल्रीनां च नोद्: कण्डूर्मुइद्वमुद्वुः ।। ९ ॥ वद्गीपरामः शास्त्राणां विन्नान् स्कुटनं त्वचाम् । बस्ताविवाम्युसञ्जूषः स्थान्छोथेऽङ्कुल्लपीडिते । १० ॥ उपद्र्षाणा श्रश्ना शिक्षणी रेप्रुटिन विवास् न रस्ता र कुर्वेच्चैतच्छोधानां पक्कलक्षणम् ॥ ११ ॥

Reduction in the severity of pain, redness and swelling; appearance of wrinkles, mild pricking or itching frequently, relief from other symptoms, slight depression at the top, opening of the skin; feeling of movement of fluid to the finger similar to that on a water bag, the pus inside can be collected to anyone place by pressing from all sides; the patient developing desire for food are the symptoms of fully ripened stage. ( 9-11 )

# नर्तेऽनिलादुङ्ग विना च पित्ते पाकः कफं चापि विना न पूर्यः । तस्माद्धि सर्वान् परिपाककाल्डै पचन्ति शोधांस्थ्य पव दोषाः ॥ १२ ॥ ( सु. मु. १७ )

Without ( the increase ) of vāta there can be no pain, without pitta, no ripening, and no pus formation without kapha; so all the three dosas are active during the period of ripening. ( 12 )

# कक्षं समासाद्य यथैव वहिर्वाय्वीरितः संदद्दति प्रसह्य ।

# तथैव पूर्यो ह्यविनिःस्त्रतो हि मांसं सिराः स्नायु च खाद्दतीह ॥ १३ ॥ ( मु. मू. अ. १७ )

Just as a small fire hidden inside a hearth ( or haystock ) activiated by the wind burus up the whole mass of hay, so also the pus which has not drained out destroys the muscless, veins, tendons, etc. ( 13 )

# अभि विद्यमानं च सम्यक् पक्कं च यो भिषक् । जानीयात् स भवेद्वेद्यः शेषास्तस्करव्यत्तयः ॥ १४ ॥

Only he deserves to be called a physician who can recognise the uripe, ripening and ripened stages ( of the inflammatory swellings ); whereas all others are only imposters. ( 14 )

# यद्रिछनत्यामम्भानाद्यो वा वा पक्षसुरेश्नते । श्वपचाविव मन्तव्याँ तावनिश्चित कारिणा हो ।। १५ ।। ( सु. सू. अ. १३ )

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचित्र माधवनिदाने व्रणशोधनिदानं समान्न ।। ४१ ।।

He who cuts open the swelling in its unripe stage with ignorance or he who delays its opening though fully ripened--- both are to be considered as very mean persons, because they are uncertain in their act. ( 15 )

Thus ends the chapter on Vranasotha.

## अध शारीरव्रणनिदानम्

#### ŠĀRĪRA VRAŅA NIDĀNAM ( ORGANIC ULCERS )

#### द्विधा व्रणः स विज्ञेयः शारीरागन्तुभेदतः। दोषैराद्यस्तयोरन्यः शस्त्रादिश्वतसंभवः ॥ १ ॥

Vraņas ( ulccrs ) are of two kinds; sārīra ( organic ) and āgantuja ( traumatic ); the first one is caused by the dosas and the second by weapons, ( 1 )

# स्तब्धः करिनसंस्पर्शों मन्दस्रावो महारजः । तुधते स्फुरति द्याचो वणो मारुतसंभवः ।। २ ।। तृष्णामोहस्वरक्रेददाहदुष्यवदारणैः । वर्णं पित्तदृतं विद्याद्रन्धः स्त्रावध्य पूर्तिकैः ।। ३ ॥ बढ़ुपिच्छो गुरुः स्निग्धः स्तिमितो मन्दवेद्नः । पाण्डुवर्णोऽरूपसंक्रेदश्चिरपाकी कफव्रवणः ।। ४ ।। रक्तो रक्तस्वृती रक्तात् द्वित्रिजः स्यात्तदन्वयैः । ( च. चि. अ. १३ )

Vrana caused by vata is immovable, hard to touch, with scanty exudation, severe pain such as pricking or pulsating and blue in colour. The symptoms of vrana caused by pitta are, the patient will be having thirst, delusion, fever, perspiration, burning sensation, cracked skin, the ulcer emits bad smell and exudes foul discharge. Vrana caused by kapha will be having large amount of exudation, is hard, smooth, immovable, slightly painful, yellowish-white in colour, moist and suppurates slowly. Vrana caused by rakta ( blood ) will be red in colour and exudes only blood. Vrana caused by the combination of either two or three dosas, will be having their respective symptoms together. ( 2-4 )

# त्वक्यांसजः सुखे देशे तरुणस्यानुपद्रवः ॥ ५ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. १३ ) धीमतोऽभिनवः कालै सुखे साध्यः सुखं वणः ।

Ulcers which are located in the skin or muscles, in a convenient place, of recent origin and uncomplicated; that in a strong person of recent onset, in appropriate time ( season, age, etc. ) are easily curable. ( 5 )

# गुणैरन्यतमैहींनस्ततः कृच्छ्रो वणः स्मृतः ॥ ६ ॥ सर्वेविंद्वीनो विश्लेयरत्वसाध्यो भूयुपद्रवः ।

Those which possess a few of the above qualities are difficult to cure; while those which are devoid of any of the above qualities and associated with severe complications are incurable. ( 6 )

Ch. 42, Organic ulcers

ये च मर्मस्वसंभूता मवन्त्यत्यवेदनाः ।। १४ ।।

द्द्यन्ते चान्तरत्यर्थं बहिः शीताश्च ये वणाः । दह्यन्ते बहिरत्यर्थं भवन्त्यन्तश्च श्रीतल्प्रः ॥ १५ ॥ प्राणमांसक्षयश्वासकासारोचकपीडिताः । प्रवृद्धपूरुधिरा वणा येषां च मर्मसु ॥ १६ ॥ म्भागिः सम्यगारब्धाः न सिध्यन्ति च ये वणाः । वर्जयेदपि तान् वैद्यः संरक्षत्रात्मनो यशः ॥ १० ॥

इनि श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने शारीरव्रणनिदानं समाप्तम् ॥ ४२ ॥

Vranas ( ulcers ) manifesting on vital organs, having severe pain, burning sensation inside and cold outside or vice versa, causing loss of strength, emaciation, increased respiration, cough, loss of appetite, di charging large quantities of pus or blood, those which do not respond to treatment though well planned, are all to be rejected keeping in mind his reputation as a physician. ( 14-17 )

Thus ends the chapter on Sarīra Vraņa.

#### अथ सद्योव्रणनिद्यनम्

# SADYOVRAŅA NIDANAM ( TRAUMATIC ULCERS ) नानाधारमुखैः शस्त्रैनानास्थाननिपातितैः । भवन्ति नानाकृतयो व्रणास्तांस्तान्निबोध मे ।। १ ॥

Different kinds of ulcers caused by assault with different types of sharp weapons with different kinds of edges, in different places of the body are going to be described further on. ( 1 )

# छिन्नं भिन्नं तथा विद्धं क्षतं पिचितमेव च । घृष्टमाहुस्तथा षष्ठं तेषां वक्ष्यामि लक्षणम् ॥ २ ॥

( सु. वि. अ. २ )

Chinna ( incised wound ), bhinna ( punctured wound ), viddha { stabbed wound ), ksata ( lacerated wound ), piccita ( crushed wound ) and ghrsta ( excoriated ) are the six types of ulcers, the features of which are as follows. ( 2 )

# तिर्यक् छिन्न ऋजुर्वाऽपि यो वणस्त्वायतो भवेत । गात्रस्य पातनं तथ्य छिन्नुमित्यभिधीयते ॥ ३ ॥ ( मु. वि. अ. २ )

Chinna vrana is defined as that ( caused by a sharp cdged cutting instrument ), in which the wound is either curved or straight, wide and causing mutilation of a part of the body. ( 3 )

# शक्तिदन्तेषुखड्गाग्रविषाणैराशयोराशयो हृतः । यतिकचित् प्रस्नवेत्तद्धि भिन्नुलक्षणमुच्यते ॥ ४ ॥ ( मु. चि. अ. २ )

Bhinna vrana is a punctured wound caused by pointed javellion, tusk, arrow, spear, or horns of animals, into different viscera; thereby causing discharge of their contents to the exterior. ( 4 )

# स्थानान्यामाश्रिपकानां मूत्रस्य रुधिरस्य च । हृदुण्डुकः फुप्फुसश्च कोष्ठ इत्यभिधीयते ।। ५ ।। तस्मिन् भिन्ने रक्तपूर्णे ज्वरो दाहश्य जायते । मूत्रमार्गमुदास्येम्यो रक्तं घाणाच्च गच्छति ।। ६ ॥ मूच्छा श्वासस्त्रषाऽऽध्मानमभक्तञ्छन्द् एव च । विण्मूत्रवातसङ्गश्च स्वेदास्त्रावोऽक्षिरक्तता ॥ ७ ॥ लोहगन्धित्वमास्यस्य गात्रदौर्गन्ध्यमेव च । हृच्छूलं पार्श्वयोग्न्नापि विशेष चात्र मे श्रणु ॥ ८ ॥

The organs of ama ( undigested food i. e., stomach ), agni ( digestive juices i. e., liver and gall bladder and pancreas ), pakva ( digested food i.e., intestines ), mūtra ( urine i. e. urinary bladder ), rudhira ( blood i.e., spleen ), hrdaya ( heart ), unduka ( caecum ), fupfusa ( lungs ), are known as kostha ( abdominal viscera ).

A punctured wound into the kosţha will produce accumulation of blood in the injured organ, fever, burning sensation, bleeding through urethra, anus or nose; fainting, increased/difficult respiration, thirst, distention of the abdomen, loss of appetite, obstruction to movement of urine, feces and flatus, continuous respiration, redness of the eyes, smell of iron from the mouth, bad smell of the body and pain in the heart and flanks generally. ( 5-8 )

# आमाशयस्थे रुधिरे रुधिरं छर्दयत्यपि । आध्मानमतिमात्रं च शूल्रं च भृशदारुगम् ॥ ९ ॥ पकार यगते चापि रज्ञा गौरवमेव च । अधःकाये विशेषेण शीतता च भवेदिह ॥ १० ॥ (मु. चि. व. २)

If amasaya ( stomach ) is punctured, there will be vomitting of blood, severe distention and pain in the abdomen.

If pakvāśaya ( small and large intestines ) is punctured there will be severe pain, heaviness and coldness of the lower extrimities. ( 9-10 )

# सूक्ष्मास्मराल्याभिद्वतं यद्दङ्गं त्वाशयं विना ।

उत्तरण्डतं निर्मतं वा तद्विद्धामिति निर्दिशेत् ॥ ११ ॥ ( मु. चि. अ. २ )

Viddha vrana ( stab wound ) is that caused by a pointed instrument, away from any viscera, with slight swelling and the instrument either lodged inside or having gone out. ( 11 )

# नातिच्छन्नं नातिभिन्नमुभयो र्लक्षणान्वितम् ।

## विषमं वण मद्भे यत्तत् यत्तत् क्षतं त्वभिधीयते ।। १२ ॥ ( सु. चि. व. २ )

Ksata vrana ( lacerated wound ) is that which is neither deeply incised nor punctured but having mild symptoms of both of them and irregular in shape. (12)

# प्रहारपीडनाभ्यां त् यदद्कं प्रथुतां गतम् । सास्थि तत् पिद्धितं विद्याम्मज्जरक्तपरिप्लुतम् ॥ १३ ॥ ( सु. चि. अ. २ )

Piccita vraṇa ( crushed wound ) is caused by a blow or pressure ( by a blunt weapon ) by which a part of the body is flattened along with the bone and marrow and blood flowing out. ( 13 )

# घर्षणाद्भिधाताद्रा यदङ्गे विगतत्वचम् । उषास्त्रावान्वितं तच्च घुष्टमित्यभिधीयते ॥ १४ ॥ ( सू. चि. २ )

Ghrsta vrana ( abrasion of the skin ) is caused by friction or mild blow; in which the skin is peeled off producing mild burning sensation and flow of watery fluid. ( 14 )

# द्यावं सशोधं पिडकाचितं च मुद्रमुद्रः शोणितवाहिनं च । म्हदूद्रतं वुद्बुदतुल्यमांसं व्रणं सशल्यं सर्रजं वद्गित ॥ १५ ॥

A wound in which the foreign body is hidden inside, will appear blue, swollen, bulged out, bleeding frequently, soft, the surrounding muscles feeling frothy and painful. ( 15 )

# त्वचोऽतील्य सिरादीनि मित्वा वा परिहृत्य वा । कोष्ठे प्रतिष्ठितं शल्यं कुर्यादुक्तानुपद्रवान् । १६ ।। ( मू. चि. अ. २ ) तत्रान्तलोहितं पाण्डुशीतपादकराननम् । शीतोछासं रक्तनेत्रमानद्धं च विवर्जयेस् ।। १७ ॥

A foreign body getting lodged inside the abdomen, injuring the skin and blood vessels or avoiding them, will have the following symptoms; accumulation of blood inside the abdomen, severe pallor of the skin, cold touch of the feet, hands and face, cold breath, redness of the eyes and distention of the abdomen. This condition is to be refused treatment. ( 16-17 )

> भ्रमः प्रलापः एतनं प्रमोहो विचेण्डनं ग्लानिरधोष्णता च । स्त्रस्ता मुन्द्रनमुध्वेवातस्तीवा रुजो घातकृताश्र तास्ताः ।। १८ ॥ मांसोदकाभे राधिरं च गढ़छेस सर्वेन्द्रयार्थोपरमस्तथैव । दशार्धसंख्येष्यथ विक्षतेषु सामान्यतो मर्मस्यु लिख्नमुक्तम् ॥ १९.॥ ( मु. सू. २.५ )

General symptoms of wounds to the five structures ( muscles, veins, tendons, bony joints and vital organs ) are giddiness, delierum, falling on the ground, delusion, undesirable movements of the hands and feet, exhaustion, feeling of warmth, drooping of the body parts, fainting, upward movements inside the abdomen, severe types of pain characteristic of vata, discharge of blood resembling water in which mutton has been washed and loss of functions of sense organs. ( 18-19 )

> स्वर्ग्द्रगोपशतिमं प्रभूनं रक्तं स्नवेत्तरक्षसम्बद्ध वायुः । करोति रोगान विविधान् यथोकान् सिरासु विद्धास्वथ वा क्षतासु ।। २० ।। कौब्ज्यं शरीराव्यवायसादः क्रियास्वर्शाक्तस्तुमुला रुजश्च ! चिराद्यणो रं।हात यस्य चापि तं स्नागुविद्धं पुरुषं व्यवस्येत् ॥ २१ ॥

Special symptoms manifesting when particular structures and injured are as follows :

If siras ( blood vessels ) are injured there will be heavy discharge of blood resembling indragopa insect ( bright red in colour ) and vata undergoing increase produces many disorders later;

Decrease in height, drooping of the body parts, loss of function, very severe pain and wound taking long time to heal are the features when snayus ( tendons/ nerves ) are injured;

> शोफाभिष्टुड़िस्तुमुला रुजश्च बलक्षयः सर्वत पव शोधः । क्षतेषु सन्धिष्वचलाचलेषु स्थात् सर्वकर्मोपरमश्च लिङ्गम् ॥ २२ ॥

If bony joints- cither mobile or immobile-are injured there will be increase of the swelling, severe pain, loss of strength, oedema in other parts of the body, and total loss of functions;

# घोरा रुजो यस्य निशादिनैषु सर्वास्ववस्थासु च नैति शान्तिम् ! भिष्यम्बुपश्चिद्विदितार्थस्त्रस्तमस्थिविद्धं पुरुषं ध्यवस्येत् ॥ २३ ॥ ( गृ. मृ. अ. २२ ) .

The intelligent physician who is well conversant with science ( of wounds ) should recognise a person as suffering from injury of the bones by the following symptoms-very severe pain continuously throughout day and night and not finding relief in any posture. ( 20-23 )

# यथास्वमेतानि विभावयेच्छ लिङ्गानि मर्मस्वामताडितेषु । ( र. कु. अ. २४ ) पाण्डुर्विवर्ण स्पृश्चितं न वेत्ति यो मांसमर्मण्यामिर्वा स्यात् ॥ २४ ॥

Similar symptoms will be found in persons whose vital organs are injured; if vital spots of muscles are injured, there will be anaemia, discolouration and loss of tactile sensation. ' 24 )

```
विसर्पः पक्षयातश्च  सिरास्तम्भोऽपतानकः ।
मोहोन्मादवणरुजो ज्वरस्तृष्णा हुनुग्रहः ।। २५ ॥ ( चं. चि. अ. २५ )
कासदखदिरतीसारो हिका श्वासः सर्वेपथ्रः ।
षोडशोपद्र्वाः प्रोक्ता वणानां वणचिन्तकैः ॥ २६ ॥ ( च. चि. अ. १२ )
```
इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने सद्योग्वणनिद्यानं सुमाप्तम् ।। ४३ ।।

Specialists in the knowledge of wounds enumerate the following sixteen as the complications/sequallae of wounds-erysepelas, hemip'egia, stifficss of the head, tetanus, delusion, insanity, severe pain in the wound, fever, thirst, lockjaw, cough, vomitting, diarrhoea. hiccough, difficulty in breathing and tremors, ( 25-26 )

Thus ends the chapter on Sadyovrana.

#### अथ भग्ननिद्यानम्

# BHAGNA NIDANAM ( FRACTURE OF BONES )

## भग्नं समासाद्रिद्वविधं हुताश काण्डे च सन्धौ च हि तत्र सन्धौ । उत्पिष्टविविद्यविवर्तितं च तिर्यगातं क्षितमधश्च घट च ।। १ ॥

Bhagna ( fracture ) is, in brief, of two kinds; one at the kanda ( shaft or body of the bones ) and the other at the sandhi ( meeting place of two bones ); of them sandhibhaguas ( fracture at the meeting place of two bones or sutures ) is of six kinds, viz., utpişta, vislişta, vivarita, tiryaggata, ksipta and adhahkşip'a. ( 1 )

# प्रसारणाकुञ्चनवर्तनोग्रा रुक् स्पर्शविद्धेषणमेतदृक्तम् । सामान्यतः सन्धिगतस्य लिङ्गं

Severe pain during extension, contraction and rotation; guarding against touch ( tenderness ) are the general symptoms of Sandhibhagna ( fracture at the sutures ).

## अथ भगन्दर्रनिद्यनम्

#### BHAGANDARA NIDANAM ( FISTULA-IN-ANO )

# गुदस्य द्यू कुले क्षेत्र पार्श्वतः पिडकाऽऽर्तिकृत । भिन्ना भगन्दरो श्रेयः स च पञ्चविधो मतः ॥ १ ॥

A papule developing on either side of the anus, within a radius of two angulis. accompanied with pain and exudation is known as Bhagandara ( fistula-in-ano). It is of five kinds. ( 1 )

# कषायरूक्षैस्त्वतिकोपितोऽनिल्हर्त्वपानदेशे पिडकां करोति याम् । उपेक्षणात् पाकमुपैति द्वारुणं रुजा च भिन्नाऽरुणफेनवाद्विनी ।। २ ।। तत्रागमो मूत्रपुरीषरेतसां वर्णैरनैकैंः शतपोनकं वदेतु । प्रकोपणः पित्तमतिप्रकोपितं करोति रक्तां पिडकां गुद्दाधिताम् ॥ ३ ॥

Indulgence in foods which are astringent and dry, causes increase of vata, which in turn produces a papule in the anus. By its neglect, it undergoes suppuration, bursts out, creates many small ulcers all around, through which crimson coloured froth, urine, feces and semen may be coming out accompanied with severe This is called as Śataponāka. ( 2-3 ) pain.

# तदाऽऽश्रुपाकाहिमपूर्तिवाहिनीं भगन्दरं तू ष्टूशिरोधरं वदेत् ॥ ४ ॥

Pitta undergoing increase greatly, produces a red coloured pustule in the anal region, which develops pus quickly inside, which is discharged hot and with foul smell. This type is called as Ustrasirodhara ( ustragriva ). ( 4 )

# कण्डूयनो घनस्नावी कठिनो मन्दवेदनः श्वेतावमासः कफजः परिस्त्रावी भगन्दरः ।। ५ ।।

Pustules having different kinds of colours and exudates, which resemble the teet of the cow, creating spiral sinuses inside resembling that of seashell is known as Sambūkavārtā ( this is produced by increase of all the three dosas together ). (6)

> क्षताद्रतिः पायुगता विवर्धते ह्युपेक्षणात् स्युः क्रिमयो विद्यायं ते । प्रकुर्वते मार्गमनैकधा मुखैर्वणैस्तदुन्मार्गि भगन्दरं वद्रेत् ॥ ७ ॥

Minor injury ( caused by thorns, etc. ) at the anus, if neglected leads to the formation of sinuses from infestation by germs; small ulcers get formed later. This kind is known as Unmargi bhagandara. ( 7 )

> घोराः साधयितुं दुःखा सर्वे पव भगन्दराः । तेष्वसाध्यस्त्रिदोषोत्थः क्षतजश्च विशेषतः ॥ ८ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. ४ )

and the same of the same of the same of the same of the same of the same of the same of the same of the states of the states of the states of the states of the states of the

All kinds of fistula are dreadful and difficult to cure, those caused by all three dosas and injury are incurable. ( 8 )

> वातमध्रपरीषाणि किमयः शुक्रमेव च । भगन्दरान् स्वचन्तु नाशयन्ति तमातुरम् ॥ ९ ॥ ( गु. नि. अ. ३३ )

Injury to the penis by the hand, nails and teeth; keeping it dirty without washing, indulging in excessive sexual intercourse, contact with contaminated or diseased vagina and such other bad habits give rise to the disease-Upadamsa ( veneral diseases ) which is of five kinds. { 1 )

> सतोदभेदैः स्फुरणैः सक्कृष्णैः स्फोटैर्व्यवस्येत् पवनोपदंशम् । पीतैबंहुक्केदयु नैः सदाहैः पित्तेन रक्तात् पिशितावभासैः ॥ २ ॥ स्फोटैः सक्नूबणै रुधिरं स्वन्तं रक्तात्मकं पित्तसमानलिङ्गम् । सकण्डुरैः शोधयूतैमद्वन्द्वः शुक्रुर्घनेः स्नावयूतः कफेन ।। ३ ।। नानाविधस्तावरुजोपपन्नमस्त्राध्यमाहस्त्रिमलोपदंश्यम् । विशीणमांस किमिनिः प्रजम्धं मुष्कावशेषं परिवर्जयेच ॥ ४ ॥

In the vataja type, there appears on the penis small papules which have pricking, cutting or pulsating type of pain and are black in colour.

In the pittaja type, the papules discharge yellowish fluid, large in quantity accompanied with burning sensation; in the raktaja type, the papules will be resembling muscle ( red in colour ) or black, discharge blood and all other symptoms of pitta will be present; in the kaphaja type, there is a big swelling, the papules are itching and exude thick white fluid.

That produced by all the three dosas together will be having different kinds of exudates and pains and is incurable.

Foolish people engrossed in sexual life do not seek treatment soon after its onset and neglecting it become victims of swelling, infection by bacteria, burning sensation, pus formation, destruction of the penis and death. ( 5 )

> अश्वरैरिव सङ्घातैरुपर्युपरि संस्थितैः । क्रमेण जायते वर्तिस्ताम्रचुडशिखोपमा ।। ६ ।। कोषस्याभ्यन्तरे सन्धौ सर्वसन्धिगताऽपि वा । ( सवेदना पिछिछला च दुश्रिकित्स्या त्रिदोषजा । ) लिक्कवर्तिरभिख्याता लिक्काश इति चापरे ।। ७ ।।

It is difficult to treat this disease also. ( 6-7 )

Uttama is the name of the disease in which papules of the size of greengram or blackgram, red in colour, appear due to vitiated rakta and pitta caused by poiso nous paste used repeatedly. ( 8 )

> छिद्रैरणुमुखैलिंड्गं चितं यस्य समन्ततः ॥ ९ ॥ वातशोणितजो ध्याधिः स क्षेयः शतपोनकः । ( मृ. नि. अ. १४ )

Sataponaka is a disease caused by increased vata and rakta in which the penis is having small holes everywhere. ( 9 )

# घातपित्तकृतो क्षेयस्त्वकपाको ज्वरदाहरूल् ।। १० ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १४ )

Tvakpāka is caused by increased vāta and pitta, with ulccration of the skin of the penis, burning sensation and fever. ( 10 )

# कृष्णैः स्फोटैः सरक्ताभिः पिडकाभिनिपीडितम् । यस्य वास्तुरुजश्चोग्रा क्षेयं तच्छोणितार्युद्दम् ॥ ११ ॥ ( ग. नि. ३. १४ )

Black or reddish black vesicles appearing all over the penis associated with severe pain is a condition known as Sonitarbuda. ( 11 )

> मांसदोषण जानीयादर्बुदं मांससंभवम् । ( मु. उ. अ. १४ ) शीर्यन्ते यस्य मांसानि यस्य सर्वाध्य वेदनाः ॥ १२ ॥ ( म. नि. अ. १४ )

विद्यात्तं मांसपाकं तु सर्वदोषकृतं भिषक् । ( सु. नि. अ. १४ ) विद्र्ष्यि सन्निपातेन यथोकसिति निर्दिंशेत् ॥ १३ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १४ )

Māṁsārbuda should be recognised by involvement of muscle tissue; Māṁaspāka is a condition which is caused by all the three dosas, in which the fleshy portion of the penis falls off, accompanied with pain; Virdadhi is another condition caused by the three dosas which resembles sannipātajavirdadhi previously descri all bed. ( 12-13 )

# कृष्णानि विश्राण्यथवा शूकानि सविषाणि वा । पातितानि पचन्त्याशु मेदूं निरवशेषतः ॥ १४ ॥ कालानि भूत्वा मांसानि शीर्यन्ते यस्य देहिनः ।

सन्निपातसमुत्थांस्तु तान् विद्यात्तिलकाल्क्रकान् ।। १५ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १४ )

Application of paste of insects which are black or of many colours and which are highly poisonous applied over the penis, cause the skin to become black, muscle to drop off and appearance of warts resembling the black moles of the skin. This condition known as Tilakalaka is due to the increase of all the three dogas together. ( 14-15 )

> तत्र मांसार्वुदं यश्व मांसपाकश्च यः स्मृतः । विद्रधिश्च न सिद्ध्यन्ति ये च रयुस्तिल्लकालकाः ।। १६ ॥ ( सु. नि. अ. १४ )

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने शूकदोषनिद्दानं समाप्तम् ।। ४८ ।।

Among the above diseases, māmsārbuda, mamsapāka, vidradhi and tilakālaka do not respond to treatment. { 16 }

Thus ends the chapter on Sukadosa,

#### अथ कुष्ठनिद्यानम्

KUST HA NIDANAM ( LEPROSY AND OTHER DISEASES OF THE SKIN ) विरोधीन्यन्नपानानि द्रवस्त्रम्यगुर्श्ण च । भजतामागता छन्दै वेगाश्चान्यान् प्रतिप्रताम् ॥ १ ॥ ब्यायाम् तिसन्तापमतिभूत्तवाः निषेविणाम् । घर्मश्रमम्भयार्तानां द्रतं शीताम्बुसेविनाम् ॥ २ ॥ अजीर्णाध्यशिनां चैच पश्चकर्मापचारिणाम् । नवान्नद्यिमरस्यातिलवणाम् ॥ ३ ॥ माषमूलक्कपिष्ठान्त्रतिलश्लीरगुडाशिनाम् । ब्यवायं चाण्यजीणेंऽप्रे निद्रां च भजतों दिवा ॥ ४ ॥ विप्रान् गुरून् धर्मयतां पापं कर्म च कुर्वताम् । वातादयस्त्रयो दुष्टास्त्वय्कं मांसमध्यु च ॥ ५ ॥ दूषयम्ति स कुष्ठानां सप्तको द्रव्यसम्बद्धानि जायन्ते सप्त वैकादशैव व । ६ ।। ( च. चि. अ. ७ )

Indulgence in incompatible foods and drinks, footls which are very watery, fatty and hard to digest; suppressing the urges of vomitting and others, heavy physical exercises and too much of exposure to heat immediately after taking food, immersing in cold water soon after exposure to sunlight, hardwork causing fatigue and incidents of fear; use of uncooked foods and over eating, improper methods of administering the five purificatory therapies ( emesis, purgation, etc. ), use of fresh grains, fish, foods which are very salty and sour blackgram, raddish; dry or powdery foods, sesame, milk, and jaggery; over indulging in sexual intercourse, sleeping during day though suffering from indigestion; showing disrespect to gods, teachers and committing many other kinds of sinful acts, etc., make for the increase of all the three dosas and derangement in the tvak { skin }, rakta ( blood ), māmsa ( muscular tissue ), ambu ( body fluids ) and produce kuştha ( leprosy and similar skin diseases ). This group of seven ( the three dosas and four dhatus ) causes seven and eleven kinds of kustha. ( 1-6 )

# कुष्ठानि सप्तथा दोषैः पृथग्द्वन्द्वैः समागतैः । सर्वेष्वयि त्रिदोषिषु ध्यपद्देशोऽधिकत्वतः ॥ ७ ॥ ( या. वि. अ. १४ )

Kustha ( leprosy, etc. ) is of seven kinds, one from each dosa, three from the combination of two dosas, and one from the combination of all the three together, This kind of naming the varieties is to indicate the predominance of the dosa, though all the three dosas are involved in the causation of all varieties of kuştha. (7)

अतिश्रुष्णखररूपर्श्वेदास्वेदविवर्णताः । दाहः कण्डूस्त्वचि स्वापस्तोदः कोठोस्नतिर्ममः ।। ८ ॥ व्रणानाम्भिकं शुल्कं श्रीघ्रोत्पत्तिश्चिरस्थितिः । रूढानामणि ब्रक्षत्वं निर्मित्ते ऽल्पेऽतिकोपनम् ॥ ९ ॥

#### रोमहर्षोऽस्त्रः काण्ण्यं कुष्ठलुक्षणमग्रजम् । ( वा. नि. अ. ४ )

The skin either very smooth or rough; presence or absence of perspiration; discolouration, burning sensation, itching, loss of tactile sensation, pricking pain, appearance of elevated patches, giddiness, severe pain on injury, ulcer forming quickly and remaining for long periods without healing; though healed with difficulty roughness of skin persisting and re-appearance of the ulcer even with trivial causes, frequent horripulations and blackish discolouration of the blood are the premonitory symptoms of kustha. ( 8-9 )

# कृष्णारुणकपोलाभं यद्रूक्षं परुषं तनु ।। १० ॥

कापालं तोदबहुलं तत्कुष्ठं विषमं स्मृतम् । हग्दाहरागकण्डूभिः परीतं रोमपिञ्जरम् ।। ११ ॥ उदुम्बरफलाभासं कुम्प्रमोदुम्बरं घदेत् । श्वेतं रक्तं स्थिरं स्त्यानं स्निग्धमुत्सन्नमण्डलम् ॥ १२ ॥ क्रच्छ्रमन्योन्यसंयुक्तं कुष्ठं मण्डलमुच्यते । कर्केञां रक्तपर्यन्तमन्तः इयावं सवेदनम् ॥ १३ ॥ यद्यदिह्नसंस्थानमृष्याजिह्नं तदुच्यते । सश्वेतं रक्तपर्यन्तं पुण्डरीकेदलोपमम् ॥ १४ ॥ सोल्सेधं च सरागं च पुण्डरीकं तदुच्यते । श्वेतं ताम्रं तनु च यद्रजो घृष्ठं विमुश्रति ॥ १५ ॥ प्रायश्रोरसि तत् सिध्ममलावुक् सुमोपमम् । यत्काकर्णन्तिकावर्णं सपाक्कं तीववेदनम् ॥ १६ ॥ त्रिदोषलिङ्गं तत्कुष्ठं काकणं नैव सिध्यति । ( च. चि. अ. ७ )

s in the stime the skin resembling a brown coloured piece of pot sherd; rough, course and thin; painful and intractable are the features of Kapala kuştha.

Pain, burning sensation, redness, itching hairs turned to pink colour and nodules developing resembling the fruits of udumbara (Ficus infectoria are the symptoms of Udumbara type of kustha.

Skin is white or red in colour, tight, thickened, smooth with round clevated patches joined to one another are the features of Mandala type of kustha.

Skin very rough, red in colour outside and black inside, painful and resembling the tongue of black deer are the features of Rsyajihva variety of kustha.

Skin white in the centre and red in the edges similar to the petal of the lotus flower with reddish elevated patches are seen in Pundarika variety of kustha.

Skin white or coppery red in colour, thin, scals coming of on scratching, more common on the chest, resembling the flower of pitchergound are the features of Sidhma variety of kustha.

That which has the colour of guitia ( Abrus precatorius ), undergoing pus formation, very painful and caused by all the three dosas and which does not respond to treatment are the features of kuştha known as Kākaņa.

( The above seven varietics are known as mahā kusthas ( major leprosies ), which are incurable. ) { 10-16 }

अस्वेदनं महावास्तु यन्मत्स्यशकल्योपमम् ॥ १७ ॥ तदेककुष्ठं, चर्माख्यं बद्दलं हस्तिचर्मवत् । द्यावं किणखरस्पर्श पुरुषं किटिभं स्मतम ॥ १८ ॥ वैपादिकं पाणिपाद्र्स्फुटनं तीववेदनम् । कण्डूमन्द्रिः सरागैश्च गण्डैरलस्यकं चितम् ॥ १९ ॥ सकण्डूरागपिडकं दद्रमण्डलमुद्रतम् । रक्तं सशूलं कण्डूमत् सरकोटं यद्रल्यपि । तचमदेहलमाख्यातं संस्पर्शासहमुच्यते ॥ २० ॥ सूक्ष्मा वह्वयः पिडकाः स्नाववत्यः पामेत्युक्ताः कण्ड्रमत्यः सदाहृद्धः सदाहृद्धः । स्वैव स्फोटेस्तीवदाहरु रुपेता ज्ञेया पाण्यो: कच्छूरुमा रिफचोश्च ॥ २१ ॥ स्फोटाः ध्यावरुणाभासा विस्फोटाः स्युस्तनुत्वचः । रक्तं द्यावं सदाहाति शतारः स्याद्रहृद्यणम् ॥२२। सकण्डू: पिडका दयावा बहुस्रावा विचाचिका । ( च. नि. अ. ७ )

Absence of perspiration, large area of skin involved, skin resembling the scales of a fish are the features of Eka kuştha; the skin becoming thick like that of an elephant skin is seen in Carmakhya kustha; black, rough and course to touch and producing noie ( on scratching ) are the features of the skin in Kitibha kuştha; hands and feet cracking and forming fissures which are painful is the feature of Vaipadika kuştha; nodules with itching and of red colour developing is in alasaka kuştha; raised patches studded with small, itching, reddish proules are the features of Dadrimandala kuşṭha; skin studded with red, painful, itching nodules, which do not allow touching are the features of Carmadala kuşthat, small, plentiful, exudating pustules with itching and burning sensation are known as Pama; the same appearing in the hands and on the buttecks with severe pain and itching are called as Kacchu; boils on the skin, black or blackish-red in colour and the skin becoming thin are the features of Visphota; innumerable small ulcers which are red or blue in colour, with severe burning sensation and pain are the features of Sataru; nodules which have itching, black colour and copious exudation are seen in Vicarcika. ( 17-22 )

( The above eleven kinds are known as Ksudra kusthas ( minor leprosies ).

## खर द्यावारुणं रूक्षं वातात्कृष्ठं सवेदनम् ॥ २३ ॥ पित्तात्प्रकथितं दाहरागस्नावान्वितं मतम् । कफात्कोदि घनं स्विग्धं सकण्डूशैत्यगौरवम् ॥ २४ ॥ द्विलिङ्गं द्वन्द्रजं कुष्ठं त्रिलिङ्गं सान्निपातिकम् ।

Roughness, blue or bluish-red colour, dryness and pain are due to vata in all the kinds of kustha; puturefaction, burning sensation, redness and exudation are due to pitta; unctuousness, thickness, greasiness, itching, cold and fecling of heaviness are due to kapha; presence of symptoms of two dosas and of all the three will also manifest respectively. ( 23-24 )

त्वक्स्थे वैवर्ण्यमद्भेषु कुण्ठे रौक्ष्यं च जायते ॥ २५ ॥ त्वक्स्वापो रोमहष्यक्ष स्वेदस्यातिप्रवर्तनम् । कण्डूविंपूयकश्चैव कुष्ठे शोणितसंश्रिते ॥ २६ ॥ बाहुल्यं वक्रशोधश्य कार्कदयं पिडकोद्रमः । तोद् : स्फोटः स्थिरत्वं च कुष्ठे मांससमाश्रिते ।। २५ ! 12 M

कौण्यं गतिक्षयोऽङ्गानां संमेदः क्षतसर्पणम् । मेदःस्थानगते लिङ्गं प्रागुक्तानि तथैच च ॥ २८ ॥ नासाभक्नोऽक्षिरागश्च क्षतेषु किमिसंभवः । स्वरोपघातश्च मवेदस्थिमज्जसमाश्रिते ॥ २९ ॥ दम्पत्योः कुष्ठबाद्दुब्याद् दुष्ठशोणित्युक्रयोः । यद्पत्यं तयोर्जातं ज्ञेयं तद्दि कुष्ठितम् ॥ ३० ॥

( स. नि. अ. ५ )

If only the skin ( rasa dhatu ) is involved by kuştha, discolouration of the skin, roughness, loss of sensation, hairs standing on end frequently and profuse perspiration are seen.

When kuştha invades rakta ( blood ), itching and appearance of pustules in large numbers, will manifest.

When kustha invades mamsa ( muscle ) there will be appearance of thick, elevated patches on the skin, dryness of the mouth, roughness, appearance of nodules, pricking pain, fissures and tightness of the skin.

When kustha invades medas ( fat ), loss of fingers, loss of movement ( difficulty to walk ), pain, spreading of the ulcer will also be seen in addition.

When kustha invades the asthi ( bones ) and majja ( bone marrow ), loss of the nose, redness of the cyes, appearance of worms in the ulcers and loss of voice will manifest.

When both the husband and wife are suffering from leprosy, the child produced by them will also be born with leprosy as both the sperm and ovum will have been affected by loprosy. ( 25-30 )

# साध्यं त्वयक्तमांसस्थं वातश्लेष्माधिकं च यत् ।

मेदस्सि द्वन्द्रजं याप्यं वर्ज्य मज्जास्थिसंश्रितम् ॥ ॥ ३१ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. ५ )

Leprosy invading wak, rakta, and māmsa and those varieties which are caused by predominance of vata and kapha are easy to cure, that which invades medas and caused by the combination of two dosas to ether become chronic, and that which invades majjā and asthi is to be refused treatment. ( 31 )

# किमित्रृङदाहमन्दागिसंयुक्तं याश्रिदोषजम् । प्रभिन्नं प्रस्नुताङ्गं च रक्तनैष्ठं इतस्वरम् ॥ ३२ ॥ पञ्चकर्म गुणातीतं कुष्ठं हन्तीह मानवम् ( मु. सू. अ. ३३ )

Presence of worms in the ulcers, thirst, feeling of burning sensation, poor digestive capacity, involvement of all the three dosas together, mutilation and loss of body parts, relness of the eyes, loss of voice and that affecting patients who are unsuitable for the five purificatory treatments-are going to kill the patient. ( 32 )

# वातेन कुष्ठं कापालं पित्तेनौदुम्बरं कफात् ॥ ३३ ॥

# मण्डलाख्यं विचर्ची च ऋष्याख्यं वातपित्तजम् । चम्मैककुष्ठं किटिभं सिध्मालसावपादिकाः ॥ ३४ ॥ वात श्रेष्मोद्भवाः श्लेष्मपित्ताद्दशतारुषो । पुण्डरीकं सविरूफोटं पामा चर्मद्लं तथा ॥ ३५ ॥ सवँः स्यात्काकणं पूर्वश्रिकं दद्र सकाकणम् । पुण्डरीकर्ण्यजिह्ने च महाकुष्ठानि सप्त तु ॥ ३६ ॥

Kapāla kuṣṭh is produced by vāta, udumbara kuṣṭha from pitta; maṇḍala and vicarcika are from kapha; rsyajihva is by vāta and pitu; carmākhya, eka, kitibha, sidhma, alasa, vipādika are caused by vāta aud kapha; From kapha and pitta are produced dadru, satāru, puņdarika, visphoļa, pāmā and carmadaļa, kākaņa is caused by all the three doss together. The first three ( kapāla, udumbara, and mandala ), dadru, kākaņa, puņdarīka, and rsyajihvā-these seven are the Mahākusthas. ( 33-36 )

# कुष्ठैकसंभवं श्वित्रं किलासं वारुणं भवेत । निर्दिष्टमपरिस्रावि विधात्रद्धवसंश्रयम् ॥ ३७ ॥ वाताद्रक्षारुणं पित्तात्तान्रं कमलपत्रवत् । सदाहं रोमविध्वसि कफाछवेतं घनं गर्छ ।। ३८ ।। सकण्डरं क्रमाद्रक्तमांसमेदःस् चादिशेत । वर्णेनैवेदगुभयं कृञ्छूं तच्चोत्तरोत्तरम् ॥ ३९ ॥ ( वा. चि. अ. १४ )

Svitra ( leucoderma ) and Kilasa ( leucolerma with pinkish tinge ) are produced by the same causes as of kustha ( leprosy ); they are non-exudative and tridosaja in origin ( caused by increase of all the three dosas together ). Roughness and pink colour are due to vata, coppery-red like a lotus petal, feeling of burning sensation and loss of hairs are due to pitta; white colour, thickness, feeling of heaviness and itching are due to kapha; involvement of rakta ( blood ), mamsa ( muscles ) and medas ( fat ) causes their respective types of colours and pain. The successive kinds are more dreadful than the preceding ones. ( 37-39 )

# अशुक्लरोमाऽवहुलससंस्रिष्ठमधो नवम् । अनन्निद्रम्घजं साध्यं श्वित्रं वर्ज्यमतोऽन्यथा ॥ ४० ॥ ग्रह्मपाणितलौष्ठेषु जानमध्यचिरन्तनम् । वर्जनीयं विशेषण किल्रासं सिद्धिमिच्छता ।। ४१ ॥ ( वा. नि. अ. १४ )

Svitra ( leucoderma ) in which the hairs are not white; the skin is not thick, patches not joined together, which is of recent origin and that not due to the effects of burns are curable, other kinds such as white patches seen on the genital organs, palms of the hands and soles of the foot and lips, that which has persisted for long time are to be refused treatment by those desirous of success. ( 40-41 )

प्रसङ्गाद्मात्रसंस्पर्शात्तिःश्वासात् सद्दभोजनात् । एकशय्यासनाच्चैव वस्त्रमाल्यानुलैपनात् ॥ ४२ ॥ कुष्ठं उवरश्च शोषश्य नेत्राभिष्यन्द पद्य च । औपसर्गिकरोगाश्च संक्रामन्ति नरान्नरम् ॥ ४३ ॥ ( सु. नि. अ ५ )

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने कुष्ठनिदानं समाप्तम् ।। ४६ ।।

Sexual intercourse, physical contact, contact with expired air, dining together, sleeping on the same bed, common use of clothing, garlands, unguents, etc., are the media of transmission of diseases from one person to another. Leprosy, fevers, tuberculosis, ophthalmias and many other diseases are trausmitted in this manner; hence they are known as Aupasargika rogas ( infectious/contagiousdiseases ). (42-43) Thus ends the chapter on Kustha.

1

# अथ श्रीतपित्तोदर्दकोठनिदानम्

ŠITAPITTA, UDARDA, KOȚHA NIDĀNAM ( SKIN RASHES, ERYTHEMA, ALLERGY, ETC. )

## शीतमा क्तसंस्पर्शात्प्रद्रश्री कफमारुतौ । पित्तेन सद्य संभूय बहिरन्तर्विसर्पतः ।। १ !!

By contact with very cold breeze, kapha and vāta getting increased, associating with pitta also, begin to travel externally ( in the skin ) and internally ( in blood and other tissues ) ( and produce sitapitta ). ( 1 )

# पिपासारुचिद्वल्लासदेहसादाद्गुगौरवम् । रक्तलोचनता तेषां पूर्वरूपस्य लक्षणम् ॥ २ ॥

Premonitary symptoms are-thirst, loss af appetite, oppression in the chest, debility, feeling of heaviness of the body and redness of the eyes. ( 2 )

# षरटीदृष्टसंस्थानः शोधः सञ्जायते बहिः। सकण्डूस्तोदबहुरूदिज्वरविदाद्युवान् ।। ३ ॥ उद्दमिति तं विद्याच्छीतपित्तमथापरे । वाताधिकं शीतपित्तमुद्देस्तु कफाधिकः ॥ ४ ॥

Elevated patches ( rashes ) resembling that produced by the sting of the wasp, associated with severe itching and pricking pain, vomitting, fever and feeling of burning sensation are the characteristic features of Udarda, called as Sitapitta also by others. In sītapitta, vāta is predominant while in udarda, kapha is predominant. ( 3-4 )

सोल्सब्रैश्च सरागैश्च कण्डूमद्भिध्य मण्डलैः । शैशिरः कफजो व्याधिरदर्द इति कीर्तितः ।। ५ ॥

Elevated, reddish, highly irritating, round patches caused by kapha during siśirā rtu ( winter ) is also known as Udarda. ( 5 )

> असम्यग्वमनोदीर्णपित्तर श्लेष्मान्न निग्रहैः । मण्डलानि सकण्डूनि रागवन्ति बहूनि च । उत्कोठः सानुबन्धश्च कोठ इत्यभिधीयते ।। ६ ।।

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदाने शीतपित्तोदर्दकोठनिदानं समाप्तम् ॥ ५० ।।

Incomplete emesis ( improper administration of cmetics and other treatments ), suppression ( obstruction ) of the movement of pitta, kapha and anna { food } lead on to the appearance of innumerable rashes, red in colour, highly itching and relapsing many times. This condition is known as Kotha. ( 6 )

Thus ends the chapter on Sitapitta, etc.

# अथाम्लपित्तनिदानम्

# AMLAPITTA NIDANAM ( HYPERCHLORHYDRIA, GASTRITIS )

# विरुद्ध दुष्टाम्लविदाहि पित्तप्रको पिपानान्न भुजो विद्युग्धम् । पित्ते स्वहेतूपचितं पुरा यत्तदम्लपित्तं प्रवदन्ति सन्तः ।। १ ।।

Indulgence in foods which are of incompatible combinations, spoiled, very sour, those causing huming sensation inside and such other food and drinks which cause increase of pitta, produce Amlapitia in persons in whom prita has already undergone increase; so say the wise. ( 1 )

# अविपाकक्कमोत्कोशतिक्ताम्लोद्रारगौरवैः । हृत्कण्ठदाहार्द्धभिध्याम्ळपित्तं वदेद्धिषक् ॥ २ ॥

、「 The discase in which the patient has indigestion, exhaustion without any exertion, nausca, erectations with bitter or sour tast, feeling of heaviness of the body, burning sensation in the chest and throat and loss of appetite-is to be called as Amlapitta, ( 2 )

#### तृडदाहमूञ्छौ भ्रममोहकारि प्रयात्यधो वा विविधप्रकारम् । हृलुासकोठानलसादृहर्षस्वेदाङ्गपीतत्वकरं कद्याचित् ॥ ३ ॥

Thirst, burning sensation, fainting, gidditiess, delusion, movement downwards of different kinds (of diarthoca) and occasional oppression in the chest, rashes on the skin, poor digestion, horripulations, perspiration and yellowish skin are the symptoms of adhoga>( downward ) am'apitta. ' 3 )

> वान्तं हरित्पीतक्षनीलुकृष्णमारकरक्ताभगतीव चाम्लम् । मांसोद्दकाभं रवा तपिच्छिलाच्छ श्लेष्मानुजानं विविधं रसेन ॥ ४ ॥ भुक्ते विद्युधे त्वथवाऽप्यभुक्ते करोति तिक्ताम्लवमि कदाचित् । उद्गारमेवंविधमेव कण्ठहन्कुरक्षिदाहं शिरसो रुजं च ॥ ५ ॥ करचरणदाहमोण्यं महतीमर्द्धं महतीमर्द्रचं ज्वरं च कर्फापत्तम् । जनयति कण्डूमण्डल पिडकाशतनिचितगाःत्ररोगचयम् ॥ ६ ॥

Symptoms of urdhvaga ( upward ) amlapitta are; vomitting of green, yellow, blue, black, slightly red or bright red coloured very sour materials, resembling mutton wash, very sticky, thin, followed by kapha; vomitting occuring during digestion of food or even on empty stomach, with bitter or sour taste occasionally, belchings also of similar nature, burning sensation in the throat, chest and

upper abdomen, headache, burning sensation in the palms and soles, feeling of great heat, loss of appetite, fever of kaphapittaja type, appearance of rashes, circular, itching, studded with numerous small vesicles on the skin and other troubles of the body. ( 4-6 )

# रोगोऽयमम्लपित्ताख्यो यत्नात् संसाध्यते नवः । चिरोत्थितो भवेद्याप्यः दृष्ठ्छूसाध्यः स कस्याचित् ॥ ७ ॥

This disease amlapitta-which is of recent onset, responds to treatment with difficulty, that which is of long duration will be chronic or curable with difficulty in a few. ( 7 )

सानित्रं सानित्रक्रफं सक्क तद्य लक्ष्येत् । दोषलिङ्गन मतिमान् भिष्ख्योहकरं हि तत् ॥ ८ ॥ स्त्रम्भव स्वास्त्रस्य नि । तमस्तो दर्शनविभ्रमवि । तमसां दर्शनविभ्रमविमोहहर्शार्यानलकोपात् ।।९॥ कफनिष्ठीचनगौरवजडतारुचिशीतसाद्वमिलैपाः । दद्दनबल्रसादकण्डूनिद्राश्चिहं कफानुगते ॥ १० ॥ उभयमिदमेव चिह्नं मारुतकफसंभवे भवत्यम्ले ।

# ( तिक्ताम्लकटुकोद्भारहर्नुः कुक्षिकण्डदाहरुत् ॥ ११ ॥ )

# भ्रमो मूर्च्छार्वरुद्धिरालस्यं च शिरोरुजा । प्रसेको मुखमाधुर्यं श्रुप्मवित्तस्य लक्षणम् ॥ १२ ॥

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचिते माधवनिदानेऽम्तवित्तनिदानं समाप्तम् ।। ५१ ।।

The physician should clearly recognise the symptoms of amlapitta caused by vāta, vātakapha and kapha as they are likely to create confusion to him;

Tremors, delerium, faining, feeling of pins and needles, weakness of the body parts, pain, darkness before the cyes, giddiness, delusion, horripilations are the symptoms caused by vāta.

Expectoration of thick phlegn, feeling of heaviness, lassitude, loss of appetite, coldness, weakness, vomitting, coating on the tongue, burning sensation, loss of strength, itching of the skin and more sleep are the symptoms caused by kapha.

Appearance of both the above symptoms together is seen in amlapitta in which both vāta and kapha are associated.

( Erectations having bitter, sour and pungent tastes, burning sensation in the chest, upper abdomen and throat ), giddiness, fainting, loss of appetite, vomitting, lassitude, headache, salivation, sweet taste in the mouth are the symptoms of amlapitta caused by kapha and pints together. ( 8-12 )

Thus ends the chapter on Amlapitta.

# CHAPTER 52 अथ विसर्पनिद्यनम्

## VISARPA NIDANAM ( ERYSEPELAS ) ... .

# लबणाम्न्त्रकटूष्णादिसंसेवादोषक्कांपतः । विसर्पः सप्तधा ज्ञेयः सर्वतः परिसर्वणात् ॥ १ ॥

Ingestion of too much of saity, sour, pungent and hot foods and such other factors which increase the three dosas produce the seven kinds of visarpa. It is so called because of its nature of spreading all over the body. ( 1 )

# पृथक् त्रयस्त्रिभिश्चैको विसर्या द्वन्द्रज्ञाल्नयः । वातिकः पैद्तिकश्चैव कफजः सान्निपातिकः ॥ २ ॥ चल्वार पते वीसर्पा वक्ष्यन्ते द्वन्द्रज्ञास्त्रयः । आन्नेयो वातापित्ताः यां प्रन्थ्याख्यः कफवातज्ञः ॥ ३ ॥ यस्तु कद्दमको घोरः सः पित्तकफसंभवः । ( च. चि. अ. १४ ) ! '

One from each dosa, one from all three together and three from combination of two dosas, viz., vātaja, pittaja, kaphaja, sannipātaja, agni visarpa from the combination of vāta and pitta; granthi visarpa from kapha and vāta; kardama visarpa which is dreadful from pitta and kapha-are its seven kinds. ( 2-3 )

# रक्तं ल्रसीका त्वज्रांसं दूध्यं दोषास्त्रयो मलाः ।। ४ ।। विसर्पोणां समुत्पत्तौ विज्ञेयाः सद्ग धातवः । ( च. चि. त्र. ११ ) १ :

Rakta ( blood ), lasika ( lymph ), tvak ( skin ), mamsa ( mucles ) are the dusyas ( tissues involved ) and the three dosas together are the seven factors in the causation of visarpa. { 4 }

# तत्र वातात् स वीसर्पो वातज्वरसम्ब्यथः ॥ ५ ॥ शोधस्फुरणनिस्तोदभेदायासातिहर्षवान् । पित्ताद्द्रुतगतिः पित्तज्वरतिङ्गोऽतिलोहितः ।। ६ ।। कफात् कण्डूयुतः स्निग्धः कफञ्चरसमानरुक् । सम्निपातसमुत्थश्च सर्वाल्रुङ्गसमन्वितः ॥ ७ ॥ ( वा. नि. अ. १३ )

Vātaja visarpa has identical symptoms as found in vāta jwara ( fever ) along with swelling; pulsating, pricking or cutting type of pain, tiredness, bodyaches and horripilations.

Pittaja visarpa has quick progress, identical symptoms as those of pittaja jwara and the leison is very red in colour.

Itching and smoothness of the leison and other symptoms of kaphaja jwara will be seen in kaphaja visarpa, presence of all the symptoms will be seen in samipātaja visarpa. ( 5-7 )

वातपित्ताज्ञ्चरच्छदिमुञ्छति।सारतृङ्क्ष्मैः । प्रन्थिमेदाग्निसद्नतमकारोचकैयुंतः ।। ८ ॥ करोति सर्वमङ्गं च दीप्राङ्गारावकीणवत् । यं यं देशों विसर्पत्व विसर्पति भवेत् स सः ॥ ९ ॥ शास्ताङ्गारासितो नीलो रक्तो वाऽऽशु च चीयते । अग्निद्ध्य इत स्फोटैः श्रीघ्रगत्वाद्दुतं स च ।१०॥ मर्मानुसारी विसर्वः स्याद्धातोऽतिवरूस्ततः । व्यथतेऽङ्गं हरेल्संज्ञां निद्रां च श्वासमीरयेत् ॥ ११ ॥ द्विकां च स गतोऽवस्थामीदृशीं लभते न ना । क्राचच्छर्मारतिग्रस्तो भूमिशयासनादिषु ॥ १२ ॥ चेष्टमानस्ततः क्रिष्टो मनोदेहप्रमोद्दवान् । दुष्प्रबोधोऽश्न्ते निद्रां सोऽग्निवीसर्प उच्यते ॥ १३ ॥ ( वा. निः ज. १३ )

Visarpa caused by combination of increased vata and pitta will have the following symptoms; fever, vomitting, fainting, diarrhoea, thirst, giddiness, pain in the joints, poor digestion, darkness before the eyes, loss of appetite, the patient feels as though his body and specially the affected part is in contact with burning coal, the skin becomes black, blue or red, blebs develop quickly an l leison spreads to other places fast, gets located in vital spots ( organs ). In view of predominance of vata, the affected part is very painful, the patient loses consciousness and sleep, develops difficulty in breathing, hiccough, does not find even slight relief by lying on the ground or any other posture; movement of any kind is extremely difficult and the person goes in for sleep from which he cannot be aroused again ( dies ). This disease is known as Agnivisarpa. ( 8-13 )

# कफेन रुद्धः पवनो भिरवा तं बहुधा कफम् । रक्तं वा वृद्धरक्तस्य त्वक्सिरास्त्रायुमांसगम् ॥ १४ ॥ दूषयित्वा तु दोर्घाणुवृत्तर्भूलस्वराम् । ग्रन्थीनां कुक्ते मालां सरक्तां तीवरुण्वराम् ॥ १५ ॥ श्वासकासातिसारास्यशोषहिकावमिभ्रमैः । ( वा. नि. अ. १३ ) मोहवैवर्ण्यमूच्छाङ्गभद्गाग्निसदनैर्युताम् ॥ १६ ॥ इत्ययं य्रन्थिवीसर्वः कफमारुतकोपजः । ( वा. नि. अ. १२ )

Vata getting obstructed by kapha or by increased rakta ( blood ), associating with kapha, invades the skin, veins, tendons and muscles and produces chains of tumors, elongated small, round, hard, rough, red in colour and very painful; associated with fever, difficulty in breathing, cough, diarrhoea, dryness of the mouth, hiccough, vomitting, giddiness, delusion, discolouration, fainting, pain all over the body and poor digestion. This disease caused by increse of kapha and vāta is called as Granthi visarpa. ( 14-16 )

कफपिल्ताउज्वरः स्तम्भो निद्रा तन्द्रा शिरोरुजा ।। १७ ।। अद्भावसादविक्षेपौ प्रलेपारोचकभ्रमाः । मुच्छी ग्रिहानिर्मेदोऽस्थ्नां पिपासेन्द्रियगौरवम् ॥ १८ ॥ आमोपवेशन लेपः स्रोतसां स च सर्वति । प्रायेणामाशयं गृहन्नवकदेशं न चातिरुक ॥ १९ ॥ पिडकैरबर्काणों प्रतिपीतलोहितपाण्डूरैः । स्त्रिग्धोऽसितो मेचकाभो महिनः शोधवान् गुरुः ।। २० ॥ गम्भीरपाकः प्राज्योष्मा स्पष्टः क्लिन्नोऽवदीयते । ( वा. नि. अ. १२ )

# पड्ढूवच्छीर्णमांस्क्य स्पष्टक्कायुसिरागणः ॥ २१ ॥ ( वा. नि. अ. १३ ) शवगन्धी च वीसर्पः कर्दमाख्यमुशन्ति तम् ।

Visarpa caused by kapha and pitta together will be having the following features; fever, stiffness of the body parts, sleep, stupor, headache, weakness, involuntary movement of the body parts, coating of the tongue, loss of appetite, giddiness, fainting, loss of digestive function, pain in the bones, thirst, dullness of the sense organs, elimination of inadequately processed feces, coating of thick material in the channels ( orifices, tis-ue spaces, cell pores, etc. ), the leison mostly invades the stomach and presents mild pain; it spreads by forming small tumors, which are of deep yellow, red, white, black or blue black in colour, smooth, dirty, swollen, heavy, forming pus inside, very hot, bursts out discharging the pus, the muscles having become semi-liquid like silt, falls off exposing the network of tendons and veins and emitting cadaveric smell. This kinds is known as Kardama visarpa. ( 17-21 )

# बाह्यहेतोः क्षतात् कृद्धः सरक्तं पित्तमीरयन् ॥ २२ ॥ ( वा. नि. अ. १३ ) वीसर्प मारतः कुर्यात्कुलत्थसदशैश्वितम् । स्फोटैः शोधज्वररुजादाद्याद्यं श्यावशोणतम् ॥ २३ ॥

Another variety of visarpa caused by ksata ( external injury ) which causes increase of rakta and pitta alond with vata, will have the following features; vesicles of the size of horsegram appear all over the body accompanied with swelling, fever, pain, burning sensation and discharge of blue coloured blood. ( 22-23 )

# ज्वरातिसारी वमधुस्त्वस्यांसदरणं क्रमः । ( सू. नि. अ. १० ) अरोचकाविपाकों च विसर्पाणामुपद्रवाः ॥ २४ ॥

The complications of visarpa are fever, diarrhoca, vomitting, depletion of skin and muscles, exhaustion, loss of appetite and indigestion. ( 24 )

# सिध्यन्ति वातकफपित्तकृता विसर्पोः सर्वात्मकः क्षतकृतश्च न सिद्धिमेति । पित्तात्मकोऽञ्जनवपुत्र्य मवेदसाध्यः कृच्छ्राश्च मर्मेसु भवन्ति हि सर्व एव ॥ २५ ॥

्ति श्रीमध्यवद्गविरचित्रं माधवनिद्यानं समाप्तम् ।। १२ भ

Visarpas produced by any one dosa-vata, kapha and pitta are curable. The tridosaja and ksataja do not respond to trcatment, that produced by pitta and that which makes the body black like eyesalve arc incurable and those appearing on vital spots or organs are difficult to cure. ( 25 )

Thus ends the chapter on Visarpa.

# अथ विस्फोटनिद्यनम्

VISPHOTA NIDANAM ( SMALL POX )

# क दम्लती क्रणोष्णाचिदा द्विरूक्षक्षार र जीर्णाध्यश्र नातपैश्च । तथर्तदोषण विपयेण कुर्व्यन्ति दोषाः पवनादयस्त्र II १ II त्वचमाश्रित्य ते रक्तमांसास्थीनि प्रदूष्य च । घोरान् कुवन्ति विस्फोटान् सर्वान् उवरपुर:सरान् ।। २ ।।

Over-indulyence in foods which are pungent, sour, heatproducing, hot, causing burning sensation during digestion, dry, alkaline, uncooked and in excess quantity, exposure to sun-light, effects of seasons which are normal or abnormal-cause the vata and other dosas to undergo increase, which in turn invade the skin, blood, muscles and bones, and produce vesicles on the skin, preceded by fever. ( 1-2 )

# अग्निद्यधानिभाः स्फोटाः सञ्चरा रक्तपित्तजाः । कचित्र सर्वत्र वा देहे विरफोटा इति ते स्मृताः ॥ ३ ॥ ( मृ. नि. अ. १२ )

Vesicles resembling hoils formed by the touch of fire, accompanied invariably with fever, symptoms of increase of rakta and pitta, in some or all the parts of the body-this discase is known as Visphota. ( 3 )

# शिरोरुकशूलिमूर्ति ज्वरम्तृट पर्यनेदनम् । सकृष्णवर्णता चेति वातविस्फोटलक्षणम् ॥ ४ ॥ ज्यरदाहरुजास्त्रायपाकृतप्णाभिरन्वितम् । पीतलोहितवर्ण च व पित्तविस्फोटलक्षणम् ॥ ५ ॥ छर्द्यरोचकजाड्यानि कण्डूकाठिन्यपाण्डुताः । अवेदनश्चिरात्पाकी स विस्फोटः कफात्मकः ।। ६ ।। बातपित्तकृतो यस्त्रु कुरुते तीववेदनाम । कण्डूस्तैमित्यगुरुमिर्जानीयात्कफवातिकम् ॥ ७ ॥ कण्डूर्दाहो उचरञ्छदिरेतैस्नु कफपौत्तिकः ।

Headache, severe pain, fever, thirst, pain in the joints and vesicles black in colour are the symptoms of vataja visphota

Fever, burning sensation, pain, exudation of pus, formation thirst, and vesicles appear as ye low or red in colour are the features of pittaja visphota.

Vomitting, loss of appetite, lassitude, vesicles having itching, hardness, white colour, wit: no pain and pus formation very slowly are the symptoms of kaphaja visphoța.

Vatapittaja variety causes severe pain, vātakaphaja variety has itching, hardness and tightness, kaphapittaja variety has itching, burning sensation, fever and vomitting. ( 4-7 )

# मध्ये निम्नोन्नतोऽन्ते कठिनोऽरुपप्रपाकवान् ॥ ८ ॥ दाहरागतुषामोहच्छदिमूचर्छारुजान्वराः । प्रलापो वेपथुस्तन्द्रा सोऽसाध्यः स्याच्चिदोषज्ञः ॥ ९ ॥

Vesicles with depression in the centre and elevation in their edges, hard, with ittle quantity of pus, with burning sensation and reduess, accompanied with thirst, delusion, vomitting, fainting, pain, fever, delerium, chills andstupor are the features of tridosaja visphota and it is incurable. ( 8-9 )

# रक्ता रक्तसम्र्त्थाना गुञ्जाविद्यमस्तुन्नु रक्तेन पैक्तिकेन च हेतुना ॥ १० ॥ न ते सिद्धि समायान्ति सिद्धैयोगशतैर्रपि ।

Visphota caused by rakta will have vesicles which are red resembling guitja seed ( Abrusprecatorius ) or coral bead and all the symptoms of increase of pitta; it loes not respond to treatment even by hundreds of efficient recipes. ( 10 )

# एकदोषोत्थितः साध्यः कृच्छस्ताध्यो द्विदोषज्ञः II सर्वद्राषोरियतो घोरस्त्वसाध्यो भूयपद्रवः ॥ ११ ॥

्ति थीमान्नवकरविरचित्रं मान्नवनिद्याने विस्फोटनिदानं समाप्तम् ॥ ४३ ॥

Visphota caused by single dosa is curable, that by two dosas together is curable with difficulty and that caused by all the three dosas and that with complications are incurable. ( 11 )

Thus ends the chapter on Visphota.

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

## अथ मनुरिकानिदानम्

#### MASŪRIKA NIDĀNAM ( CHICKEN POX )

# कटम्लल्क्लबणसारविरुद्धाध्यशनाशनैः । दृष्टुनिष्पावशाका द्यैः प्रद्रष्टपवनोद्दकैः ॥ १ ॥ करण्रहेक्षणा घापि देशे दोषाः समुद्धताः । जनयन्ति शरीरेऽस्मिन् दुष्टरकेन सङ्गताः ।। २ ।। मसूराहृतिसंस्थानाः पिडकाः स्युर्मसूरिकाः ।

Over-indulgence in foods which are pungent, sour, salty, alkaline; of incompatible nature, excess quantity, too frequently or not taking any food at all; ingestion of contaminated beaus, leafy vegetables, air and water; and bad planetary influences on certain arcas of the country ( in the community of people ) cause an increase of the dosas in the body which, in association with, bau-blood, produce cruptions ( on the body ) resembling masura ( lentils ) in shape and size. ( 1-2 )

# तासां पर्वे ज्वरः कण्डूर्गान्त्रभक्तोऽरतिर्भ्रमः ।। ३ ॥ त्वचित्रोथः संवैवण्यों नैत्ररागश्च जायते ।

This disease known as Masūrikā will have, in its premonitory symptoms; fever, itching, pain all over the body, restlessness, giddiness, swelling and discolouration of the skin and redness of the eyes. ( 3 )

# स्फोटाः इयावारुणा रूक्षास्तीव्रवेदनयाऽन्वताः ॥ ४ ॥ कृतिनाश्चिरपाकाश्च भवन्त्यनिल्नसंभवाः । सन्ध्यस्थिपर्वणां भेदः कासः कम्पोऽरतिः क्लमः ॥ ५ ॥ शोषस्तास्वोष्टजिह्वानां तृष्णा नार्राचन्नायुता ।

Eruptions, black or crimson in colour, rough, very painful, bard, forming pus very slowly, pain in the joints, cough, shivering, restlessness, exhaustion, dryness of the palate, lips and tongue, thirst and loss of appetite are the symptoms of masurika caused by vata. ( 4-5 )

# रक्ताः पीतस्मिताः स्फोटाः सदाह्यस्तीववेदनाः ॥ ६ ॥ भवन्त्यचिरपाकाश्च पित्तकोपसमुद्भवाः । विड्भेदश्चाद्गमद्दश्च दाहस्तृष्णाऽर्श्वस्तथा ।। ७ ॥ मुखपाकोऽक्षिरागश्च ज्वरस्तीवः सुदारुणः ।

Red, yellowish white eruptions, with severe burning sensation and pain, forming pus quickly, diarrhoea, pain all over the body, burning sensation, thirst, loss of appetite, ulceration of the mouth, redness of eyes, very high fever and great distress are the symptoms of masūrikā caused by pitta. ( 6-7 )

# रक्तजायां भवन्त्येते विकाराः पित्तलक्षणाः ॥ ८ ॥

In raktaja masūrikā ( that produced by bad blood ) the symptoms similar to those of pittaja type will be seen. ( 8 )

# कफप्रसेकः स्त्रीमित्यं शिरोरुग्गात्रगौरवम् । हृद्यासः सार्राचनिद्रा तन्द्रालयसम्बताः ॥ ९ ॥ श्वेताः स्निग्धाः भृशं स्थूलाः कण्ड्वराः मन्द्वेदनाः । मसूरिका: कफोर्त्याश्र चिरपाका: प्रवीर्तिता: 11 १० 11

In kaphaja masūrikā, the symptoms arc-watery discharge ( f om the mouth, nose and eyes ), inactivity, headache, feeling of heaviness of the body, nausea, loss of appetite, sleep, stupor and lassitude, the eruptions are white, soft, big in size, itching, slightly painful and forming pus slowly. ( 9-10 )

# नीलाश्चिपिटविस्तीर्णा मध्ये निम्ना महारजः । चिरपाकाः प्रभूताः सर्वदोषज्ञः ॥ ११ ॥ कण्ठरोधारुचिस्तम्भप्रतापारतिसंयुताः । दुश्चिकित्स्याः समुद्दिष्टाः पिडकाश्यर्मसंब्रिताः ॥ १२ ॥

Eruptions blue in colour, flat, broad, depressed in their centre ( elevated at their edges ), highly painful, forming pus slowly, exuding pus of offensive smell in large quantity, "obstruction in the throat, loss of appetite, stiffness of the body, delerium and restlessness; this condition known as Carmapidaka, caused by all the three dosas together, is difficult to treat. ( 11-12 )

# रोमकूपोन्नतिसमा रागिण्यः कर्फापन्तजाः । कासारोचकसंयुक्ता रोमान्त्यो ज्वरपूर्विकाः ॥ १३ ॥

Small, red eruptions at the roots of the hairs making slight elevations, associated with cough, loss of appetite and preceded by fever are known as Romantika ( measles ) and caused by increase of kapha and pitta. ( 13 )

तोयवुद्बुद्सङ्काशास्त्वगतास्तु मसूरिकाः । स्वरुपदोषाः प्रजायन्ते भिन्नास्तोयं स्नवन्ति च ॥ १४ ॥ रक्तस्था लोहिताकाराः श्रीघ्रपाकास्तनुत्वचः । साध्या नात्यर्थदुष्टाश्च मिन्ना रक्तं स्वर्धन्तु च ॥ १५ ॥ म् सस्थाः कठिनाः स्निग्धाश्चिरपाका घनत्वचः । गात्रशुलतुपाकण्ड्वज्वरारतिसमन्विताः ॥ १६ ॥ मेदोजा मण्डलाकारा मृदवः किंचिदुन्नताः । घोरञ्चरपरीताश्च स्थूलाः स्निग्धाः सर्वेदनाः ॥ १७ ॥ संमोहारतिसंतापाः कश्चिदान्यो विनिस्तरेत् । क्षुद्रा गात्रसमा रूक्षाश्चिपिटाः किंचिद्युत्ताः ॥ १८ ॥ मज्जोत्था भृशसंमोहृदेदनारतिसंयुताः । छिन्दन्ति मर्मधामानि प्राणानाश्रु हरन्ति हि ।। १९ ॥ भ्रमरेणेव विद्धानि कुर्वन्त्यस्थीनि सर्वतः । पक्काभाः पिडकाः स्त्रिग्धाः सूक्ष्माश्यात्यर्थवेदनाः ॥ २० ॥ स्तैमित्यारतिसंमोहदाहोन्माद्समन्विताः । शुक्रज्ञायां मसूर्यो तु लक्ष्णानि भवन्ति द्वि ॥ २१ ॥ निर्दिष्टं केवलं चिह्नं दृश्यते न तु जीवितम् । दोषमिश्रास्तु सम्रैता द्रृष्ठया दोषलक्ष्णः ॥ २२ ॥ ्त्वग्गता रक्तजाश्वैव पित्तजाः श्लेष्मजास्तथा । श्लेष्मपित्तकृताश्रैव सुखसाध्या मसूरिकाः ॥ २३ ॥

If masurika is localised in the skin ( affecting the rasa dhātu ) the cruptions will be like bubbles of water, exude thin watery fluid on pricking and will have symptoms of mild increase of dosas.

If it is localised in the rakta ( blood ), the cruptions will be red, forms pus quickly, skin is thin and the abnormality of dosas is more and cruptions bleed heavily on pricking. Thus it is difficult to cure,

If it invades mamsa ( muscles ), the eruptions are hard, greasy, form pus quickly, skin is thick, associated with pain all over the body, thirst, itching, fever and restlessness

If it invades medas ( fat ) the eruptions are in round patches, soft, slightly elevated, large, greasy, painful and accompanied with very high fever, delusion, restlessness, and distress; few persons will get over these conditions ( easily ) . ~ '

If it invades majjā ( marrow ) the eruptions are small, of the same level of the skin, rough, flat or slightly elevated and associated with severe delusion, pain pro di th and restlessness.

If it invades asthi ( bone ), there will be cutting pain in yital organs cndangering life, and pain in the bones as though stung by bees. 3 ' '

If it invades sukra ( semen ) the eruptions are ripe, waxy, small in size, very painful, associated with stiffness of the body, restlessness, delusion, burning sensation and toxicity and the patient may soon lose his life. /

The above seven types might also be having the symptoms of one or more of the increased dosas which are to be recognised.

Masurika which resides in the skin and those caused by rakta, pitta and kapha separately and kaphapitta together are easy to cure. ( 14-23 )

# ञ्चात्ज्ञा वातपित्तौत्थः श्रोष्मवातकृताश्च याः । कृच्छूसाध्यतमास्तस्माद्यत्नादेता उपाचरेन् ॥ २४ ॥ यसाध्याः सन्निपातोत्थास्तासां वक्ष्यामि लक्षणम् । प्रवालसदृशाः काश्चित् काश्चित्रम्बूफलोपमाः ।। २५ ।। लोहजालसमाः काश्चिद्रतसीफलसंनिभाः । आसां बहुविधा वर्णा जायन्ते दोषभेद्दः ॥ २६ ॥

Those by vāta, vātapitta, kaphavāta are very difficult to cure and so are to be treated with great effort and care.

Those caused by increase of all the three dosas together are incurable, their symptoms are the following : eruptions resembling in colour the coral beads, some like fruits of jambu ( Eugeina jambolana ) some like an iron seive, some like fruits of atasi ( linseed ) and many other colours due to admixture of dosas. ( 24-26 )

# कासो हिक्का प्रमेहृध्य ज्वरस्तीवः सुदारुणः । प्रलापश्चारतिर्मूच्छा तृष्णा दृढोऽतिमूर्णता ॥ २० ॥ मुखेन प्रस्वेद्रक्तं तथा घाणेन चक्षुषा। कण्ठे घुर्घुरकं कृत्वा श्वसित्यत्यर्थवेदनम् ॥ २८ ॥ मसूरिकासिमूतस्य यस्यैतानि भिषग्वरैः । लक्षणानि च हृद्यन्ते न दद्यादश्र मेषजम् ॥ २९ ॥

Cough, hiccough, polyureal frequent micturition, high fevere, severe delerium, restlessness, fainting, thirst, burning sensation, curvatures of the body, bleeding

.

through mouth, nose and eyes, cooing in the throat with severe difficulty sound in breathing-if these symptoms are noticed in a patient of masuriks, he should very not be a lministered medicine ( but to be refuse ! ). ( 27-29 )

#### मसूरिकाभिभूतो यो भृशं घ्याणेन निःश्वसंस्त् । 11 - 1 -स भूशं त्यजनि प्राणान् तृषातों वायुदूषितः ।। ३० ।।

The patient of masurika who is breathing only expiration in its ... . having very shallow breathing ), who is having severe thirst and who is having profital. I symptoms of vāta-are going to die soon. ( 30 )

# मसूरिकान्ते शोधः स्यात् कूर्यरे मणिबन्धको । तथांऽसफलक चापि दुश्चिकित्थः सुदारुणः ।। ३१ ॥

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरचित्र माधवनिदाने म्मूरिकानिद्यान ममासम् । ५४

A profound swelling in the elbow, wrist and shoulder joints might develop in the last stages of masurika and this is also incurable. ( 31 )

Thus ends the chapter on Masūrikā.

# अथ क्षुद्ररोगनिदानम्

## KȘUDRA ROGA NIDĀNAM ( MINOR DISEASES )

# स्त्रिग्धाः सवर्णा ग्रशिता नोरुजो मुद्रसंनिभाः । कफवातोत्थिता श्रीया बालानामजगलिकाः ।। १ ।। ( सु. नि. अ. १३ )

A disease characterised by appearance of waxy, non-discoloured, painless papules (on the skin) of the size of green-gram caused by increase of kapha and vāta together, specially in children, is known as Ajagallika. ( 1 )

## यवाकारा सुकाठिना प्रशिता मांससंधिता । पिडका कफवाताभ्यां यवप्रख्येति सोच्यते ॥ २ ॥ ( मु. नि. ब. १३ )

Pidaka ( papules ) of the size of barley, hard, localised in the muscle, caused by kapha and vāta together is known as Yavaprakhyā. ( 2 )

#### घनामवन्त्रां पिडकामुन्ननां परिमण्डलाम् । अन्त्राल्त्रजीमरूपपूर्यां तां विद्यात्कफवातजाम् ॥ ३ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १३ )

A hard pidaka ( papule ) without an opening, round in shape with little quaatity of pus inside caused by kapha and vāta together is known as Antrālajī ( or Andhālajī to be more appropriate ). ( 3 )

# विवृतास्यां महादाद्दां पक्कोदुम्बरसंनिभाम् । विद्यृतामिति तां विद्यारिपत्तोत्थां परिमण्डलाम् ॥ ४ ॥ ( सु. नि. अ. १३ )

That which is having wide opening, of the size of a ripened udumbara fruit ( Ficus glomerata ), round in shape associated with severe burning sensation and produced by pitta is known as Vivrta. ( 4 )

# य्रथिताः पञ्च वा पड्वा दारुणाः ऋच्छपोपमाः । कफानिलाभ्यां पिडका ज्ञेयाः कञ्चर्ज्यिका बुधैः ॥ ५ ॥ ( सु. नि. अ. १३ )

Five or six pidakas ( papules ), hard, resembling the shell of a tortoise caused by kapha and vāta together is named as Kacchapikā by the wise. ( 5 )

# श्रीवांसकक्षाकरपाददेशे सन्धौ गले वा विभिरेव दोषैः । प्रन्थः स वल्मीकवदकियाणां जातः क्रमेणैव गतः प्रवृद्धिम् ॥ ६ ॥ मुखरनेकैः स्त्रतितोदवन्द्रिविसर्पवत्सर्वति चोघतायैः । ववसीकमाहुर्मिषजो विकारं निष्प्रस्यनीकं चिरजं विशेषात् ॥ ७॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १३ )

A granthi ( tumor ) resembling an ants' hill, gradually increasing in size, appearing at the neck, shoulders, axillae, hands, feet, joints or throat, caused by the increase of all the three dosas together having many mouths discharging fluid; painful, spreading to other parts like visarpa ( crysepelas ), with pointed sprouts is named by the wise physicians as Valmika. This is incurable, especially if chronic. ( 6-7 )

# पद्मकरणिकवन्मध्ये पिडकासिः समाचिताम् । इन्द्रविद्धां तु तां विद्याद्वातपित्तोत्थितां भिषक ॥ ८ ॥ ( मू. नि. अ. १३ )

Small pidakas ( papules ) resembling the central part of a lotus flower, caused by vāta and pitta together is known to physicians as Indraviddhā. ( 8 )

# मण्डलं वृत्तमुल्सन्नं सरक्तं पिडकाचितम् । रुजाकरीं गर्दभिकां तां विद्याद्वातपित्तजाम् ॥ ९ ॥

Round, elevated, red mandalas { patches }, studded with small pidakās ( papules ) causing pain is known as Gardhabhikā and is due to vātapitta. ( 9 )

# वातश्लेष्मसमुद्भूतः श्वयशुर्हेनुसन्धिजः । स्थिरो मन्दर्श्जः स्निन्धो क्षेयः पाषाणगर्द्भः ॥ १० ॥

A sotha ( swelling ) which is hard, slightly painful and greasy, appearing at the joint of the lower jaw caused by vātakapha is known as Pāṣaṇagardabha. ( 10t)

# कर्णस्याभ्यन्तरे जातां पिडकामुग्रवेदनाम् । रिथरां पनसिकां तां तु विद्याद्वात्त्रकोरिथताम् ।। ११ ॥

A pidakā ( papule ) inside the ear, hard and very painful produced by vātakapha together is known as Panasika. ( 11 )

# विसर्पवत्सर्पति यः शोधस्तनुरपाकवान् । दाहच्चरकरः पित्तात्स क्वेयो जालगर्दभः ॥ १२॥

A thin swelling which spreads quickly like visarpa, without the formation of pus or an ulcer but causing burning sensation and fever, due to increase of pitta is known as Jalagardabha. ( 12.)

## पिडकामुत्तमा कृस्थां वृत्तामुग्ररुजाज्वराम् । सर्वात्मिकां सर्वालिङ्गा जानीयादिरिवेलिकाम् ॥ १३ ॥

A round pidaka ( papule ) on the head with severe pain, fever and other symptoms of increase of all the three dosas is known as Inivellika. ( 13 )

## बाहु पाश्वीसकक्षेषु कृष्णस्फोटां सवेदनाम् ।

## पित्तप्रकोपसंभूतां कक्षामित्यभिनिर्दिजोत् ॥ १४ ॥ ( मृ. नि. अ. १३ )

Black coloured, painful sphota ( vesicle ) appearing in the sides of the arms, shoulders or axillae, due to increase of pitta is known as Kakşa. ( 14 )

# पकामेतादृशी दृष्ट्वा पिडकां स्फोटसंनिमाम् । त्वग्गतां पिनकोपेन गन्धमालां प्रचक्षते ॥ १५॥

A similar ( vesicle ) manifesting superficially on the skin which bursts out and caused by increase of pitta is called Gandhamala. ( 15 )

# कक्षभागेषु ये स्फोटा जायन्ते मांसदारणाः । अन्तर्दाहरुवरकर दीप्रपावकसंनिभाः ॥ १६ ॥ सम्बद्धाः दशाहाद्वा पक्षाद्रा द्वन्ति मानवम् । तामग्निरोहिणों विद्यादसाध्यात्राणित्तः ए ।। १० ।।

Sphotas ( vesicles ) developing in the axillac, which burrow into the muscles and destroy them, associated with severe burning sensation inside the body, fever as though in contact with fire, which kills the parient within seven, twelve or fifteen days is known as Agnirohini - It is caused by increase of all the three dosas and is incurable. ( 16-17 )

# नखमांसमधिष्ठाय वायुः पित्तं च देहिनाम् । कुर्वते दाहपाको च तं व्याधि चिप्पमादिशेत् ॥ १८ ॥ तदेवाल्पतरैद्रों : परुषं कुनखं वदेत् ॥ १९ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १३ )

Vata and pitta together undergoing increase, get localised in the muscles surrounding nails and produce the bunning sensation and formation of pus. This disease is known as Cippa; a similar condition is Kunakha which is caused by mild increase of dosas. ( 18-19 )

# गम्भीरामब्र्यसंरम्भां सवर्णानुपरिस्थिताम् । पादस्यानुशयीं तां तु विद्यादन्तःप्रपाकिनीम् ॥ २० ॥ ( मू. नि. अ. १३ )

A mild swelling on the upper portion of the foot, developing slowly, without any discolouration of the skin and producing pus from deep inside is known as Anuśayī. ( 20 )

# विदारीकन्दवहत्ता कक्षावड्वणसन्धिषु । विदारिका भवेद्रका सर्वज्ञा सर्वलक्षणा ॥ २१ ॥

A round papule manifesting at the axillae or groins resembling vidarikanda ( Peuraria-tuberosa ) caused by and presenting the symptoms of all the three dogas together is known as Vidārikā. ( 21 )

# प्राण्य मांससिरास्नायूः श्रेष्मा मेदस्तथाऽनिलः । ग्रन्थि करोत्यसौ भिन्नो मधुसर्पिर्वेसानिभम् ॥ २२ ॥ स्रवत्यास्त्रायमनिन्द्रस्तेत्र वृद्धि गतः पुनः । मांसं संशोध्य ग्रशितां भारतीय मधुसायचसनितमम दुर्गन्धि क्लिम्नमत्यर्थं नानावर्ण ततः सिराः ।

स्रवन्ति रक्तं सहसा नं विद्याच्छर्करार्युद्म् ॥ २४ ॥ ( मु. नि अ. १३ )

Kapha, medas and vāta undergoing increase invade the māmsa, sirās and snāyus and produce a granthi ( tumor ), which produces an exudate resembling honey, ghee or muscle fat. Vata, in course of time, dries up the muscles and produces sand like particles. The veins discharge foul-smelling blood of different colours, instantly. This discase is known as Sarkararbuda. ( 22-24 )

#### वायुरत्यर्थरूपयोः । परिक्रमणशीलस्य पादयोः कुरुते दारीं पाददारीं तमादिशेत् ॥ २५ ॥ ( गु. नि. अ. १३ )

In persons who are in the habit of too much ( on rough ground without footwear, etc. ) vata getting increased produces fissures in the soles of the feet. This disease is called as Pādadārī. ( 25 )

# शर्करोन्मश्रिते पादे क्षते वा कण्टकादिभिः । ग्रन्थिः कोलवदुत्सन्नो जायते कदरं हि तत् ॥ २६ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १३ )

Treding on rough stones or on thorns causing a wound on the soles gradually This condition is known as Kadara. ( 26 )

# क्रिम्नाङ्गल्यन्तरौ पादौ कण्डूदाहरुजान्वितौ । दुष्कद्वमसंस्पर्शादल्रसं तं तं विभावयेत् ॥ २७ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १३ )

Alasa is a disease characterised by exudating ulcers, between the toes with itching, burning sensation and pain caused by contact with contaminated slush. ( 27 )

> रोमकूपानुगं पित्तं वातेन सह मूर्च्छितम् । प्रच्यावयति रोमाणि ततः श्लेष्मा संशोणितः ।। २८ ।। रुणद्धि रोमकूपांस्तु ततोऽन्येषामसंभवः । तदिन्द्रलुप्तं खाल्रित्यं रुह्मेति च विभाव्यते ।। २९ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १३ )

Pitta present at the root of the hairs, getting increased in association with vata, makes for falling of the hairs. Then kapha and rakta together block the follicles of the hairs not allowing fresh ones to grow. This condition is known as Indralupta, Khālitya or Ruhyā. ( 28-29 )

# दारुणा कण्डुरा रूक्षा केशभूमिः प्रपाट्यते । कफमारुतकोपेन विद्याद्दारुणकं तु तम् ॥ ३० ॥ ( सु. नि. अ. १३ )

The skin on the scalp gets cracked, becomes rough, dry and itching, due to increase of kaph-vāta together. This is known as Dāruņaka. ( 30 )

> अरूंषि बहुवक्राणि बहुकोदीनि मूर्श्वि तु । कफास्विकसिकोपेन नृणां विद्यादरू विद्यादरू धिकाम् ॥ ३१ ॥ ( नृ. नि. अ. १३ )

Small ulcers with many openings manifesting on the skin of the scalp due to kapha-rakta and infection by bacteria is called Arümsika. ( 31 )

> क्रोधशोकश्रमकृतः शरीरोष्मा शिरोध्मा शिरोगतः । पित्तं च केशान् पचति पलितं तेन जायते ।। ३२ ॥ ( नु. नि. अ. १३ )

The heat produced by frequent bouts of anger, grief and physical strain, reach the head and makes the hairs to become grey. This condition is known as Palita. ( 32 )

# शाल्मलीकण्टकप्रख्याः कफमारुतरक्तज्ञाः । युवानपिडका यूनां विज्ञेया मुखद्रूषिका: 11 ३३ 11 ( सु. नि. अ. १३ )

Papules, resembling the sprouts on the bark of salmali tree { Bombax malabaricum ) appearing on the face of adolescents caused by increase of kapha, vata and rakta together is known as Yuvanapidaka which makes the face ugly. ( 33 )

# कण्टकैराचितं वृत्तं मण्डलं पाण्डुकण्डुरम् । पश्चिनीकण्टकप्रस्ख्यैस्तदाख्यं कफवातजम् ॥ ३४ ॥ ( सु. नि. अ. १३ )

Circular, white patches, studded with papules, with itching, caused by increase of kapha and vāta together is called Padminikaņṭaka. ( 34 )

# सम्मत्सन्नमरुजं मण्डलं कफरक्तजम् । सहज तक्ष्म चैकेषां लक्ष्यो जनुमणिस्तु सः 11 ३५ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १३ )

A congenital, slightly clevated, even and painless patch on the skin caused by kapha and rakta is known as Laksma; also Jatumani by others. ( 35 )

# अवेदनं स्थिरं चैच यस्मिन् गान्ने प्रदृष्ठयते । माधवरकृष्णमुत्सन्नम्निल्डान्मणको तु तत् ।। ३६ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १३ )

Painless, immovable, black, round nodules on the skin resembling blackgram is called Maṣaka and is caused by vāta. ( 36 )

## क्रणानि तिलमात्राणि नीरुजानि समानि च । वातपित्तककोच्छोषात्तान्विद्यास्तिलकालकान् ॥ ३७ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १३ )

Black, painless spots resembling sesamum seeds, not raised above the level of theskin is known as Tilakālaka and is caused by vāta, pitta and kapha together. ( 37 )

# महद्वा यदि वा चाल्पं ध्यावं वा यदि वाऽसितम् ।

नीरुजं मण्डलं गात्रे न्यच्छमित्यभिधीयते ॥ ३८ ॥ ( सु. नि. अ. १३ )

Broad or small, blue or black, painless patches on skin is known as Nyaccha. ( 38 )

# क्रोधायासप्रकुपितो वायुः पित्तेन संयुतः । मुखमागत्य सहस्रा मण्डलं विस्वजत्यतः ॥ ३९ ॥ नीरूज तनुकं द्यावं मुखे व्यङ्कं तमादिशेत् । ( मु. नि. अ. १३ )

Vata undergoing increase, in association with pitta, due to anger and physical exertion, gets localised in the face producing painless, thin, black patches on the skin. This condition is known as Vyanga. ( 39 )

# कृष्णमेवंड्डुणं गात्रे मुखे वा नीलिकां विदु: !! ४० ॥ ( सु. ति. अ. १३ )

Similar black patches appearing in other parts of the body is known as Nilikā. ( 40 )

# मर्दनात् पीडनाद्याऽति तथैवाप्यभिघाततः । मेढूचर्म यदा वायुर्भजते सर्वतश्वरन् ॥ ४१ ॥ तदा वातोपस्यूष्टत्वात्तर्थमं परिवर्तते ।

# मणेरधस्तात् कोशश्च ग्रन्थिरूपेण लम्बते ।। ४२ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १३ ) सरजां वातसंभूतां तां विद्यात् परिवर्तिकाम् । सकण्डूः कठिना चापि सैव श्रेष्मसमुद्धिता ॥ ४३ ॥

By indulgence in hard friction or squeezing or by injury, the skin on the penis becomes upturned by the action of vata, hangs like a sac under the glans, causing pain if vata is predominant and itching and hardness if kapha is predominary wathis is known as Parivartikā. ( 41-43 )

# अल्पीयःखां यदा हर्षाद्बलाद्रच्छेत् स्त्रियं नरः । द्वस्ताभिघातादृपि वा चर्मण्युद्वतिते बलात् ॥ ४४ ॥ ( यु. नि. अ. १३ ) यस्यावपाद्यते चर्म तां विद्यादवपाटिकाम् । वातोपस्यु मेढ़े चै चर्म संश्रयते मणिम ॥ ४५ ॥

By forceable coitus with a woman heving narrow vagina or by hard masturbation by hand, the skin on the glans penis is forced upwards and gets torn. This condition is known as Avapätikā. ( 41-1 )

# मणिश्चर्मोपनद्धस्तु मूत्रस्रोतो रुणद्दि च । निरुद्ध्यकशे तस्मिन् मन्द्रधारमवेद्नम् ।। ४६ ।। मूत्रं प्रवर्तते जन्तोमणिविधिययते नच । निरुद्धप्रकशं विद्यात् सर्रजं वातसंभवम् ॥ ४७ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १३ )

The skin over the glans covers the glaus tightly by the action of increased vata or other causes, blocking the passage of urine. In this conceation of thireascee urinary flow is slow and without the sensation; the glans does not show clearly and the condition is painful. This is known as Niruddhaprakasa caused by vata. (40-47)

# वेगसंधारणाद्वायुर्विहतो गुद्दसंश्रितः । निरुर्णाद्ध महास्त्रोतः सुक्ष्मद्वारं करोति च ॥ ४८ ॥ मार्गस्य सौक्ष्म्यात् कृञ्छेण पुरीयं तस्य गच्छति ।

स्त्रिरुद्धगुदं व्याधिमेतं विद्यात् सुदारुणम् ॥ ४९ ॥ ( सु. नि. १३ )

By constantly indulging in suppression of the urge of defecation, the vata functioning in the region of the rectum gets increased, blocks the rectal passage narrowing its lumen. Because of such narrowing, the movement of fees becomes This, highly distressing disease is known as Sanniru Idhagu la. ( ( 48–49 )
सम्माम के दुस्तीने द्वारा (विज्ञानी के बाद कि difficult

# शक्रम्भूत्रसमायुक्तेऽधौतेऽपानै शिश्वेत् । स्विन्ने वाऽस्त्रम् वाऽस्त्रम्य वितिषुषा ति. ( 48–49 )
शक्तम् सम्बद्धाः क्रात्रमुखस्याया विज्ञे ना स्त्राध्यमानै वा करडू रक्तक्रफोद कण्डूयनात्ततः क्षिपं स्फोटः स्नावश्य जायते ।

पकीभूतं व्यणैघोर्रं तं विद्याद्हिपूतनम् ॥ ५१ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १३ )

In infants, whose anal region is not washed well of feces and urine or during the act of fumigation or bathing; there develops itching in that region due to increase of rakta and kapha. Itching makes for scratching, which in turn produces exudating pustules, which join together forming an intractable ulcer. This condiis known as Ahipütana. ( 50-51 )

# स्नानोत्सादनहीनस्य मलो वृषणसंस्थितः । यदा प्रक्रियते स्वेदात् कण्डूं जनयते तदा ॥ ५२ ॥ कण्डूयनात्ततः क्षिप्रं स्फोटः स्नावश्च जायते ।

प्राहुर्वृष्णकच्छूं तां श्रुडेष्मरक्तप्रकोपजाम् ॥ ५३ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १३ )

In persons who do not take bath or oil massage regularly every day, dirt accumulates on the skin of the scrotum, helped by the sweat and causes itching. Itching makes for scratching which in turn produces exudating pustules. This condition known as Vrsanakacchū, is causcd by the increase of kapha and rakta. ( 52-53 )

# प्रवाहणातीसाराभ्यां निर्गच्छति गर्दं बह्निः । रूक्षदुबंलदेहस्य गुद्भवंशं तमादिशेत् ॥ ५४ ॥ ( सु. नि. अ. १३ )

In persons who are emaciated and weak, who indulge in straining at stools or by the effect of frequent attacks of diarrhoea, the muscles of the anus come out. This condition is known as Gudabhramsa. { 54 }

> सदाहो रक्तपर्यन्तरत्वकपाकी तीववेदनः । कण्डूमान् उवरकारी च स स्याच्छूकरदृष्ट्रकः ।। ५५ ॥ ( मु. नि. अ. १३ ) .

इति श्रीमाधवकरविरनिते माधवनिदाने क्षु द्वरोगनिदानं समाप्तम् 11 ५५ 11

The disease characterised by severe burning sensation, reddish edged ulcer of the skin with severe pain, itching and accompanied with fever is known as damstraka. ( 55 )

Thus ends the chapter on Ksudra rogas.

# अथ मुखरोगनिदानम्

## MUKHA ROGA NIDANAM ( DISEASES OF THE MOUTH )

# आनूपपिशितक्षीरद्धिमत्स्यातिसेवनात् । मुखमध्ये गदान् कुर्युः क्रुद्धा दोषाः कफोत्तराः ॥ १ ॥

By over-indulgence in foods prepared with flesh of animals of aquatic regions, milk, curd, fish, etc. the three dosas with the predominance of kapha, undergo abnormal increase and produce diseases of the mouth ( 1 )

# कर्कशौ परुषौ स्तब्धौ संप्रातानिल्छवेदनौ । दाल्येते परिपाट्येते ओष्ठौः मारुतकोपतः ॥ २ ॥

With the increase of vata the lips become rough, hard, immovable, painful and develop cracks and fissures. ( 2 )

# चीयते पिडकाभिध्य सरुजाभिः समन्ततः । सदाहपाकपिडकौ पीताभासौ चर्च पित्ततः ।। ३ ॥ ( मु. चि. अ. १६ )

With the increase of pitta, the lips develop painful piciakās ( papules ), with burning sensation, formation of ulcers and yellowish discolouration. ( 3 )

# सवर्णाभिश्च चीयेते पिडकाभरवेदना । भवतस्तु कफादोष्ठौ पिच्छिल्रौ शीतल्डौँ गुरू ॥ ४ ॥ ( सु. चि. अ. १६ )

With the increase of kapha, the lips develop slightly painful, non-discoloured pidakās ( papules ) and lips are unctuous, cold and heavy. ( 4 )

#### सकृत्कृत्रणौ सकृत्पीतौ संकृत्व्युतौ तथैव च । विश्लेयावनैकपिडकाचितो ॥ ५ ॥ ( सु. चि. अ. १६ ) सन्निपातेन

Increase of all the three dosas together will make the lips to appear black, yellow or white, from time to time, and also cause appearance of many pidakās ( papules ). ( 5 )

# खर्जूरफलवर्णाभिः पिडकाभिनिपीडितौ । रक्तोपस्युष्यौ रुधिरं स्ववतः शोधितम्भौ ॥ ६ ॥ ( सु. चि. अ. १३ )

If rakta ( blood ) gets increased, pidakis ( papules ) resembling fruits of date palm ( Phoenix sylvestris ) in appearance, rel in colour and discharging blood, develop on the lips. ( 6 )

# गुरू स्थूलौँ मांसदुध्यौ मांसपिण्डवदुद्गतौ । जन्तवश्चात्र मूर्चर्छन्ति नरस्योभयतो मुखात् ॥ ७ ॥ ( मु. चि. अ. १६ )

If mamsa ( muscles ) gets invaded, the lips become heavy and thick with lumps of muscles, which discharge exudates containing worms, from the angles of the mouth. ( 7 )

# सर्पिर्मण्डप्रतीकाशा मेदसा कण्डुरौ गुरू । अच्छं स्फटिकसङ्काशमास्वावं स्वयतो भूशम् ॥ ८ ॥ तयोर्वणो न संरोहेन्सृदुत्वं च न गच्छति । ( सु. चि. अ. १६ )

If medas ( fat ) get involved, the lips appear like the fluid portion of the ghee, develop itching and heaviness; exudes clear, colourless fluid in large quantity and forms an ulcer which does not heal and the lips do not assume the normal softness. ( 8 )

# क्षतजाभौ विदीयेंते पाख्येते चामिघाततः ॥ ९ ॥ ग्रथितौ च तथा स्यातामोष्ठौ कण्डूसमन्वितौ । ( सु. चि. अ. १६ )

If the lips become the place of assault by weapons, etc., they will be seen with bleeding, cut or swelling and will be having itching also. ( 9 )

# शोणितं दन्तवेष्टेभ्यो यस्याकस्मात्मवर्तते । दुर्गन्धीनि सक्टूब्णानि प्रक्रुद्दीनि मृद्र्नि च ॥ १० ॥ दन्तमांसानि शीर्यन्ते पचम्बन्धि च पररस्परम् । शीतादो नाम स व्याधिः कफशोणितसंभवः ॥ ११ ॥ ( मु. चि. अ. १६ )

Occasional bleeding from the gums, with foul smell, gums becoming black, exudative, soft, the muscles tear up and give room to formation of pus. This disease known as Sttada is produced by increased kapha and rakta. ( 10–11 )

# दन्तयोस्त्रिषु वा यस्य श्वयथुर्जायते महान् । दन्तपुष्पुटको नाम स व्याधिः कफरक्तजः ॥ १२ ॥ ( मु. चि. अ. १६ )

Severe swelling of the gums of two or three teeth together, caused by increase of kapha and rakta is known as Dantapuppuṭaka. ( 12 )

# स्नवन्ति पूर्यरुधिरं चला दन्ता भवन्ति च । दन्तवेष्टः स विश्लेयो दुष्टशोणितसंभवः ॥ १३ ॥ ( सु. चि. अ. १६ )

Pus and blood coming out, teeth becoming shaky are the features of a disease known as Dantavesta caused by bad blood. ( 13 )

#### श्वयथुर्दन्तमुलेषु रुजावान् कफरक्तजः । लालास्त्रावी स विश्लेयः शौधिरो नाम नामतः ॥ १४ ॥ ( सु. चि. अ. १६ )

A swelling at the root of the tooth, painful and causing free flow of saliva is a disease known as Śausira caused by increased kapha and rakta together. ( 14 )

> दन्ताश्चलन्ति वेश्वेभ्यस्तालु चाण्यवदीय ते । यस्मिन् स सर्वजो व्याधिमेहरशौधिरसंबितः ॥ १५ ॥ ( मु. चि. अ. १६ )

Teeth getting loose from the gums, palate becoming fissured ( in addition to the other symptoms of sausira) is a disease known as Mahāsausira, which is caused by the increase of all the three dosas together. ( 15 )

# दन्तमांसानि शीर्यन्ते यस्मिन् ष्ठीवति चाप्यस्तुक् । पित्तास्त्रकृतजो व्याधिक्षेयः परिद्ररो हिन् सः ॥ १६ ॥ ( सु. चि. अ. १६ )

The gums undergoing decomposition and causing frequent spitting of blood is a disease called Paridara, resulting from increase of pitta, rakta and kapha. ( 16 )

# वेषु दाहः पाकश्च ताभ्यां दन्ताश्चलन्ति च । यस्मिन् सोपकुशो नाम पित्तरक्तकृतों गद् : ।। १७ ॥ ( मु. चि. अ. १६ )

The gums having burning sensation, ulcers on them, teeth shaky are the symptoms of the disease Upakusa, produced by increase of pitta and rakta together. (17)

# घुष्टेषु दन्तमांसेषु संरम्भो जायते महान् । चला भवन्ति दन्ताश्र्व स वेदर्भोऽभिघातजः ॥ १८ ॥ ( मु. चि. अ. १६ )

The gums excoriated, having burning sensation and ulcers appear on them, This discase is que to trauma and is known as Vaidarbha. ( 18 ) teeth are shaky.

# मारुतेनाधिको दन्तो दन्तो जायते व तीव्रवेदनः । खलिवर्धनसंक्षोऽस्तौ जाते रुक् च प्रशास्यति ।। १९ ॥ ( सु. चि. अ. १६ )
