# II. Prajñaparadha ( transgression of regimen )

- 1. Kayaka ( physical activities )
	- a ) Ativyayama ( excessive physical activity ) for long periods, such as, for example -Adhwa (walking long distances ) Bharavahana ( carrying heavy loads ) Vyavaya ( sexual intercourse ) Drutayana ( fast riding ), Uccabhasana ( speaking in high pitch ).
	- b ) Sāhasa ( activities which produce quick exertion ) Such as Balavad Vigraha ( wrestling with stronger persons ).

Dhanurākarṣaṇa ( archery ) Matta pranidamana ( restraining an animal which is in heat ) Mahānaditarana ( swimming across mighty rivers ).

Saha Dhavana ( running along with horses, dogs, bulls etc ).

Throwing heavy objects like stones, log of wood etc. fast dancing ) etc.

- B ) Ayoga-Sarvathā Apravritti-Acesta ( not doing any physical activity ) Avyayama ( absence of exercise )
- C ) Mithyayoga ( improper, irregular activity )
	- a ) Vegadhāraṇa ( suppression ( of urges of urine, feces, flatus etc ).
	- b ) Vegodirana ( voluntary initiation of these urges prematurely )

Gymnastics and athletic feats, weight lifting.

Mountain climbing, sea swimming, sky diving, horse and motor cycle race etc.

Throwing discus, hammer, iron shot etc.

- c ) Vişama cesta ( improper acts ) Skhalana ( sliding ) patana ( falling from a height, jumping and such activitics improperly done ).
- d ) Prāņoparodha ( activities which require control of breath and risky to life )
- e ) Anga pradusana ( scratching ) Snāna/Abhyanga (oil massage and bath ).prahāra ( blows ) mardana ( massaging ) etc., of the body.
- f ) Sankleśanādi kāryās ( actions which produce severe exertion )
- 2. Mānasika ( Psychological activity )
	- A ) Atiyoga -Atipravritti ( excessive mental activity )
	- B ) Ayoga -- Apravritti of mental activity }
	- C ) Mithyayoga ( improper activity ) a ) Manodwega from bhaya ( fear ), soka (grief), krodha ( anger ), lobha ( greed ), moha ( passion ), kāma ( lust ), īrsya ( jealosy ), dweşa ( hatred ), mana ( pride ), mada ( infatuation) etc. and many more mental stressors ).
	- b ) Mithyadarsana ( improper understanding and improper response }.
- 3. Vacika ( Vocal ) activity of speech.
	- A ) Atiyoga -Talking for long periods. Uccabhāsaņa ( speaking in high pitch ) gayana ( singing ) etc.
	- B ) Ayoga Apravritti-Mouna (observing silence for long periods ).

Acrobatics.

Magical feats.

Air flights without oxygen etc. Yogic exercises.

Excessive mental work, effect of drugs etc.

Inadequate or absence of mental work, effert of drugs etc.

By the effect of psychodelic substances like hashish, charas etc. many other chemicals and drugs. Brainwashing and such other methods.

## C. Certain Articles of Diet :-

# A. Dhānya ( grains )-

- l . Navānna ( Nava dhānya )--- Just harvested/acutely fresh grains
- 2. Virūḍha dhānya—Germinating grains.
- 3. Adaki-Cajanus indicus, Spreng.
- 4. Cinaka-Panicum miliaccum. Linn.
- 5. Hayanaka-A variety of rice, Oryza sativa Linn.
- 6. Kalāya—Pisum sativum Linn.
- 7. Koradūṣa—Paspalum scorbiculatum. Linn.
- 8. Kulattha-Dolichos biflorus. Linn.
- 9. Madhūlaka—Triticum sativum. Lam.
- 10. Masūra-Lens culinaris-medic.
- 11. Māṣa—Phaseolus mungo. Linn.
- 12. Mudga-Phaseolus qureus Roxb.
- 13. Niṣpāva—Dolichos lablab Linn.
- 14. Sarșapa-Brassica campestris Var.
- 15. Tila-Sesamum indicum. Linn.
- 16. Uddālaka-Pasapalum scorbiculatum. Linn.
- 17. Yavaka-Hordeum vulgare. Linn,
- B. Sakas ( Leafy Vegetables )
	- 1. Šuska śāka-dried leaves.
	- 2. Bhūtriņa-Cymbopogon citratus. DC.
	- 3. Gandira ( Bhandira )-Amaranthus spinosus. Linn.
	- 4. Kākamāci-Solanum nigrum, Linn.
	- 5. Kālamāla-Ocimun sanctum. Linn.
	- 6. Karanja-Pongamia glabra. Vent.
	- 7. Karavīra—Narium odorum, soland.
	- 8. Ksavaka-Brassica Juncea Linn.
	- 9. Kuteraka-Ocimum basilicum. Linn.
	- 10. Lasuna-Allium sativum Linn.
	- 11. Madhusigru-Moringa Pterygosperma. Geartri.
	- 12. Phanijjaka-Origanum Majorana. Linn.
	- 13. Rohiņi ( Kaṭukaroħini ) Picrorhiza Kurroa. Royle.
	- 14. Sarşapa-Brassica campestris. Linn.
	- 15. Sigru-Moringa Pterygosperma. Geartn
	- 16. Sumukha-A variety of Ocimum sanctum. Linn.
	- 17. Surasã-Ocimum sanctum Linn.

#### C. Kanda, Mila ( Tubers, Roots, Rhizomes )

- 1. Bisa-Rhizome of Nelumbium speciosum willd.
- 2. Kaseru-Scirpus kysoor Roxb.
- 3. Krouncadana lotus stalk
- 4. Lasuna-Allium sativum Linn.
- 5. Mūlaka ( Āma )-Tender Raphanus sativus. Linn.
- 6. Pindālu-Dioscorca alata. Linn.
- 7. Śringātaka-Trapa bispinosa. Roxb.

#### D. Phala ( Fruits )

- 1. Badara-Zizyphus Jujuba, Lam. ( large sized fruit )
- 2. Jambū Eugenia Jambolana. Lam.
- 3. Kuvala ( kola )-Zizyphus jujube ( moderate sized fruit )
- 4. Lakuca-Artocarpus lakoocha. Roxb.

#### E. Mamsa ( meat, fish etc. )

- 1. Anupa māmsa-Flesh of animals and birds living in marshy regions.
- 2. Oudaka mamsa-Flesh of animals and birds living in water.
- 3. Gramya mamsa-Flesh of domestic animals and birds.
- 4. Suska mamsa ( vallura )-Dried meat.
- 5. Püti mamsa-meat which smells bad.
- 6. Visakta mamsa -- Poisoned meat, or of animals killed by poisoning
- 7. Aja mamsa-meat of goat
- 8. Avi mamsa-meat of sheep
- 9 Go mamsa-meat of cow, ox, bull.
- 10. Kanākapota-meat of dove
- 11. Mahisa mamsa-meat of buffalo
- 12. Matsya mamsa-fish, in general
- 13. Cilicima fish-a kind of red coloured fish.
- 14. Varāha māmsa-flesh of boar, hog.

#### F. Drava ( Liquids )

- 1. Jala Atipana-drinking of large quantities of water.
- 2. Navambu ( Fresh water )-In rivers, brooks etc., which are muddy.
- 3. Atisitambu ( Very cold water )-Cooled with ice, or by refrigerator.
- 4. Dustambu ( Polluted water )-Contaminated by sewage, sullage, industrial effluents, pesticides, stagnant, bacteria infested.
- 5. Trisna Nigraha Vis a Vis-Jala Varjana Controlling of thirst or avoidance of drinking water.

#### G. Ksīra and Ksīra Vikrti ( Milk and Milk Products )

- 1. Ama ksira-uncooked milk
- 2. Dadhi-Coagulat milk
- 3. Kilata-Residue of clotted milk after filtering
- 4. Kurcīka-Residue of butter-milk
- 5. Mandaka-Inadequately fermented curds
- 6. Takra-Buttermilk diluted
- 7. Takra pinda-Solid portion of buttermilk
- 8. Udaświt-Diluted butter-milk

H. Ikshu Rasa and Vikyti--( Sugar cane juice and its products )

- 1. Ikshurasa ati pana-Drinking large quantitites of cane juice
- 2. Guda-Jaggery | molasses | treacle
- 3. Phāņita—Half cooked molasses
- 4. Śarkara-Sugar

#### I. Tailas ( Oils )

- 1. Atasi Taila-Oil of Linum usitalissimum, Linn.
- 2. Kusumba Taila—Oil of Carthamus tinctorius Linn.
- 3 Sarsapa Taila-Oil of Brassica campestris. Linn.
- 4. Tila Taila-Oil of Sesamum indicum D. C.

- l . Ati madyapãna—Too much indulgence in alcoholic drinks
- 2. Tikshna madya-Orinking strong liquors.
- 3. Nava madya—Fresh liquors
- 4. Asava ( arista ) -Fermented infusions decoctions.

## K. Prepared Foods :-

Prthuka ---Rice made into flakes Pişta - Flour Krsara Rice cooked along with green gram Saskuli-Pastry fried in oil Upadamśa ( Vyanjana ) -Pickles, fried eatables, side-dishes, condiments etc.

- L. Bhojana Krama ( Nature of Dining, Eating habits )
	- 1. Adhyasana-Eating large quantity and too frequently ( gluttony )
	- 2. Alpasana/Anasana -Eating very less quantity of food Pramitasana, Upavasa - Fasting, starving
	- 3. Visamasana-Eating foods which is a mixture of suitable and unsuitable articles, incompatable foods; contaminated, dirty foods etc.
	- 4. Atidruta bhojana -- Eating very fast, gulping.
	- 5. Akala-eating before the usual time.
	- 6. Kalatita--eating after the lapsc of usual time.
	- 7. Akrama eating violating the regimen of dining.
	- 8. Asatmya eating inonaccustomed foods c.g. mud, chalk, hairs. Mridbhakshana { eating mud } etc.

#### APPENDIX IV

# DISEASES MENTIONED IN MĀDHAVA NIDANA WITH THEIR NEAREST MODERN EQUIV ALENTS

1. Jwara-Pyrexia, Fever.

- A. Sama Jwara -Regular fevers ( Organic fevers ).
	- a ) Vātaja Jwara --Fever due to increase of Vāta
	- b ) Pittaja Jwara-Fever due to increase of Pitta
	- c ) Kaphaja Jwara -Fever due to increase of Kapha
	- d ) Vāta Pitta Jwara -- Fever due to increase of Vāta Pitta together
	- e ) Vata Kapha Jwara --Fever due to increase of Vāta and Kapha together
	- f ) Kapha pitta Jwara -Fever due to increase of Kapha and Pitta together
	- g ) Sannipata Jwara -Fever due to increase of all the three dosas together

B. Agantu jwara -Fever due to external causes

- a ) Abhighātaja Jwara-Fever duc to assault, trauma
- b ) Abhichāraja Jwara -Fever due to witchcraft, sorcery etc.
- c ) Abhiśāpaja Jwara -Fever due to curses, abuses etc.
- d ) Abhisangaja Jwara -- Fever due to worms, pathogenic bacteria
- e } Visaja Twara-Fever due to poisons
- f ) Oşadhigandhaja jwara—Fever due to inhalation of pollen
- g ) Mānasika --- Udwega ( emotions ) { Psychic fevers due to Kama ( lust ) Bhaya ( fear ) emotional bouts occuring Soka ( grief ) kopa ( anger ) l suddenly.

C. Visama Jwara-Irregular, intermittant fevers

- a ) Santata jwara -continuous for 7, 10, 12 days followed by remission and recurrance
- h ) Satata jwara -- double quotidian fever
- c ) Anyeduska jwara-Quotidian fever

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- d ) Tritīyaka įwara-Tertian fever
<sup>1.</sup> Further on, in all diseases, those varities which are caused by increase of any two dosas together are designated as Dwandwaja ( Dwidosaja ) and those caused by increase of all the three dosas together as Sannipataja ( Tridosaja )

- e ) Caturthaka jwara-Quartan fever
- f ) Caturthaka viparyaya -- Reversed quartan fever.
- g ) Vātabalāsaka jwara-Fever in Beriberi, helmenthiasis etc.
- h ) Pralepaka jwara Fever in tuberculosis

## 2. Atīsāra -Diarīhoea

- A. a } Vātaja b } Pittaja c ) Kaphaja d ) Sannipātaja e ) Šokaja/Bhayaja-diarrhoea caused by excess of grief, fear f ) Amaja-diarrhoea caused by eating uncooked/undigested food.
- B. Pravāhika-Dysentery
	- a ) Vātaja b ) Pittaja c ) Kaphaja d ) Sannipātaja c ) Raktaja ( Šonitaja ) due to vitiation of blood

## 3. Grahani Roga -Disease of the duodenum, sprue, Malabsorption syndrome.

- a ) Vātaja b ) Pittaja c ) Kaphaja d ) Sannipātaja
- e ) Sangraha grahani
- Incurable stages of the disease f ) Ghatiyantra Grahani

#### 4. Arsas-Haemorrhoids, piles

- a ) Vātaja b ) Pittaja c ) kaphaja d ) Sannipātaja e ) Soņitaja ( Raktaja )
- f ) Sahaja congenital

#### 5. Agnimandya-Dyspepsia, weak digestive activity.

- A. a ) Mandagni-Feeble or very weak digestive function
	- b ) Tiksnagni -- intense or very powerful digestive function
	- c ) Visamagni Erratic digestive function

## B. Ajīrņa-Indigestion

- a ) Amajirna-due to increase of kapha
- b ) Vidagdhajirna due to increase of Pitta
- c ) Vistabdhājirņa-duc to increase of Vāta
- d ) Rasa sesajirna-due to increase of undigested|improperly processed Ahāra Rasa
- e } Visūcika-Gastro-enteritis.
- f ) Alasaka
- Food poisoning. g ) Vilambika

#### 6. Krimiroga -discases due to parasites, helmenthiasis.

- a ) Bahya -- external parasites
- b ) Abhyantara----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- a ) Kaphaja ( b ) Puriṣaja ( born in feces ) ( c ) Raktaja ( born in blood )
- 7. Pānduroga-Anacmia
	- A. a } Vātaja ( b ) Pittaja ( c ) Kaphaja ( d ) Sannipātaja
		- e ) Mrid bhaksanaja -anaemia due to eating of mud
	- B. Kamala-Jaundice, Hepatitis.
		- a > Kosthasraya-residing in the abdomeu
		- b ) Shakhasraya-residing in the tissues
		- c ) Kumbhakamala -- cirrhosis of the liver with jaundice
		- d ) Halimaka Chlorosis
		- e ) Pānaki
- 8. Rakta-pitta-Haemorrhagic diathesis, polycythemea Vera ?
	- a ) Urdhwaga-bleeding from upper orifices
	- b ) Adhoga-bleeding from lower orifices
	- c ) Tiryagga -bleeding from all directions including skin pores
- 9. Raja vaksma -pulmonary tuberculosis
	- A. a ) Trirūpa with three symptoms
		- b ) Sa Irūpa—with six symptoms
		- c ) Ekadasa rūpa-with eleven symptoms
	- B. Sosa-consumption, wasting, emaciation,
		- a ) Vyavaya-due to exessive sexual intercourse
		- b ) Soka-due to excess of grief
		- c ) Vārdhikya due to old age
		- d ) Vyayama-due to excess of physical activity
		- e ) Adhwa due to excess of walking long distances
		- f ) Vrana---- due to protracted ulcer/wound
		- g ) Uraksata due to injury of chest ( lungs )
- 10. Kasa-cough/pertussis/Bronchitis
	- a ) Vātaja ( b ) Pittaja ( c ) Kaphaja ( d ) Kṣataja-due to injury to lungs
	- e ) Ksayaja-due to wasting, depletion of tissues.

#### 11. A. Hikka-Hiccup

- a ) Annaja-due to improper food
- b ) Yamala-hiccup in pairs
- c ) Ksudra-mild hiccup
- d ) Gambhira-deep seated
- e ) Mahati-powerful, with great force
- B. Swasa-Dyspnoea
	- a ) Mahā Śwāsa-powerful, long, painful breathing
	- b ) Urdhwa Swasa-breathing outwards with pronouned expirations-
	- c ) Chinna śwāsa-discontinued breathing
